Comprehensive experiments on four benchmarks (How2Sign, Phoenix14T, CSL-Daily, and MeineDGS) demonstrate state-of-the-art translation performance, and \textbf{SIGNET} also surpasses prior methods on WLASL for sign language recognition.

</details>


### [35] [Physics-Grounded Disentangled Flow Modeling for Brain Disease Progression Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28630)
*Jun Wang,Peirong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PDF框架，一种基于物理约束的解耦流匹配方法，将脑部病灶进展建模分解为形态演化（病灶生长与结构变形）和强度演化（信号变化），并引入PDE正则化损失约束形态演化过程，在三个公共纵向数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑部病灶纵向预测方法直接从基线图像映射到未来观测，未显式建模病灶进展的物理机制，导致结构变形与图像强度变化纠缠在一起，限制了物理合理性、模型泛化能力和可解释性。

Method: 提出PDF（Physics-grounded Disentangled Flow matching）框架：(1) 将纵向建模显式解耦为两个独立过程——形态演化（流匹配网络捕获病灶生长与结构变形）和强度演化（流匹配网络建模由病灶浓度变化驱动的信号改变）；(2) 引入基于病灶生长动力学的PDE正则化损失，以扩散-反应-对流方程约束形态演化过程。

Result: 在涵盖多种脑疾病的三个公共纵向数据集上进行实验，取得了当前最优性能，验证了解耦建模框架和物理约束学习设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦形态演化与强度演化，并引入物理驱动的PDE约束，PDF框架显著提升了脑部病灶纵向预测的物理合理性、泛化能力和可解释性，为疾病监测和治疗规划提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Forecasting longitudinal brain lesion evolution is critical for disease monitoring and treatment planning. Existing approaches typically learn a direct mapping from a baseline image to a future observation, without explicitly modeling the physical mechanisms underlying the lesion progression. Such an entangled modeling of structural deformation and image intensity variation limits physical plausibility, model generalization, and interpretability. To address this, we propose PDF, a Physics-grounded Disentangled Flow matching framework for longitudinal brain disease forecasting. We explicitly decompose the longitudinal modeling of lesion growth into two processes, each learned by a dedicated flow matching network: morphology evolution, which captures lesion growth and structural deformation; and intensity evolution, which models signal changes driven by variations in lesion concentration. To enforce physics-grounded constraints, we introduce a PDE-regularized loss based on lesion growth dynamics, that enforces a diffusion-reaction-advection formulation for morphological evolution. Experiments on three public longitudinal datasets spanning diverse brain diseases demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, validating the effectiveness of the disentangled modeling framework and physics-grounded learning design. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/jhuldr/PDF.

</details>


### [36] [AEGIR: Modeling Area Emitters for Indoor Inverse Rendering using Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28635)
*Mohamed Shawky Sabae,Philipp Langsteiner,Jan-Niklas Dihlmann,Hendrik Lensch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AEGIR 提出在可重光照高斯泼溅框架中显式建模局部面光源，通过可微延迟渲染管线结合多重重要性采样和针对性正则化，解决了逆向渲染中光照与材质分离的歧义问题，在复杂局部光照场景中显著提升了光照重建和下游任务效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的可重光照方法使用离散点光源、全局环境贴图或隐式表示近似场景光照，忽略了真实光源的物理空间范围，导致光衰减不准确和阴影不真实。需要一种能显式建模局部面光源的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 AEGIR 框架：(1) 在可重光照高斯泼溅表示中显式参数化局部面光源；(2) 设计可微延迟渲染管线，集成多重重要性采样（MIS）；(3) 引入针对性正则化策略，以应对光源、材质、几何联合优化中参数增多和光照-材质歧义加剧的挑战。

Result: 实验表明，显式面光源建模改善了光照重建质量，在新视角合成、可控重光照和虚拟物体插入等下游任务中均取得更优效果，尤其在具有复杂局部光照的场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: AEGIR 通过显式建模面光源的物理空间范围，实现了更准确的光传输模拟和更一致的光照-材质分解，验证了面光源表示在逆向渲染中的重要性。

Abstract: Inverse rendering requires separating illumination from surface materials, which is highly ambiguous due to their tight coupling in observed images. While Gaussian Splatting is efficient for novel view synthesis, existing relightable methods approximate scene lighting using discrete point lights, global environment maps, or implicit representations. By ignoring the physical spatial extent of real-world emitters, these approaches produce incorrect light attenuation and unrealistic shadows. We present AEGIR (Area Emitters for Gaussian Inverse Rendering), a framework that explicitly models local area emitters within a relightable Gaussian Splatting representation. Joint optimization of emitters, materials, and geometry is challenging due to flexible emitter parameterization, which increases both the number of parameters and the ambiguity between illumination and materials. We address this by introducing a differentiable deferred rendering pipeline that integrates multiple importance sampling with targeted regularization. As a result, AEGIR accurately simulates local light transport and achieves more consistent decomposition. Experiments show that explicit area emitters improve illumination reconstruction and enhance downstream tasks, including novel view synthesis, controlled relighting, and virtual object insertion, particularly in scenes with complex local lighting.

</details>


### [37] [Obliviate: Erasing Concepts from Autoregressive Image Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28643)
*Hossein Shakibania,Jonas Henry Grebe,Tobias Braun,Ege Aktemur,Saleh Aslani,Mehmet Görkem Yiğit,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Obliviate 是一种针对自回归图像生成模型的引导式概念擦除方法，通过 KL 散度监督视觉 token 分布、轨迹级更新和视觉前缀对齐三项设计，在多个模型上显著降低了有害内容生成（如裸露图像从 91.58 降至 3.15），同时保持模型整体效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前概念擦除研究主要集中在扩散模型上，而自回归图像生成模型的概念擦除几乎未被探索。随着统一多模态架构趋势下自回归模型的日益普及，亟需填补这一空白，以遏制生成式 AI 被滥用于生成不安全或令人不适的图像。

Method: 提出 Obliviate，一种基于引导的概念擦除方法，包含三个核心设计：(1) 基于 KL 散度的视觉 token 分布监督；(2) 在完整自回归 rollout 上进行轨迹级更新；(3) 利用对齐的视觉前缀实现稳定的目标构建。

Result: 在 Liquid、Emu3-Gen 和 Janus-Pro 三款最先进的自回归文生图模型上评估，覆盖裸露内容、暴力画面和品牌图像擦除。Obliviate 持续优于现有替代方法：在防御性 RAB 基准上，裸露内容生成率从 91.58% 降至 3.15%，同时保持模型整体效用。

Conclusion: Obliviate 填补了自回归图像生成领域概念擦除的空白，提供了一种高效且模型无关的解决方案，在显著降低有害内容生成的同时不损害模型通用性能，为未来统一多模态架构的安全部署提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative AI models has intensified concerns about misuse, including the creation of unsafe or disturbing imagery. To mitigate such issues, several concept erasure approaches have been proposed to remove harmful content from multimodal generative models. Yet concept erasure for autoregressive image generation remains largely unexplored, despite the growing relevance of these models in recent trends toward unified multimodal architectures. In this work, we fill this gap by introducing Obliviate, a guidance-based concept erasure method for autoregressive image generation. Our method builds on three key design choices: KL-based supervision over visual token distributions, trajectory-level updates over full autoregressive rollouts, and aligned visual prefixes for stable target construction. We evaluate Obliviate on three state-of-the-art autoregressive text-to-image models, Liquid, Emu3-Gen, and Janus-Pro, covering the erasure of explicit content, graphic violence, and branded imagery. Obliviate consistently outperforms current alternatives, reducing nudity on the defensive RAB benchmark from 91.58 to 3.15 while preserving overall model utility.

</details>


### [38] [FedLAS: Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing for Neural Network Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28654)
*Thiru Thillai Nadarasar Bahavan,Sachith Seneviratne,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedLAS 提出一种基于特征范数的双向标签平滑算法，通过特征范数置信度指标 (NCI) 和双向校准门控 (BCG) 模块，针对每个样本动态调整平滑程度，同时处理过度自信和欠自信问题，在多视觉基准上提升了模型校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标签平滑方法（LS 和 MbLS）依赖预定义的均匀平滑规则，仅处理过度自信问题。实际训练中，不同样本具有不同的难度和歧义性，可能表现出不同程度的过度自信或欠自信，需要一种能识别每个样本具体置信状态并动态调整平滑策略的机制。

Method: 提出 FedLAS（Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing）：(1) 基于特征范数的置信度指标 (NCI)，利用特征范数控制平滑强度；(2) 双向校准门控 (BCG) 模块，检测过度自信和欠自信两种状态并分别处理。该算法可作为即插即用模块集成到 LS 和 MbLS 等标签平滑损失中。

Result: 在标准和细粒度高分辨率视觉基准上，FedLAS 相比现代基线方法持续提升校准性能，降低了 Expected Calibration Error (ECE) 和 Adaptive ECE，同时保持了 Top-1 准确率。

Conclusion: FedLAS 通过样本级自适应双向标签平滑有效解决了 DNN 校准问题，其即插即用特性使其可广泛集成于各类标签平滑损失，为模型校准提供了更灵活有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep Neural Network (DNN) classifiers suffer from poor calibration when their softmax outputs (predictive confidence) deviate from the empirical likelihoods. This manifests itself as either overconfident incorrect predictions or under-confident correct predictions. Label smoothing (LS) enhances model calibration by introducing entropy regularization during training through redistributing probability mass from the ground-truth label to the remaining classes. LS, including Margin-based LS (MbLS), have restrictive assumptions: they rely on predefined, uniform smoothing rules and only tackle overconfidence. In reality, samples exhibit diverse characteristics, such as difficulty/ambiguity, that interact with the evolving nature of the model being trained. In training, samples may have various degrees of under- or overconfidence. To overcome this, a mechanism that identifies the specific confidence state of each sample and determines the appropriate degree of smoothing in each training step is needed, tailoring the adjustment to the individual sample. We propose FedLAS: Feature-Modulated Bidirectional Label Smoothing, a plug-and-play algorithm for label smoothing-based losses. In FedLAS, we introduce a Feature Norm-based Confidence Indicator (NCI) to control smoothing and a Bidirectional Calibration Gating (BCG) module to detect both over and under-confidence. Our algorithm can be integrated with LS and MbLS based losses when applied to standard DNNs, enhancing performance. Extensive experiments on standard and fine-grained high-resolution vision benchmarks show that FedLAS consistently improves calibration compared to modern baselines, reducing Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Adaptive ECE while maintaining Top-1 accuracy. Code: github.com/nadarasarbahavan/FEDLAS

</details>


### [39] [SemDynReg: Semantics-Guided Deformation Regularization for Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28656)
*Ruitao Chen,Mozhang Guo,Jinge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种语义引导的动态3D高斯泼溅正则化框架，通过SAM提取分割掩码并构建对象ID映射，对每个对象内top-k贡献高斯点的变形参数（位置、尺度、旋转）施加一致性正则化，解决了现有方法缺乏对象级建模导致变形不一致和对象间耦合的问题，在PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS指标上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于变形场的动态3DGS方法缺乏显式的对象级建模，导致单个对象内部高斯变形不一致，以及不同对象之间产生不希望的变形耦合。需要引入对象级语义约束来改善这一问题。

Method: （1）使用SAM提取分割掩码，从输入图像中获取语义特征；通过特征相关性匹配与预定义对象词典构建object-ID映射。 （2）基于object-ID映射，识别每个对象像素级top-k贡献高斯点。 （3）对这些高斯点的变形参数（位置、尺度、旋转）施加对象内一致性正则化损失，约束同一对象内高斯变形行为一致。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的语义感知正则化方法改善了对象级变形一致性，在动态3DGS渲染质量上超越了基线方法，获得了更高的PSNR和SSIM，以及更低的LPIPS。

Conclusion: 通过引入语义引导的对象级变形正则化，有效解决了动态3DGS中对象内变形不一致和对象间耦合的问题，显著提升了动态场景渲染质量。该框架为动态3DGS提供了一种新的对象感知建模范式。

Abstract: Deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an efficient approach for rendering dynamic scenes in a wide range of 3D applications. However, existing deformation field-based approaches largely lack explicit object-level modeling, often resulting in inconsistent Gaussian deformations within individual objects and unwanted coupling between different objects. To address this limitation, we introduce a semantics-guided framework that enforces dynamic regularization at the object level, aiming to achieve spatially consistent object-wise deformation. Specifically, we first extract segmentation masks using the Segment Anything Model (SAM) and derive semantic features from input images. An object-ID map is then constructed via feature relevance matching with a predefined object dictionary. Guided by this object-ID map, we identify the pixel-wise top-k contributing Gaussians for each object and impose consistency regularization on their deformation parameters, including position, scale, and rotation. Unlike prior methods that learn deformation fields without explicit object-level constraints, our approach incorporates semantic cues to guide deformation behavior at the object level. Experimental results demonstrate that our semantics-aware regularization improves object-level deformation consistency and outperforms baseline methods in rendering quality, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM and lower LPIPS in dynamic 3DGS rendering. Our project page is available at https://dyn-reg-3dgs.github.io/.

</details>


### [40] [BackTranslation2.0 -- A Linguistically Motivated Metric to Assess Sign Language Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28673)
*Oliver Cory,Maksym Ivashechkin,Karahan Sahin,Oline Ranum,Jianhe Low,Edward Fish,Anton Pelykh,Ozge Mercanoglu Sincan,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BackTranslation2.0 是一种面向文本到手语翻译的评估指标，通过智能体框架编排多个专用工具，从语法正确性、音系准确性、动作流畅度和生成保真度四个维度进行评估，并利用LLM进行跨工具交叉验证，最终在BSL数据集上表现出与人类评分高度一致的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语生成评估指标过于简化，与人类判断对齐差，缺乏语言学基础。需要一种更全面、可解释且语言学上严谨的评估框架。

Method: 提出BackTranslation2.0，采用智能体（agentic）框架：确定性管道编排一组专用工具，评估四个维度（语法正确性、音系准确性、动作流畅度、生成保真度）；引入基于LLM的交叉参考比较模块，检查工具输出间的一致性及其是否符合语言学预期；最终通过确定性加权公式计算各维度得分。

Result: 在BSL数据集上进行人工评分研究（由语言学家与聋人专家合作设计协议），与六种基线指标对比，BackTranslation2.0在所有维度上与人类判断均表现出强相关性。

Conclusion: BackTranslation2.0为手语生成系统提供了更全面、可解释且语言学原则驱动的评估框架，有效弥补了现有指标的不足。

Abstract: Sign Languages (SLs) are the primary means of communication for millions of deaf individuals, yet existing evaluation metrics for generated SL remain simplistic and poorly aligned with human judgements. We introduce BackTranslation2.0, a linguistically grounded evaluation metric for text-to-sign translation that moves beyond naïve backtranslation. Our approach adopts an agentic framework in which a deterministic pipeline orchestrates a suite of specialised tools to assess four scoring dimensions - grammatical correctness, phonological accuracy, motion fluency, and generation fidelity - aligned with human rater assessments. Tool outputs are not treated independently: a set of large language model (LLM)-based cross-referential comparison modules evaluates consistency across tools and checks outputs against linguistic expectations, enabling structured reasoning over grammatical, phonological, and motion-level evidence. Final dimension scores are computed through deterministic weighted formulas over validated tool outputs. To validate BackTranslation2.0, we introduce and evaluate on a British Sign Language (BSL) dataset rated in a human rater study across the same quality dimensions, following a protocol developed in collaboration between linguists and deaf experts, benchmarking against six baseline metrics. Our method demonstrates strong correlation with human judgements across all dimensions, providing a more comprehensive, interpretable, and linguistically principled evaluation framework for sign language production systems.

</details>


### [41] [Predicting Metastatic Risk from Primary Tissue Architecture via Distance-Aware Spatial Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28676)
*Sandesh Pokhrel,Hamid Manoochehri,Bodong Zhang,Beatrice S Knudsen,Tolga Tasdizen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DTMf-MIL方法，通过在MIL框架中引入符号距离函数(SDF)编码肿瘤微环境的空间几何信息，显式建模肿瘤细胞、成纤维细胞和浸润淋巴细胞之间的空间关系，在远处转移预测任务上显著优于忽略空间布局的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIL方法将组织切片视为无序的图像块集合，丢弃了定义转移潜能的空间布局信息。作者认为转移风险本质上由肿瘤微环境在肿瘤细胞界面处的几何排布决定，因此需要一种能够显式捕获空间关系的方法。

Method: 提出Distance aware Tissue Modeling for Multiple Instance Learning (DTMf-MIL)。核心思想是计算相对于不同组织表型（肿瘤细胞、肿瘤相关成纤维细胞、浸润淋巴细胞）的符号距离函数(SDF)，将显式的空间先验作为视觉特征的增强信息融入MIL框架，使模型能够学习转移风险的结构性特征。

Result: DTMf-MIL在原发肿瘤组织预测远处转移的任务上显著优于现有最先进方法；在公开基准数据集上的验证表明，空间感知能力在多种临床任务中均能持续提升诊断准确性。

Conclusion: 将肿瘤微环境的空间几何信息显式纳入MIL框架，能够显著提升对远处转移风险的预测能力，空间感知应成为计算病理学模型设计中的关键考量因素。

Abstract: Predicting the risk of distant metastasis from primary tumor tissue histology is a critical yet challenging task in computational pathology. Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) approaches can attend to subdomains in tumor regions that harbor features of metastatic cancer progression. However MIL models treat tissue patches as unordered bags, discarding the spatial layout that defines the metastatic potential. We propose that metastatic risk is inherently dictated by the geometric arrangement of the tumor microenvironment at the interface with tumor cells. Our model is designed to explicitly capture the spatial relationships between tumor cells, tumor associated fibroblasts and infiltrating lymphocytes. For this purpose, we propose Distance aware Tissue Modeling for Multiple Instance Learning(DTMf-MIL), a novel method that reinforces visual features with explicit spatial priors. By computing signed distance functions (SDF) relative to tissue phenotypes, our model learns to recognize structural signatures of metastatic risk. This geometric awareness translates directly to superior clinical performance as DTMf-MIL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods that ignore spatial layout on metastasis prediction from tissue in the primary tumor. We further validate our approach on public benchmarks, demonstrating that spatial awareness consistently improves diagnostic accuracy across diverse clinical tasks.

</details>


### [42] [SATB-VR: Training Few-Step Video Restoration Diffusion Model using SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28677)
*Haoran Bai,Xiaoxu Chen,Xiaoyu Liu,Zongsheng Yue,Sibin Deng,Wangmeng Zuo,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SATB-VR 是一种少步视频复原范式，通过辅助预测器跳过早期低信噪比去噪步骤来加速推理，并提出信噪比感知轨迹混合（SATB）策略与去噪器驱动一致性（DDC）损失来解决训练-推理偏差问题，在合成、真实及AIGC场景下以≤5步即取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在视频复原中迭代步数多、效率低，而激进的单步蒸馏又常牺牲细粒度纹理恢复质量，亟需一种在少步推理下兼顾效率与复原质量的平衡方案。

Method: 核心方法包括三部分：(1) 辅助预测器跳步启动策略，显式绕过早期低SNR去噪步骤；(2) SNR感知轨迹混合（SATB），在前向过程中根据SNR动态混合预测器输出与真值轨迹构建噪声输入，迫使去噪器鲁棒补偿预测误差并平滑收敛至干净数据流形；(3) 去噪器驱动一致性（DDC）损失，利用同步更新的去噪器作为动态评估器，显式对齐内部特征以提升预测器精度。

Result: 在≤5步的灵活少步推理设置下，SATB-VR 在合成、真实世界及AIGC视频复原基准上均表现优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SATB-VR 成功实现了扩散模型在视频复原中效率与质量的最优平衡，解决了联合训练中的训练-推理偏差问题，为少步扩散视频复原提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: While diffusion models excel in video restoration, their reliance on extensive iterative steps limits efficiency. Conversely, aggressive single-step distillation often compromises fine texture recovery. To achieve an optimal balance, we present SATB-VR, a few-step paradigm that jump-starts the denoising process via an auxiliary predictor, explicitly bypassing early low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) steps. However, naive joint training of the predictor and the denoiser inherently introduces a severe train-inference discrepancy. To resolve this, we propose the SNR-Aware Trajectory Blending (SATB) strategy. During the forward process, SATB constructs the noisy input by dynamically blending the predictor's output with the ground-truth trajectory based on the SNRs. This forces the denoiser to robustly compensate for initial prediction errors while smoothly converging to the clean data manifold. Furthermore, we introduce a Denoiser-Driven Consistency (DDC) loss, leveraging the concurrently updated denoiser as a dynamic evaluator to explicitly align internal features and boost predictor accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, under flexible few-step inference regimes (\eg, $\le 5$ steps), SATB-VR performs favorably against existing approaches on synthetic, real-world, and AIGC benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [43] [Generating in the Limit with Infinitely Many Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28354)
*Irene Strauss,Alexandra Butoi,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将经典的语言识别极限范式重新构建为召回率-精确率权衡问题，引入"精度"概念，分析了在枚举、新颖性和有效性约束下的语言生成极限。核心贡献在于允许学习者犯无限次错误（只要错误频率趋零以保证精度为1），并证明当对手永久隐藏目标语言的大部分时，这种放松可严格提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的语言识别极限模型未能反映现代语言建模的实际需求——生成有效且新颖的字符串。现有研究表明，对目标语言的广泛覆盖（高召回）往往以牺牲有效性（高精度）为代价。本文旨在将这种张力形式化为经典的召回率-精确率权衡，并构建更贴近大语言模型实际场景的理论框架。

Method: （1）引入"精度"（precision）概念，将语言生成问题形式化为召回率-精确率权衡；（2）分析不同约束条件（枚举、新颖性、有效性）下的生成极限；（3）提出"非最终有效"学习者模型，允许无限次错误但要求错误频率趋于零以保持精度为1；（4）引入新颖性约束的连续放松——仅要求输出中固定比例是新颖的；（5）理论分析上述放松对召回率的影响。

Result: （1）当对手永久隐藏目标语言的大部分时，允许无限次但频率趋零的错误可严格提升召回率；（2）将新颖性约束放松为仅要求固定比例输出为新颖，使得模型更贴近实际场景中不可避免的重复和错误的现实。

Conclusion: 本文为语言生成建立了一个更现实的模型，承认偶尔的错误和重复不可避免，但可以通过理论保证对其速率进行控制。该框架更好地反映了大规模语言模型的实际行为特征。

Abstract: The classic paradigm of language identification in the limit models learning as a game between an adversary, who reveals strings from an unknown target language, and a learner tasked with identifying that language. The recently introduced framework of language generation in the limit shifted the objective to better reflect modern language modeling, requiring the learner to produce valid, unseen strings from the target language. Related work highlighted a fundamental tension: a broad coverage of the target often comes at the cost of validity. We introduce a new notion of precision and recast this problem as the classic recall-precision trade-off. We analyze generation in the limit under varying constraints on enumeration, novelty, and validity, aimed at reflecting settings closer to those encountered by large language models. A key contribution is our analysis of learners that are not eventually valid: we allow infinitely many mistakes, provided their frequency tends to zero so that precision remains one. We show that this relaxation can strictly increase recall when the adversary permanently withholds a large portion of the target language. We also study a continuous relaxation of the novelty constraint that requires only a fixed fraction of outputs to be novel. Taken together, our results move toward a more realistic model of language generation where occasional errors and repetitions are unavoidable, but their rates are controlled.

</details>


### [44] [Extracting Knowledge from an Arabic-English Machine-Readable Dictionary Using Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28457)
*Diaa M. Fayed,Aly A. Fahmy,Mohsen A. Rashwan,Wafaa K. Fayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种从阿拉伯语-英语Al-Mawrid词典中自动提取词汇信息的方法，利用n-gram分析和KWIC分析发现词汇模式，再通过手写规则进行信息抽取，获得了高精确率和同义词的高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP应用需要大量丰富的语言知识，而电子词典、百科全书和语料库等电子语言资源的普及使得自动提取词汇信息成为可能，以此克服知识获取瓶颈。

Method: 采用n-gram分析和关键词上下文（KWIC）分析来发现体现形态、句法或语义信息的词汇模式；随后使用手工编写的基于规则的信息抽取方法提取这些信息；此外，利用标点符号和启发式规则提取子词条中的同义词集合。

Result: 所有信息类型的精确率均较高；同义词的召回率高，但其他类型信息的召回率较低。研究还揭示Al-Mawrid词典中含有大量派生（形态信息）、同义词、领域标签以及上下位关系（语义信息）。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过自动模式发现与规则抽取相结合的方法，可以高效地从双语词典中获取结构化词汇知识，尤其是同义词和派生信息，但形态和语义信息的召回率仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Natural language processing (NLP) applications need large and rich amount of linguistic knowledge. Furthermore, electronic language sources such as dictionaries, encyclopedia, and corpora became available. So, automatic methods are emerged to extract lexical information from those sources to overcome the knowledge acquisition bottleneck. We presented a method to automatically extract lexical information from a machine-readable version of the Arabic-English Al-Mawrid dictionary. We used n-gram analysis and key-word-in-context (KWIC) analysis to discover lexical patterns that manifest morphologic, syntactic, or semantic information. Then, we used hand-crafted rule-based information extraction to extract that information. Furthermore, we used punctuation marks and some heuristics to extract a set of synonyms in a subentry. This study registered high precision for all types of information, high recall for synonyms, and low recall for the other information. The study also showed that the Al-Mawrid has significant amount of derivations (morphologic information) and synonyms, domain labels, and hyponym/hypernym relations (semantic information).

</details>


### [45] [Developmental Trajectories of Situation Modeling and Mentalizing in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28524)
*Pamela D. Rivière,Cameron Jones,Sean Trott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 采用发展心理学视角，追踪Olmo2和Pythia系列语言模型在不同训练阶段的心理状态推理行为模式，发现错误信念任务（FBT）表现依赖于模型规模和充足训练量，出现于预训练后期，并由后训练干预（SFT、DPO）改善，但该表现具有脆弱性——非事实性动词会干扰判断，且情境模型虽先于FBT能力出现，却存在令人惊讶的不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明LLM对文本中描述的主体信念状态敏感（通过错误信念任务测量），但其构念效度仍存疑。作者旨在通过发展性视角，系统追踪心理状态推理能力的涌现过程及其前提条件，以更深入地理解LLM是否真正具备心理化能力。

Method: 采用发展性分析框架，在Olmo2和Pythia两个语言模型套件的多个训练阶段（预训练不同checkpoint、SFT、DPO等）上，系统评估两类能力：（1）错误信念任务（FBT）表现，包括真实信念和错误信念、外显和内隐条件；（2）情境建模能力，即模型对场景基本事实属性的报告准确性。同时通过操控动词的事实性（如"thinks" vs "knows"）进行压力测试。

Result: （1）高于随机水平的FBT表现依赖于模型规模和充足训练量，在预训练中较晚出现，后训练干预（SFT、DPO）对最诊断心理化的条件（错误信念、内隐）改善最大；（2）FBT表现脆弱：非事实性动词（如thinks）即使在真实信念条件下也增加错误信念归因；（3）情境建模准确性通常先于并超过FBT准确性，但Olmo2 13b在评估始终知晓物品真实位置的反派主体知识状态时，仍受目标主体知识状态和非事实性动词的持续影响，表现出不一致性。

Conclusion: 更大且充分训练的模型能以发展上合理的顺序构建部分连贯的情境模型，但仍表现出令人惊讶的脆弱性。这凸显了发展性视角和压力测试方法在评估LLM能力中的重要价值，也表明当前模型的心理化能力尚不稳健。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that Large Language Models (LLMs) are sensitive to the belief states of agents described by text, as measured by the false belief task (FBT), yet persistent concerns of construct validity remain. We adopt a **developmental perspective**, tracing the pattern of mental state reasoning behavior -- and likely **preconditions** for this behavior -- across multiple training stages in the Olmo2 and Pythia language model suites. We find that above-chance FBT performance depends both on model size and sufficient training volume, emerges relatively late in pretraining, and is most improved by post-training interventions (SFT, DPO) in the condition most diagnostic of mentalizing (False Belief, Implicit). However, FBT performance is fragile: consistent with past work, the use of non-factive verbs (e.g., thinks) increases false belief attributions even in the True Belief condition. To contextualize these findings, we track the emergence of **situation modeling**: the ability to report on basic factual properties of a described scene. Situation modeling accuracy generally precedes and exceeds FBT accuracy, yet situational representations also prove surprisingly incoherent in certain respects: when asked about the knowledge states of the Antagonist agent -- who always knows the item's true location -- Olmo2 13b is consistently influenced both by the Target agent's knowledge state and the presence of non-factive verbs. Together, these results suggest that larger, sufficiently trained models build partially coherent situation models in a developmentally appropriate sequence, yet display surprising fragility -- highlighting the value of developmental and stress-testing approaches for evaluating LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [46] [A French OSCE Dialogue Dataset and Controllable Virtual Patient System for Clinical Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28526)
*Doria Bonzi,Tom Bourgeade,Fabrice Lefèvre,Irina Illina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于LLM的可控合成管道，用于生成法语OSCE医患对话，包含检索增强与反思循环等模块，并设计了多层级评估框架，最终实现了一个可交互的虚拟患者原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学教育中OSCE训练受限于标准化病人（SP）资源稀缺，现有虚拟患者（VP）系统在真实性和可控性方面存在不足，亟需一种能够大规模生成高质量、可控的OSCE训练对话的方法。

Method: （1）构建了一个包含240条法语OSCE医患对话的数据集；（2）设计了基于LLM的可控对话生成管道，整合检索增强（retrieval-based grounding）与反思循环（reflection loop）等模块化组件以保障患者保真度和对话连贯性；（3）提出多层级评估框架，采用LLM-as-a-Judge方式从患者模拟质量、学生表现和语言质量三个维度进行评估；（4）实现了交互式原型，支持学生与VP练习并获取自动反馈。

Result: 实验表明，可控性模块普遍提升了患者保真度和学生评估的一致性，验证了检索增强与反思循环等组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了可控LLM管道在生成高质量OSCE训练对话方面的潜力，为缓解标准化病人资源短缺提供了可行方案，并通过交互式原型初步验证了其实用性。

Abstract: The clinical and communication skills of medical students are commonly assessed through Objective Structured Clinical Examinations (OSCEs), which consist of brief scenario-driven simulations of doctor-patient interactions. However, training is often limited by the low availability of human standardized patients, motivating the development of realistic virtual patients (VPs). To address this gap, we introduce a French OSCE dialogue dataset comprising 240 student-patient training interactions. We build upon it a controllable LLM-based pipeline to generate synthetic OSCE dialogues. The pipeline integrates modular components, such as retrieval-based grounding and a reflection loop, to ensure patient fidelity, coherence, and realism. Additionally, we propose a multi-level evaluation framework assessing patient simulation quality, student performance, and linguistic quality, using an LLM-as-a-Judge approach. Experiments suggest that controllability modules generally improve patient fidelity and student evaluation consistency. Finally, we implement an interactive prototype in which students can practice with a VP and receive automatic feedback.

</details>


### [47] [Legal Domain Adaptation of Modern BERT Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28538)
*Dominik Stammbach,Peter Henderson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究ModernBERT在法律领域的领域自适应，通过对所有美国法院判决书进行掩码语言建模的进一步预训练，发现即便ModernBERT已使用约500倍于原始BERT的数据训练，仍能从法律领域自适应中显著获益，在涉及美国法院判决书的所有数据集上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索现代BERT模型（ModernBERT）在法律领域的领域自适应效果。尽管ModernBERT的训练数据量远超原始BERT，但法律文本具有独特的术语、句法和文体风格，通用预训练模型可能无法充分捕捉这些领域特征，因此有必要研究进一步领域预训练是否仍能带来性能增益。

Method: 以ModernBERT为基础模型，使用掩码语言建模（MLM）目标在所有美国法院判决书（US court opinions）上进行进一步的领域自适应预训练；同时对比了从头开始在法律数据上预训练与在现有ModernBERT检查点上继续预训练两种策略的效果差异。

Result: 1）进一步预训练后的模型在所有与美国法院判决书相关的下游数据集上均显著优于原始ModernBERT；2）性能增益幅度与早期BERT类模型领域自适应的研究结果相当；3）从头开始预训练的表现不及在现有ModernBERT检查点上继续预训练；4）最终模型支持处理长达8,192个token的序列，可用于法律段落嵌入计算和法律检索重排序。

Conclusion: 即使在已经过海量数据训练的ModernBERT上，法律领域自适应仍然能带来与早期BERT时代相当的显著提升。继续预训练策略优于从头预训练。作者公开了所有模型检查点，该模型适用于法律文本嵌入和检索重排序等实际任务。

Abstract: We investigate domain adaptation of modern BERT models in the legal domain. We further pre-train ModernBERT on all US court opinions using the masked language modeling objective. Although ModernBERT has been trained on roughly 500x more data than original BERT, we still find that this model benefits from further pre-training and domain adaptation in the legal domain: we report significant improvements compared to vanilla ModernBERT on all datasets connected to US court opinions. We find gains similar to those reported in early work on domain adaptation of BERT-like models. However, from scratch pre-training does not match the performance of further pre-training an existing ModernBERT checkpoint in our experiments. The resulting models are capable of processing sequences up to 8,192 tokens, and can be used to compute meaningful embeddings of legal passages, or could quickly rerank hundreds of legal passages for a given search query. We release all model checkpoints publicly.

</details>


### [48] [Turn-Averaged SAEs for Feature Discovery and Long-Context Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28548)
*Kevin Der,Harish Kamath,Ben Thompson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"轮次平均稀疏自编码器"（Turn-Averaged SAEs），通过对单个对话轮次中所有token激活值取平均后重建，将每个轮次用固定数量的特征表示，从而解决了标准SAEs特征数量随上下文长度线性增长的问题，使得长文本可解释性分析更加实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器（SAEs）逐token提取特征，导致活跃特征数量随上下文长度线性增长，处理长对话文本时计算和分析极其困难。需要一种能够以固定规模表示高层语义特征的架构。

Method: 提出轮次平均稀疏自编码器（Turn-Averaged SAEs）：对一个完整对话轮次（Human或Assistant单轮发言）内所有token的模型激活值取平均，然后训练SAE学习重建该轮次平均激活。每个轮次用固定数量的稀疏特征表示，而非逐token表示。

Result: 经LLM评判，轮次平均特征在描述单轮高层特性方面比逐token特征更完整；轮次平均SAEs显著简化了归因图等下游SAE应用，使可解释性技术在长上下文场景下变得实用。

Conclusion: 轮次平均SAEs通过压缩时序维度，将SAE特征数量与上下文长度解耦，在保持语义表达完整性的同时大幅降低了计算复杂度，为长文本可解释性分析提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a useful tool for extracting interpretable features in language models. However, standard SAE architectures operate on individual token activations, meaning that the number of active features scales linearly with context length, and studying long model transcripts becomes difficult. We introduce turn-averaged SAEs, which represent a single Human or Assistant turn with a fixed number of features by learning to reconstruct the average model activation across the turn. We find that turn-averaged features describe a single turn's high-level characteristics more completely than per-token features when judged by an LLM. We also demonstrate that turn-averaged SAEs greatly simplify common downstream uses of SAEs like attribution graphs. Broadly, turn-averaged SAEs make interpretability techniques practical at long context lengths.

</details>


### [49] [Depth-Staggered Fibonacci Spacing for Sparse Attention: Static Schedules Beat Learned Dilation and Extrapolate Where Dense Attention Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28560)
*Chad A. Capps*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了稀疏自注意力机制，每个查询关注一个密集局部窗口加上一组基于斐波那契间距的偏移位置，并通过逐层标量α压缩或扩展间距。在21个统一规格的语言模型上比较了四种α设置方式，发现静态逐层交错排列在困惑度上优于固定和可学习方案，且所有稀疏变体均能外推至训练长度的4倍而无明显退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer自注意力的二次复杂度限制了长序列建模。稀疏注意力可以降低计算成本，但如何设计注意力模式以及跨层分配稀疏模式（α参数）仍未充分探索。作者希望系统性地研究不同α设置策略对模型质量、外推能力和推理效率的影响。

Method: 采用Fibonacci自注意力：每个查询关注一个密集局部窗口加上一组斐波那契数列间距的稀疏偏移位置。引入逐层标量α来控制间距的压缩或扩展。在60M参数、16层、512隐藏维度、426M训练token的统一配方下训练21个语言模型，比较四种α设置方式：(1)固定α；(2)逐层可学习α；(3)静态线性交错排列；(4)互质重分配。同时以2的幂次间距作为基线对照。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1)静态逐层交错排列在困惑度上优于固定和可学习α，且该收益与基底无关——将相同交错应用于2的幂次基底可使其超越固定Fibonacci并达到与可学习Fibonacci持平；(2)逐层学习α表现惰性，未能击败静态调度，且推理延迟约为其5倍；(3)所有稀疏变体可外推至4倍训练长度而几乎无退化，而密集基线在4倍长度时困惑度上升201%。此外，最佳稀疏模型在训练长度上的困惑度比密集基线高约26%，且交错收益在上下文位置上分布均匀而非集中于长程。

Conclusion: 静态逐层交错排列是一种简单且有效的稀疏注意力跨层分配策略，兼具良好的外推能力。逐层可学习方案并未带来额外收益且推理开销大。固定偏移的稀疏注意力天然支持长度外推，但以训练长度困惑度为代价。交错收益的均匀分布暗示其机制可能不同于直觉预期的长程建模改善。

Abstract: We study sparse self-attention in which each query attends to a dense local window plus a set of Fibonacci-spaced offsets, with a per-layer scalar alpha that compresses or expands the spacing. Across 21 language models trained under one matched recipe (60M parameters, 512 hidden, 16 layers, 426M tokens), we compare four ways of setting alpha across depth: fixed, per-layer learned, a static linear stagger, and a coprime (anti-gridding) reassignment of that stagger, together with a reach-matched power-of-2 control. Three results stand out. First, a static per-layer stagger improves perplexity over both fixed and learned alpha, and the gain is base-agnostic: applying the same stagger to a power-of-2 base lifts it above fixed Fibonacci and to parity with learned Fibonacci attention. Second, learning per layer is inert: it does not beat the static schedule and costs roughly five times the inference latency. Third, and most consequential, all sparse variants extrapolate to four times their training length with little or no degradation, whereas a recipe-matched dense baseline collapses (perplexity rises by 201% at 4x length); we attribute this to fixed-offset attention only ever querying relative positions seen during training. We also report two honest negatives: at training length the best sparse model has about 26% higher perplexity than the dense baseline, and the staggering gain is uniform across context positions rather than concentrated at long range.

</details>


### [50] [SEAD: Competence-Aware On-Policy Distillation via Entropy-Guided Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28562)
*Chia-Hsuan Lee,Zelei Cheng,Yu Wang,Renkun Ni,Sambit Sahu,Shi-Xiong Zhang,William Campbell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEAD 提出利用熵作为统一探针，从三个尺度（token、训练阶段、prompt）解决 on-policy distillation 中因学生能力变化导致的监督质量退化问题，在 OLMo 系列模型上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 on-policy distillation (OPD) 方法采用均匀监督策略，忽略了教师监督质量依赖于学生能力的特性：不连贯的 rollout 产生噪声梯度，已掌握的 token 产生冗余梯度。这在 token、训练阶段和 prompt 三个尺度上造成计算浪费。

Method: SEAD 以联合教师-学生熵作为统一探针，在三个尺度上设计组件：(1) Token 级别：根据联合熵将 token 分区，分配定制化散度或零梯度，约 50% token 被跳过；(2) 训练阶段级别：采用余弦调度，随学生能力增长从 forward KL 逐步退火至 reverse KL；(3) Prompt 级别：设计能力门控课程，按先易后难顺序引入 prompt。三个组件具有共生必要性。

Result: 在 OLMo-3（7B 到 32B）上，SEAD 在六个数学基准上比 vanilla OPD 平均提升 +4.8 准确率，消融实验证实组件间存在超加性交互。

Conclusion: SEAD 通过熵驱动的多尺度自适应监督，有效解决了 OPD 中学能力依赖的梯度质量退化问题，三个组件协同工作缺一不可，实现了显著的性能增益。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has a property absent in offline distillation and RL: teacher supervision quality depends on student competence. Incoherent rollouts yield noisy gradients; already-mastered tokens yield redundant ones. This creates waste at three scales (tokens, training phases, and prompts) yet existing methods supervise uniformly. We introduce SEAD, which uses entropy as a unified probe of this competence-dependent degradation at three scales: (1) joint teacher-student entropy partitions tokens into zones receiving tailored divergences or zero gradient (approx. 50% skipped); (2) a cosine schedule anneals from forward to reverse KL as competence grows; (3) a competence-gated curriculum introduces prompts easy-to-hard. These components are symbiotically necessary: token selection requires coherent rollouts (curriculum), annealing requires monotonic improvement (also curriculum). On OLMo-3 (7B to 32B), SEAD achieves +4.8 avg accuracy over vanilla OPD across six math benchmarks, with ablations confirming super-additive interactions.

</details>


### [51] [Correct codes for the wrong reasons? validating LLMs as measurement instruments for theoretical constructs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28574)
*Manuel Pita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"颗粒校准"(grain calibration)方法，通过将构念分解为子句级组件、以抽取式证据逐一检验、再经由显式的理论推导规则进行整合，从而区分LLM是真正测量了目标构念还是仅通过相关变量达成表面一致，将验证从"输出与人工标注对齐"转向"证明工具按理论指定的构念运行"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估LLM编码可靠性的方法仅关注编码结果与人类标注者的一致性，但这无法区分LLM是真正理解了构念的理论内涵，还是通过一个与构念相关的表面特征（correlate）达成了伪一致。构念效度（construct validity）的问题被完全忽视，且现有方法无法甄别这种差异。

Method: 提出"颗粒校准"(grain calibration)方法：(1)将构念分解为子句级别的理论组件；(2)用抽取式证据（extractive evidence）逐一检验每个组件是否在文本中得到满足；(3)通过显式的、由理论导出的组合规则对各组件结果进行整合。因规则是显式声明的而非封装在单次黑箱推理中，其结构本身即构成关于编码过程的证据。

Result: 该方法能够揭示：(1)哪些组件决定了一个编码结果；(2)当编码错误时，是某个组件被遗漏，还是相邻构念被误认。验证范式从"对工具输出与人工标注进行评分"转向"证明工具按照理论所指定的构念运行"。

Conclusion: 颗粒校准弥合了LLM编码中可靠性与构念效度之间的鸿沟，使验证从输出导向转向过程导向，为社会科学与人文领域中LLM编码的理论有效性提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: When a large language model (LLM) codes a construct in text as a human annotator would, that agreement makes the LLM a reliable coder. Yet reliability leaves construct validity untouched. The instrument may be theory-naive, reaching the code through a correlate that meets none of the demands the construct's theory makes, and no current method tells that apart from genuine measurement. We propose grain calibration as a method that closes the gap. It decomposes a construct into clause-level components, tests each against the text with extractive evidence, and combines the results through an explicit, theory-derived rule. Because the rule is stated rather than lodged in one opaque pass, its structure is evidence about the process rather than the output. It shows which components settled a code, and, when the code is wrong, whether a component was missed or an adjacent construct mistaken for it. Validation shifts from scoring an instrument's outputs against an annotator to showing that the instrument runs on the construct its theory specifies.

</details>


### [52] [Phonological Perception of Sign Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28667)
*Kayo Yin,Jessica Carter,Alex Xijie Lu,Annemarie Kocab*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究手语识别（SLR）深度学习模型是否具备音系感知能力，通过在ASL最小对立对上的探测实验和与人类行为数据的表征对齐分析，发现模型展现出涌现的音系敏感性，但受限于架构归纳偏置：基于姿态的模型对手形敏感，基于像素的模型对位置变化更敏感，且姿态模型与人类感知相似度判断的相关性达r≈0.49。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SLR模型在翻译基准上性能不断提升，但这些模型究竟是学会了抽象的音系特征（如handshape、location、movement等子词级参数），还是仅依赖低级统计相关性，这一问题尚未得到澄清。理解这一点对于构建真正理解手语结构的模型至关重要。

Method: 以美国手语（ASL）为对象，采用两种探测方法：(1) 利用最小对立对（minimal pairs）探测模型的音系敏感性，检验模型能否区分仅在某一个音系参数上有差异的手语词；(2) 评估模型潜在表征与人类感知相似度行为数据之间的表征对齐程度。同时对比了基于姿态（pose-based）和基于像素（pixel-based）两种架构的SLR模型。

Result: SLR模型确实展现出涌现的音系敏感性，但存在明显的架构差异：基于姿态的模型对手形（handshape）对比更敏感，基于像素的模型则更善于捕捉位置（location）变化。此外，基于姿态的模型学到的潜在表征与人类感知相似度判断显著相关（r≈0.49）。

Conclusion: 虽然SLR模型展现出涌现的音系能力，但当前训练范式尚不足以使模型超越其架构归纳偏置，暗示需要新的训练方法来实现更全面的音系特征学习。

Abstract: Sign languages are compositional systems where meaning arises by combining sublexical phonological parameters, such as handshape, location, and movement. While deep learning models for Sign Language Recognition (SLR) have achieved increased performance on translation benchmarks, it remains unclear whether these models distinguish abstract phonological features or merely rely on low-level statistical correlations. This work evaluates the phonological perception of SLR models trained on American Sign Language (ASL) by probing phonological sensitivity using minimal pairs and evaluating representational alignment with human behavioral data. Our results reveal that SLR models exhibit emergent phonological sensitivity, but with clear architectural trade-offs: pose-based models are sensitive to handshape contrasts, while pixel-based models better capture location changes. Furthermore, pose-based models learn latent representations that correlate with human perceptual similarity judgments (r~0.49). These findings suggest that while SLR models exhibit emergent phonology, current training paradigms are insufficient to scale them beyond their architectural inductive biases.

</details>


### [53] [AnTenA: Actionable and Explainable Tensor Analysis System with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28708)
*Dawon Ahn,Auder Der,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AnTenA方法，利用大语言模型(LLM)的知识来解释多维度数据中通过张量分解提取的共聚类潜在模式，并通过任务无关和任务特定的提示策略生成解释，以前向和后向推理任务评估解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多维度数据模式解释依赖标签或辅助元数据，但这些数据往往不准确（如不规范、不一致）、不充分（如静态表格元数据无法描述时变记录）或完全不可用，因此需要一种不依赖标签和辅助数据的解释方法。

Method: 提出AnTenA方法：首先利用张量分解从多维度数据中提取共聚类潜在模式，然后设计任务无关(task-agnostic)和任务特定(task-specific)两类提示，引导大语言模型对提取的潜在模式生成可读的自然语言解释。

Result: 通过前向推理(forward inference)和后向推理(backward inference)任务评估LLM生成解释的质量，验证了方法的有效性。演示系统已开源发布。

Conclusion: AnTenA成功利用LLM的预训练知识为无标签、无辅助元数据的多维度数据中的隐藏模式提供可解释性，为张量分解结果赋予了人类可理解的语义解释，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Accurately explaining hidden patterns in multi-aspect data has typically been done by leveraging labels and/or accompanying auxiliary metadata. However, labels and auxiliary data may be inaccurate (e.g. nonstandard, inconsistent), insufficient (e.g. static tabular metadata for time-dependent recordings), or unavailable. %
We propose \fullmethod (\method), which leverages the knowledge of large language models (LLMs) to explain the hidden patterns in human narratives. \method uses task-agnostic and task-specific prompts to explain extracted co-clustered latent patterns from tensor decomposition. To evaluate these explanations, we test the LLMs on forward and backward inference tasks. % Our demo system is available at https://github.com/dawonahn/ECML_PKDD_AnTenA.

</details>


### [54] [SEATauBench: Adapting Tool-Agent-User Evaluation Into Low-Resource Southeast Asian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28715)
*My Chiffon Nguyen,Aulia Adila,Saksorn Ruangtanusak,Kittiphat Leesombatwathana,Vissuta Gunawan Lim,Patomporn Payoungkhamdee,Samuel Cahyawijaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEATauBench 是首个面向东南亚主权AI的智能体评估框架，将 TauBench 适配到五种东南亚语言，发现仅改变对话语言时智能体能力迁移尚可，但随任务语境本地化程度加深，质量与鲁棒性急剧下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 东南亚地区AI开发与评估发展迅速，但智能体在区域语言中的能力仍被严重忽视，这对主权AI建设至关重要。现有基准缺乏针对东南亚语言的多维度智能体评估。

Method: 将 TauBench 适配到普通话、越南语、泰语、印尼语和菲律宾语五种语言，设计渐进式本地化设置：依次改变用户-智能体交互语言、工具规范语言和任务领域，在三款近期模型上进行系统评估。

Result: 仅改变对话语言时，英语智能体能力迁移尚可；但随着工具规范和任务领域本地化，质量与鲁棒性急剧下降，全领域适配时损失最大；同时揭示了仅用英语评估智能体在东南亚语言中能力的局限性。

Conclusion: SEATauBench 为语言多样地区构建可靠多语言智能体提供了诊断基准和可复用适配流水线，呼吁超越英语中心的智能体评估范式。

Abstract: While AI development and evaluation for Southeast Asia (SEA) has grown rapidly, agent capabilities in regional languages are still poorly understood despite its importance to sovereign AI. To fill this gap, we introduce SEATauBench, the first agent-focused evaluation framework for SEA sovereign AI. SeaTau adapts TauBench to five languages -- Mandarin, Vietnamese, Thai, Indonesian, and Filipino -- and evaluates agents across progressively localized settings that vary the language of user-agent interaction, tool specifications, and task domains. Across three recent models, we find that English agent capabilities transfer reasonably well when only the conversation language changes, but quality and robustness degrade sharply as more task contexts are localized, with the largest losses in full domain adaptation. We also the limits of English-only agent assessment for measuring agent capabilities in SEA languages. More broadly, SeaTau provides a diagnostic benchmark and reusable adaptation pipeline for building reliable multilingual agents for linguistically diverse regions. Data and code can be accessed at github.com/SEACrowd/SEATauBench.

</details>


### [55] [DriftGuard: Safety-Aware Multi-Monitor Detection and Selective Adaptation for Evolving Toxicity Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28725)
*Yuting Xin,Hanyu Cai,Binqi Shen,Lier Jin,Lan Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DriftGuard 提出了一种安全感知的自适应毒性审核框架，通过多维漂移检测（全局文本漂移、身份危害漂移、模型不确定性、毒性风险漂移、假阴性风险漂移）与选择性模型更新相结合，在 Civil Comments 和 DynaHate 数据集上显著提升了毒性召回率，证明了安全感知监控比全局漂移检测更能捕捉安全关键性变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漂移检测方法主要关注全局分布变化，容易遗漏出现在局部危害子空间或高风险模型错误区域的安全关键性漂移。在线毒性审核环境中有害行为通过编码语言、目标转移和策略性规避不断演化，因此需要更细粒度的安全感知漂移检测与自适应更新机制。

Method: 提出 DriftGuard 框架，包含两大组件：(1) 多监控器漂移检测系统，同时跟踪全局文本漂移、身份危害漂移、模型不确定性、毒性风险漂移和假阴性风险漂移五类信号；(2) 当检测到安全相关变化时，采用 hard-mix 自适应集进行选择性模型更新，该集合优先纳入疑似假阴性样本、身份相关高风险样本、假阳性风险样本及不确定边界案例。

Result: 在 Civil Comments 时序漂移和 Jigsaw-to-DynaHate 跨数据集漂移实验中，安全感知监控器成功检测到全局漂移所遗漏的风险。Hard-mix 自适应更新在毒性召回率和准确率上均优于无更新和随机平衡基线：Civil Comments 毒性召回率达 0.8777，DynaHate 毒性召回从 0.7107 提升至 0.8523。Bootstrap 分析显示 DynaHate 上毒性召回提升 0.1418，假阴性率下降 0.0781，安全收益稳定。

Conclusion: DriftGuard 将安全感知漂移检测与定向轻量模型更新有效关联，为实现更鲁棒的自适应毒性审核提供了可行框架，证明多维安全监控与选择性重训练能显著提升模型在动态有害内容演化场景下的检测能力。

Abstract: Automated toxicity moderation systems operate in dynamic online environments where harmful behavior evolves through coded language, shifting targets, and strategic adaptation to enforcement. Existing drift detection methods often focus on global distributional change, but such signals may miss safety-relevant shifts that emerge in localized harm subspaces or high-risk model-error regions. This paper introduces DriftGuard, a safety-aware adaptive moderation framework that combines multi-monitor drift detection with selective model updating. The framework tracks global text drift, identity-harm drift, model uncertainty, toxic-risk drift, and false-negative-risk drift. When safety-relevant change is detected, the model is updated using a hard-mix adaptation set that prioritizes likely false negatives, identity-related high-risk examples, false-positive-risk examples, and uncertain boundary cases. Experiments on Civil Comments temporal shift and Jigsaw-to-DynaHate cross-dataset shift show that safety-aware monitors detect risks missed by global drift alone. Hard-mix adaptation improves toxic recall and accuracy over no-update and random-balanced baselines, raising toxic recall to 0.8777 on Civil Comments and from 0.7107 to 0.8523 on DynaHate. Bootstrap analysis further shows stable DynaHate safety gains, with toxic recall increasing by 0.1418 and false-negative prevalence decreasing by 0.0781. Overall, DriftGuard links safety-aware drift detection to targeted, lightweight model updating for more robust adaptive toxicity moderation.

</details>


### [56] [5ting at SemEval-2026 Task 8: Strong End-to-End Multi-Turn RAG via LLM-Based Reranking and Faithfulness Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28737)
*Thien-Qua-T-Nguyen,Chi Hoang,Nguyen Tran,Tri Le,Khanh Truong,Chinh Trong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了用于SemEval-2026 Task 8（MTRAGEval）的5ting系统，该系统针对多轮RAG场景中的上下文漂移、欠规范化和幻觉风险，采用BGE-M3稠密检索+FAISS索引、双查询合并检索、LLM重排序以及基于证据约束的角色分离式生成策略，在Task A中nDCG@5达到0.4719，在Task C中谐波分数为0.5597、RL_F为0.7692。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮检索增强生成（Multi-turn RAG）面临三大核心挑战：上下文漂移（context drift）、用户查询欠规范化（under-specification）以及幻觉风险（hallucination risk）。现有单轮RAG系统难以有效应对这些多轮交互中的动态变化，亟需设计一个能够跨轮次维护检索精度并约束生成忠实度的端到端系统。

Method: 5ting系统采用四阶段流水线架构：（1）使用BGE-M3稠密检索模型结合FAISS向量索引进行高效语义检索；（2）提出双查询合并检索（dual-query merged retrieval）策略，融合当前轮次与历史上下文信息以缓解上下文漂移；（3）引入基于大语言模型的重排序模块对候选文档进行精排；（4）在生成阶段采用角色分离（role-separated）架构，将生成过程显式约束于检索到的证据之上，以抑制幻觉输出。

Result: 在SemEval-2026 Task 8的两个子任务上取得如下结果：Task A（检索子任务）中，nDCG@5达到0.4719；Task C（端到端子任务）中，谐波分数（harmonic score）为0.5597，RL_F指标为0.7692。

Conclusion: 5ting系统通过稠密检索、双查询融合、LLM重排序与证据约束生成的组合策略，有效缓解了多轮RAG场景下的上下文漂移与幻觉问题，在MTRAGEval基准上取得了具有竞争力的检索与端到端生成性能。

Abstract: We introduce 5ting, our system for the SemEval2026 Task 8 (MTRAGEval), which evaluates multi-turn Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems. Multi turn RAG involves context drift, under specification, and hallucination risk. Our system combines BGE-M3 dense retrieval with FAISS indexing, dual-query merged retrieval, and LLM based reranking, followed by role separated generation constrained to retrieved evidence. The retriever achieved nDCG@5 = 0.4719 in Task A, while the end to end system ranked in Task C with a harmonic score of 0.5597 and RL_F = 0.7692.

</details>


### [57] [Majority Vote Silences Minority Values: Annotator Disagreement at the Hate/Offensive Boundary in HateXplain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28772)
*Joshua Muhumuza,Joab Ezra Agaba,Mercy Amiyo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究揭示仇恨言论标注中，多数投票聚合掩盖了标注者在"仇恨/冒犯"边界上的系统性分歧（42.6%的分歧集中于此）。硬标签和软标签模型在边界样本上准确率均大幅下降（22个百分点），且硬标签模型对边界错误表现出更高的置信度，使标准评估指标无法检测到失败。三种下游干预均未能恢复边界准确率，作者认为问题在于将争议性判断作为真值标签的结构性缺陷，主张干预应上移至标注设计阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仇恨言论检测流程中，标注者分歧被常规地通过多数投票压缩为单一硬标签用于训练，但这种聚合过程的非中立性及其对模型行为和评估的影响尚未被系统研究。作者旨在揭示：标注分歧并非随机噪声，而是集中在特定边界上的结构性现象，且这种聚合会系统性地掩盖模型在争议性样本上的失败。

Method: 使用HateXplain数据集进行标注分歧分布分析（卡方检验验证非均匀分布）。设计三种模型架构对比：硬标签BERT（Model A）、软标签模型（Model B）、以及逐标注者多头模型（Model C）。在"一致标注帖子"与"分歧帖子"上分别评估准确率，并分析模型置信度差异。进一步尝试三种渐进复杂度的下游干预（未具体列明但声明均失败）。

Result: 42.6%的标注分歧集中在"仇恨言论/冒犯性言论"边界（χ²=135.199, df=2, p<0.0001）。Model A和Model B在分歧帖上的准确率比一致帖下降约22个百分点（~80%→~58%），Model C差距扩大至28个百分点，且冒犯类准确率降至24.5%。Model A对边界错误表现出比Model C更高的置信度（0.710 vs 0.495, p<0.0001），标准评估指标因此无法检测到此失败。三种下游干预均未能恢复边界准确率。

Conclusion: 问题本质是结构性的：多数投票将争议性判断伪装成客观真值，模型继承了这种虚假确定性。修复不应在下游进行，而必须从上游标注设计入手，承认并保留标注分歧中的信号，而非将其粗暴消除。

Abstract: Hate speech annotation pipelines routinely collapse annotator disagreement into majority vote labels before training. We show that this aggregation is not neutral: 42.6% of all annotator disagreement in HateXplain concentrates specifically at the hate/offensive boundary, a pattern consistent with annotators applying different thresholds for where hate begins (chi-squared = 135.199, df = 2, p < 0.0001). Both a hard-label BERT model (Model A) and a soft-label model (Model B) drop 22 percentage points in accuracy from agreed posts (~80%) to disagreement posts (~58%), confirmed at p < 0.0001. A per-annotator multi-head model (Model C) widens this gap further to 28 points while collapsing offensive disagreement accuracy to 0.245. Critically, Model A expresses significantly higher confidence on boundary case errors than Model C (0.710 vs. 0.495, p < 0.0001), meaning standard evaluation metrics will not detect the failure. Three downstream interventions of increasing sophistication all fail to recover boundary accuracy. We argue the problem is structural. Majority vote presents a contested judgment as ground truth, and models inherit that false certainty. The intervention must be upstream in annotation design.

</details>


### [58] [Structure-Preserving Document Translation via Multi-Stage LLM Pipeline: A Case Study in Marathi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28796)
*Manasi Waghe,Danish Chandargi,Mohammad Aamir Rayyan,Raviraj Joshi,A. R. Deshpande*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保留文档结构的马拉地语到英语的政府文件翻译框架，通过布局感知OCR、大语言模型翻译和HTML结构化重建，实现端到端的文档转换，同时保持版面布局保真度和术语一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度政府文件多以马拉地语等地方语言发布，现有神经机器翻译系统忽视文档结构、格式完整性和领域术语，导致非本地读者和行政机构面临严重的可访问性障碍。

Method: 提出结构保留的翻译框架，整合了布局感知光学字符识别（OCR）、基于坐标的文本提取、大语言模型（LLM）翻译，以及通过HTML表示的结构化文档重建。通过施加空间对齐约束和保留层级文档元素，确保源文档与译文之间的结构一致性。

Result: 在真实马拉地语政府PDF上的实验评估表明，相比传统纯文本翻译流程，该框架在结构保留度、翻译连贯性和术语一致性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为电子政务和行政文件处理提供了可扩展的多语言可访问性解决方案，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Government documents in India are predominantly issued in regional languages such as Marathi, creating substantial accessibility barriers for non-native readers, interstate administrative bodies, and policy analysts. Although recent advances in neural machine translation have improved sentence-level translation quality, existing systems largely neglect document structure, formatting integrity, and domain-specific terminology, thereby limiting their applicability to official documentation. This paper presents a structure-preserving Marathi-to-English government document translation framework capable of performing end-to-end document transformation while maintaining layout fidelity. The proposed system integrates layout-aware optical character recognition, coordinate-based text extraction, large language model based translation, and structured document reconstruction through HTML representations. By enforcing spatial alignment constraints and preserving hierarchical document elements, the framework ensures structural consistency between the source and translated documents. Experimental evaluation on real-world Marathi government PDFs demonstrates improved structural preservation, translation coherence, and terminological consistency compared to conventional text-only translation pipelines. The proposed framework contributes toward scalable multilingual accessibility solutions for e-governance and administrative document processing.

</details>


### [59] [Labeling Training Data for Entity Matching Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28823)
*Aaron Steiner,Christian Bizer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用知识蒸馏工作流，以LLM为教师模型自动标注训练数据，再训练小型学生模型（如RoBERTa、Ditto）进行实体匹配。实验表明，机器标注训练的学生模型与人工标注训练的模型性能接近（F1差距不超2个点），而成本仅约28-41美元，大幅节省约470小时人工标注工作量；推理速度比直接使用LLM快41.5-534倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在实体匹配上表现优异但推理慢、成本高；传统小模型推理快但需要大量任务特定的人工标注训练数据。本文试图通过知识蒸馏，用LLM自动标注来消除人工标注需求，兼具两者优势。

Method: 以LLM为教师模型，通过不同配对选择策略筛选候选实体对，由LLM标注后（含后处理），用于训练小型学生模型（如RoBERTa、Ditto）。系统考察了配对选择策略、教师模型、标签后处理方法和学生模型四个维度，在五个基准数据集上进行评估。

Result: 学生模型在机器标注数据上训练后，与使用基准训练集训练的性能相当，F1差距在两个方向均不超过2个点。GPT-5.2标注五个基准数据的成本为28.31-40.88美元，而人工标注同类数据估计需470小时。Ditto推理速度比直接使用LLM快41.5到534倍。

Conclusion: 当前LLM结合适当的配对选择方法，可以大幅减少甚至消除实体匹配中特定用例训练数据的人工标注工作，知识蒸馏是连接LLM强大能力与小模型高效推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on entity matching without requiring task-specific training data. However, applying these models to large sets of candidate pairs remains slow and costly. In contrast, entity matchers using traditional machine learning methods or small language models (SLMs), such as RoBERTa, offer much faster inference but require task-specific training data.
  This paper investigates whether the need to provide task-specific training data can be avoided by using knowledge-distillation workflows, in which an LLM serves as a teacher model to label training pairs that are subsequently used to train a smaller student model. We investigate knowledge distillation for entity matching along the following dimensions: pair-selection strategy, teacher model, label post-processing method, and student model. We evaluate the workflows using the Abt-Buy, Walmart-Amazon, WDC Products, DBLP-ACM, and DBLP-Scholar benchmarks, and compare the performance of student models trained with machine-labeled data to the performance of the same models trained using the benchmark training sets.
  Our experiments show that student models trained using the machine-labeled sets perform approximately on par with models trained on the benchmark training sets, with the remaining differences in both directions staying below two F1 points. Using GPT-5.2 to label the training sets for all five benchmarks costs US\$28.31 to US\$40.88, whereas manually labeling the same training sets is estimated to require 470 hours of work. At inference time, Ditto is 41.5 to 534 times faster than directly using an LLM to perform the matching tasks.
  These results indicate that current LLMs, when combined with a suitable pair-selection method, can substantially reduce or even eliminate the manual effort required to label use case-specific training data for entity matching.

</details>


### [60] [The Heterogeneous Safety Impacts of Benign Multilingual Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28843)
*Will Hawkins,Kaivalya Rawal,Jonathan Rystrøm,Stratis Tsirtsis,Zihao Fu,Greta Warren,Ryan Brown,Eoin Delaney,Sandra Wachter,Brent Mittelstadt,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统实证研究了多语言场景下的“安全漂移”现象：使用九种语言的良性数据微调大模型，发现即使是非对抗数据，也会显著增加模型对不安全提示的服从率（最高四倍），且该现象与通用能力变化解耦，在不同语言和模型间呈现异质性。单纯评估英语安全性不足以保障部署安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，即使在良性数据上微调也会损害大模型的安全性，但此前所有工作仅限于英语场景。在多语言部署日益普遍的背景下，缺乏对跨语言安全漂移现象的理解，这构成了严重的安全盲区。

Method: 作者在Llama-3.2、Qwen3、Gemma-3三个模型上，使用翻译成九种语言的良性微调数据（Multilingual-Benign-Tune数据集）进行微调，并构建SORRY-Bench-Multilingual评估套件，系统测量不同微调语言与评估语言组合下的安全服从率、内部表征漂移及通用能力变化。

Result: 安全性结果对微调语言和评估语言的选择高度敏感，某些设置下对抗服从率增加四倍；多语言安全漂移与通用能力指标脱钩；非英语微调产生的内部表征漂移通常小于英语，但这些漂移会导致模型走向极端服从或极端拒绝；仅评估英语安全性无法保证多语言部署的可靠性。

Conclusion: 多语言安全漂移是一个被忽视的关键问题，与能力变化相互独立。研究者与从业者必须在多语言维度上评估微调后的安全性，而非仅依赖英语评估。作者开源了相关数据集与评估套件以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model is a ubiquitous method for enhancing its capability on a specific downstream task. However, prior work has shown that this increase in capability comes with a cost: it can increase a model's tendency to respond to unsafe adversarial prompts, even when fine-tuning with non-adversarial data. We present the first comprehensive empirical study of this phenomenon in multilingual settings by fine-tuning Llama-3.2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models using benign data translated across nine languages. We find that safety outcomes are highly sensitive to both the choice of fine-tuning language and the evaluation language, with adversarial compliance rates increasing four-fold in some settings. Multilingual safety drift is decoupled from general capability metrics, and occurs heterogeneously across languages and models. Fine-tuning in non-English languages often induces smaller internal representational drifts than English, but these shifts lead models to default to either exaggerated compliance or refusal. As such, assessing fine-tuning impacts solely in English provides inadequate assurance for deployment. To facilitate further research into these cross-lingual safety blind spots, we release the Multilingual-Benign-Tune dataset and the SORRY-Bench-Multilingual evaluation suite.

</details>


### [61] [Open but Incompatible: A License Compatibility Analysis of Corpora for Low-Resource African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28867)
*Ernst van Gassen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文审计了非洲NLP语料库的许可证合规性，发现Creative Commons许可证虽占主导地位，但其兼容性规则常被忽视。作者构建了六层兼容性矩阵，通过三个案例语言揭示了四类失败模式，并提出了预标注尽职调查清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲NLP领域大量语料库采用Creative Commons许可证发布，但许可证兼容性规则（如CC-BY-SA与CC-BY-NC不可混合、NoDerivs条款默认禁止分词和标注等衍生操作）在实践中鲜少被遵守，导致法律风险和语料不可用问题。需要系统性地审计许可证来源并揭示合规失败。

Method: 作者对20多个非洲NLP语料库家族进行了许可证溯源的全面审计，构建了一个六层兼容性矩阵（从完全开放到完全禁止的梯度），并选取Kituba/Munukutuba、Zarma和Moore三种语言作为案例进行深入分析，通过一手来源证据记录具体的合规失败模式。

Result: 揭示了四种典型失败模式：（1）JW300因违反服务条款被从OPUS中移除，属于彻底禁止；（2）WAXAL声称CC-BY 4.0，但其HuggingFace数据集卡片自相矛盾，构成复合许可证误标；（3）Tanzil在CC-BY标签下隐藏了NoDerivs条款；（4）刚果广播语料库405个源URL中有402个已失效，属于数据持久性失败。

Conclusion: 非洲NLP语料库在许可证合规方面存在系统性问题，需要建立预标注尽职调查清单机制，并积极利用法律上合规的语料富集机会来改善现状。

Abstract: Creative Commons licenses dominate African NLP corpus releases, but their compatibility rules are rarely applied. CC-BY-SA and CC-BY-NC cannot be combined in a single published dataset; a NoDerivs clause silently prohibits tokenisation and annotation. This paper audits the license provenance of over twenty corpus families used in African NLP, constructs a six-tier compatibility matrix, and applies it to three case-study languages: Kituba/Munukutuba, Zarma, and Moore. Four failure modes are documented with primary-source evidence: outright prohibition (JW300, removed from OPUS after a legal audit confirmed Terms of Service violation); composite license misrepresentation (WAXAL, whose CC-BY 4.0 claim is contradicted by its own HuggingFace dataset card); a NoDerivs clause hidden behind a CC-BY label (Tanzil); and data persistence failure (the Congolese Radio Corpus, where 402 of 405 source URLs are now dead). A pre-annotation due diligence checklist and a survey of legally clean enrichment opportunities close the paper.

</details>


### [62] [Memory-Managed Long-Context Attention: A Preliminary Study of Editable Request-Local Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28876)
*Junyi Zou,Avrova Donz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种"内存管理式长上下文注意力"机制，将快速循环/稀疏骨干网络与显式可编辑的记忆槽及查询时稀疏回退相结合。通过结构化合成任务、自然语言生成和冻结模型诊断等实验，证明纯固定状态或纯稀疏方法在覆盖写入、版本管理、抗污染等场景中存在不足，而混合方法能同时覆盖两类路径，并指出开放域的选择机制仍是主要架构瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文语言模型常将"历史压缩为高效状态"与"维持可靠长期记忆"两个目标混为一谈。线性、循环及稀疏注意力虽能降低长序列处理成本，但未明确解决何时写入、覆盖、保护或丢弃事实的问题。因此需要探索将记忆生命周期管理与注意力机制解耦的研究路径。

Method: 提出内存管理式长上下文注意力：将快速循环或稀疏骨干网络与显式可编辑的请求局部记忆槽分离，并辅以查询时的稀疏回退机制。实验设计涵盖：(1) 结构化合成任务；(2) token/chunk/序列桥接；(3) 生成式自然语言；(4) 局部冻结模型诊断。使用2M token的压力测试、2.74M参数的最小因果事件-token模型、以及六族冻结隐藏状态桥接进行验证，并在RULER 4K和LongBench v1子集上进行诊断评估。

Result: 2,097,152 token压力测试在2-132个活跃chunk下达到50/50的池化准确率；2.74M参数的因果事件-token模型在轻量写入监督下达到595/600；六族冻结隐藏状态桥接在有oracle元数据的情况下达到1079/1080的控制指针准确率。局部RULER 4K诊断接近全上下文表现，但33条LongBench v1的16K子集显示朴素词汇选择不够通用。纯固定状态或纯稀疏方法在覆盖写入、版本管理、抗污染、无写入信号等场景中失败，混合方法覆盖两种路径。

Conclusion: 证据分离出三个论断：(1) 受控槽生命周期是可行的；(2) 当写入缺乏未来查询信号时需要稀疏回退；(3) 学习到的开放域选择仍是主要架构瓶颈。作者明确不声称最终生成架构、全局槽轨迹收敛或系统优越性。

Abstract: Long-context language models often conflate two different goals: compressing history into an efficient state, and maintaining reliable long-term memory. Linear, recurrent, and sparse attention reduce the cost of processing long sequences, but they do not by themselves specify when a fact should be written, overwritten, protected from distractors, or discarded. We study memory-managed long-context attention, a research route that separates a fast recurrent or sparse backbone from explicit editable request-local memory slots and query-time sparse fallback. Across structured synthetic tasks, token/chunk/sequence bridges, generated natural language, and local frozen-model diagnostics, pure fixed-state or pure sparse methods fail some overwrite, version, anti-pollution, or no-write-signal cases, while a hybrid covers both routes. A small 2,097,152-token mechanism stress test reaches 50/50 pooled accuracy with 2-132 active chunks. A 2.74M-parameter minimal causal event-token model reaches 595/600 with lite write supervision, supporting proof of trainability rather than scale. A six-family frozen-hidden-state bridge reaches 1079/1080 controlled pointer accuracy, but it uses generator-provided integer key IDs and separately encoded canonical key strings; it is an oracle-metadata probe, not open-text entity resolution. Local non-leaderboard RULER 4K diagnostics remain close to full context, whereas a 33-record LongBench v1 16K subset shows that naive lexical selection is not general. The evidence separates three claims: controlled slot lifecycle is feasible, sparse fallback is needed when writes lack future-query signals, and learned open-domain selection remains the main architectural bottleneck. We do not claim a final generative architecture, global slot-trajectory convergence, or systems superiority.

</details>


### [63] [PASTA: A Paraphrasing And Self-Training Approach for Knowledge Updating in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28898)
*Takayuki Yamamoto,Daisuke Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PASTA 框架通过数据增强、QA生成和新颖的自学习DPO过程，将新闻文章的事实知识整合进LLM，实现知识更新，准确率从0.02提升至0.82，同时保持通用语言能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练LLM的知识更新面临重大技术困难，且在回答特定事实信息（如新闻文章）问题时表现不佳。现有持续训练方法效果有限，亟需一种能够有效整合新事实知识、同时抑制幻觉的专门化方法。

Method: 提出PASTA框架，包含三个核心组件：（1）数据增强——对新闻文章进行多样化改写；（2）问答生成——基于增强后的文章自动生成问答对；（3）新颖的自学习DPO（Direct Preference Optimization）过程——同时实现知识覆写（knowledge overwriting）和幻觉抑制（hallucination suppression）。还通过系统分析学习参数和数据配置来探究有效知识更新的规律。

Result: 在基座模型知识截止日期后发布的网络文章上进行实验评估，PASTA将问答准确率从0.02大幅提升至0.82，同时保持了模型的通用语言能力。

Conclusion: PASTA是一种简单而强大的框架，能够有效将新闻文章中的详细事实信息作为新知识整合进LLM，成功构建了能够准确回答领域特定问题的专门化模型，为LLM知识更新提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Knowledge updating in pre-trained Large Language Models (LLMs) remains an important challenge. While continual training provides a potential avenue for knowledge updating, it continues to present substantial technical difficulties. Furthermore, LLMs often struggle with accurately answering questions about specific factual information, such as news articles - a capability limitation widely recognized in the research community. This paper proposes PASTA, a simple yet powerful framework for integrating detailed factual information from news articles as new knowledge into LLMs, with the primary goal of building specialized models that accurately answer questions about this knowledge. Our framework combines data augmentation, question-answering generation, and a novel self-learning DPO process that simultaneously enables knowledge overwriting and hallucination suppression. We provide insights into effective knowledge updating through systematic analysis of learning parameters and data configurations. In our experimental evaluation with web articles published after the base model's knowledge cutoff, PASTA achieved remarkable improvement from 0.02 to 0.82 accuracy while maintaining general language capabilities, demonstrating its effectiveness for creating domain-specialized LLMs.

</details>


### [64] [Latent Bridges for Multi-Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28916)
*Simone Varriale,Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts,Paolo Papotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GRAB 是一种用于表格问答的“构造器-编码器-桥接”流水线架构，将关系数据转化为异构图并通过消息传递编码，再以少量查询条件化的潜在 Token 桥接到冻结的 LLM 中，在保持 LLM 通用推理能力的同时高效实现关系深度学习的注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格问答方法难以有效将关系数据库中的结构化信息传递给 LLM，尤其在多表场景下表现不佳。作者希望找到一种原则性的方法，既能向 LLM 注入关系结构信息，又不破坏 LLM 原有的通用推理能力，同时保持训练高效。

Method: 提出 GRAB 流水线，分三步：(1) 构造器（Constructor）：将关系数据提升为异构图；(2) 编码器（Encoder）：通过消息传递对图进行编码；(3) 桥接（Bridge）：生成一小簇查询条件化的潜在 Token，将图结构信号与扁平化文本一起传递给 LLM。LLM 完全冻结，仅训练图编码器和潜在桥接模块（共 91M 参数）。

Result: GRAB 在关系问答任务上显著提升性能，尤其在更复杂的多表场景中增益最大，证明了该方法能以高效、原则性的方式连接关系深度学习与 LLM。

Conclusion: GRAB 提供了一种高效、轻量且原则性的方案，在不牺牲 LLM 通用推理能力的前提下，成功将关系结构信息注入表格问答流程，在多表等挑战性场景中尤为有效。

Abstract: We introduce GRAB, a constructor-encoder-bridge pipeline for table question answering. Our method lifts relational data into an heterogeneous graph, encodes it via message passing, and transfers the signals to an LLM through a small set of query-conditioned latent tokens. This provides the LLM with a compact, task-relevant structural representation together with the flattened text. Crucially, the LLM remains strictly frozen to preserve its general reasoning capabilities; we train only the lightweight graph encoder and latent bridge (91M parameters), allowing the entire pipeline to be trained efficiently. Our pipeline significantly improves performance on relational Question Answering, with the largest gains in demanding multi-table settings, offering an efficient, principled way to connect relational deep learning with LLMs.

</details>


### [65] [FinInvest-GTCN: Explainable Graph-Temporal-Causal Modeling for Risk-Aware Investment Decision Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28933)
*Junyan Tan,Yifan Li,Minghao Wang,Zihan Chen,Haoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FinInvest-GTCN（图-时序-因果网络），将VC投资决策从内容推荐重新定义为量化风险-收益评估，结合关系图编码器、多尺度时序融合模块和因果决策头，并引入元因果自适应策略以应对数据稀缺场景，在专有VC数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VC投资决策面临多源异构数据、非平稳时间序列以及高 stakes 低数据场景下对可解释预测的需求等三大核心挑战。现有方法难以同时应对这些难题，亟需一个能整合异构信息、捕捉长期时序依赖、并提供因果可解释风险-收益评估的统一框架。

Method: 提出FinInvest-GTCN架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 关系图编码器，建模投资生态系统的拓扑结构；(2) 多尺度时序融合模块，处理长期依赖与非平稳性；(3) 因果决策头，生成风险调整预测并提供可解释的因果归因。创新性地引入元因果自适应（MCA）策略，通过将微调更新与元预训练导出的因果合理结构对齐，实现对数据稀缺新行业的稳健适配。

Result: 在专有VC数据集上的实验表明：FinInvest-GTCN将主要指标RA-MSE从基线3.05显著降至2.51，模拟投资组合的累计收益提升18.7%。消融实验验证了各组件的必要性，额外分析证实了模型的稳定性、可解释性和增强的适应性。

Conclusion: FinInvest-GTCN开创了一个数据驱动、可解释的投资决策支持框架，通过图学习、时序建模与因果推断的深度融合，有效解决了VC投资中的异构数据融合、非平稳时序预测和低数据场景适配三大难题，为量化投资决策提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Venture capital (VC) investment decisions face distinct challenges, such as multi-source heterogeneous data, non-stationary time series, and the demand for explainable predictions in high-stakes, low-data settings. To overcome these issues, we introduce \textbf{FinInvest-GTCN}, a Graph-Temporal-Causal Network that redefines the task from content recommendation to quantitative risk-return assessment. This architecture combines a relational graph encoder to capture the investment ecosystem's topology, a multi-scale temporal fusion module to handle long-term dependencies and non-stationarity, and a causal decision head that generates risk-adjusted predictions with interpretable causal attributions. A core innovation is the Meta-Causal Adaptation (MCA) strategy, which facilitates robust fine-tuning for new, data-scarce sectors by aligning updates with causally-plausible structures derived from meta-pretraining. Comprehensive experiments on proprietary VC datasets show that FinInvest-GTCN delivers state-of-the-art results, markedly lowering the primary Risk-Adjusted Mean Squared Error (RA-MSE) to 2.51 from a baseline of 3.05 and boosting the cumulative return of a simulated portfolio by 18.7\%. Ablation studies underscore the essential role of each component, while additional analyses confirm the model's stability, interpretability, and enhanced adaptability. This work pioneers a data-driven, explainable framework for investment decision support.

</details>


### [66] [EVLA: An Electro-Aware Multimodal Assistant for Physically-Grounded Driving Reasoning and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28938)
*Yuxin Liu,Zihan Chen,Haoyu Wang,Mingxuan Zhang,Ruijie Lin,Siyuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EVLA框架，将多模态场景理解与车辆电动力总成实时状态感知相结合，通过统一共状态编码器（UCSE）和电感知结构化推理链（ESRC），实现物理约束下的节能驾驶决策，在驾驶QA基准上显著优于现有VLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前面向驾驶辅助的视觉-语言模型将车辆动力学视为黑箱，缺乏对车辆实时电机械状态（如电机扭矩、电池SOC）的感知，导致决策缺乏物理基础。作者旨在填补这一空白，使驾驶决策具备车辆状态感知和能效优化的能力。

Method: 提出EVLA框架，包含两大创新：1）统一共状态编码器（UCSE），将视觉、文本和车辆状态输入融合到共享潜在表示中，并引入能效场建模空间能量成本；2）电感知结构化推理链（ESRC），用基于物理约束和优化目标的内部确定性推理过程替代外部思维链提示。整个框架以物理引导的联合损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在驾驶QA基准上，EVLA相比强微调的VLM基线在最终分数上提升+0.0871，准确率提升+5.6%。消融实验验证了各组件的必要性，效率分析表明EVLA比多阶段流水线推理速度提升36%。

Conclusion: 整合车辆状态感知与结构化物理推理对于开发下一代具有物理基础的驾驶助手至关重要，EVLA框架为实现这一目标提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) for driving assistants typically treat vehicle dynamics as a black box, resulting in decisions that lack awareness of the vehicle's real-time electro-mechanical state. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Electro-Visual-Language Assistant (EVLA) -- a novel framework that combines multi-modal scene understanding with real-time perception of the electrified powertrain state (e.g., motor torque, battery SOC). Our approach features two key innovations: first, a Unified Co-State Encoder (UCSE) that fuses visual, textual, and vehicle-state inputs into a shared latent representation, augmented with an Energy-Efficiency Field to model spatial energy costs; and second, an Electro-aware Structured Reasoning Chain (ESRC), which replaces external chain-of-thought prompting with an internal, deterministic reasoning process grounded in physical constraints and optimization objectives. Trained end-to-end with a physics-guided joint loss, EVLA learns to generate context-aware and energy-optimal driving decisions. Extensive evaluations on a driving QA benchmark demonstrate that EVLA substantially outperforms strong fine-tuned VLM baselines, improving the final score by +0.0871 and accuracy by +5.6\%. Ablation studies validate the necessity of each component, and efficiency analyses show that EVLA achieves 36\% faster inference than multi-stage pipelines. This work underscores that integrating vehicle-state awareness and structured physical reasoning is crucial for developing next-generation, physically-grounded driving assistants.

</details>


### [67] [A3M: Adaptive, Adversarial and Multi-Objective Learning for Strategic Bidding in Repeated Auctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28943)
*Junhan Li,Yuxin Zhang,Haoran Wang,Minghao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A3M框架，融合自适应深度强化学习、对手建模与多目标奖励设计，在重复多物品拍卖中实现更优的竞拍策略，相比基线方法将遗憾降低30-40%，并具备对抗非平稳对手的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重复多物品拍卖中的出价学习方法存在三个关键局限：(1)依赖刚性的"先探索后利用"策略，缺乏动态平衡能力；(2)假设对手是平稳的，无法应对策略漂移的对抗性环境；(3)仅以竞拍者自身效用为单一优化目标，忽略拍卖平台收入与公平性。这导致现有方法的适应性和战略鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出A3M框架，包含三大核心模块：(1)基于Actor-Critic的深度强化学习骨干网络，实现探索与利用的动态平衡；(2)对手建模模块，通过虚拟博弈（fictitious play）应对非平稳对手的策略变化；(3)复合奖励函数，联合优化竞拍者效用、拍卖商收入与公平性三个目标。实验在歧视性拍卖和统一价格拍卖两种机制下进行系统评估。

Result: 在标准设置下，A3M将最终遗憾降低30-40%；面对对手策略突变时保持鲁棒性能；随拍卖物品数量K的增长展现出良好的可扩展性；复合奖励函数支持可调节的多目标权衡。消融实验验证了三大核心模块各自的必要性。

Conclusion: A3M为复杂拍卖环境中的在线学习提供了一个强大且灵活的统一框架，其集成式设计（自适应DRL + 对手建模 + 多目标优化）在回报、鲁棒性和可扩展性上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Learning to bid in repeated multi-unit auctions with bandit feedback poses a fundamental challenge. Existing methods often rely on rigid explore-then-exploit schedules, assume stationary adversaries, and optimize solely for bidder utility, thereby limiting adaptability and strategic robustness. To address these limitations, we introduce the A3M framework, which integrates adaptive deep reinforcement learning (DRL), explicit adversarial reasoning, and principled multi-objective reward design for online auction strategy optimization. A3M employs an actor-critic DRL backbone to dynamically balance exploration and exploitation, an opponent model for fictitious play against non-stationary adversaries, and a composite reward function to jointly maximize utility, auctioneer revenue, and fairness. We provide the first comprehensive empirical evaluation of this integrated approach against established baselines in both discriminatory and uniform price auctions. Results show that A3M reduces final regret by 30--40\% in standard settings, maintains robust performance against adversarial strategy shifts, scales favorably with the number of units $K$, and enables tunable multi-objective trade-offs. An extensive ablation study confirms the necessity of each core component. Our work establishes A3M as a powerful and flexible framework for learning in complex auction environments.

</details>


### [68] [Beyond the Mean: Three-Axis Fidelity for Aligning LLM-Based Survey Simulators from Small Pilot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28963)
*Eun Cheol Choi,Youngrae Kim,Prabhu Pugalenthi,Hong-En Chen,Bo-Ruei Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了用大语言模型（LLM）模拟社会调查回答时的系统偏差问题，并提出以小型人类样本为基准，通过提示、校正和微调三类方法恢复统计特征，发现微调在不同子样本间保真度存在差异，可能威胁多元对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地被用于模拟社会调查回答，但其输出存在系统性偏差：边际分布偏移、响应方差校准不良、预测变量与结果变量间的关系被削弱。作者希望探究：能否利用少量人类样本引导 LLM 恢复更广泛人群的统计特征。

Method: 将统计恢复分解为三个维度——结构保真度（变量间关系）、边际保真度（单变量分布）和个体保真度（逐个回答准确性）。以 COVID-19 错误信息调查为案例，对三类方法进行基准测试：(1) 提示工程（prompting），(2) 后处理校正（rectification），(3) 在小规模试点样本上微调（fine-tuning）。

Result: 在小规模试点样本上进行微调，能够在多种保真度之间取得平衡；然而，不同子样本（如人口统计子群体）间的保真度水平存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 微调方法虽能较好平衡结构、边际和个体保真度，但其在各子群体上的不一致表现可能对"多元对齐"（pluralistic alignment）构成威胁，即 LLM 可能无法公平地反映不同群体的统计特征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to simulate social survey responses, yet their outputs exhibit systematic biases: marginal distributions are skewed, response variance is poorly calibrated, and predictor-outcome relationships are attenuated. We ask a simple question: given a small pilot sample of human responses, can an LLM recover the statistical characteristics of a broader population? We decompose recovery along three axes: structural fidelity, marginal fidelity, and individual fidelity. Using a COVID-19 misinformation survey as a case study, we benchmark three families of approaches: prompting, rectification, and fine-tuning. The findings suggest that fine-tuning on small pilot samples offers a balanced approach for achieving multiple forms of fidelity, but the levels of such fidelity can vary across subsamples, potentially threatening pluralistic alignment.

</details>


### [69] [Can LLMs Hire Fairly? Racial Bias in Resume Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28978)
*Zhenyu Gao,Wenxi Jiang,Yutong Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对14个主流大语言模型进行招聘歧视审计，发现2023年模型再现亲白人偏见，而2024年及之后发布的模型则呈现亲黑人或无显著差异的反转趋势，性别维度同理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在招聘场景中的算法偏见，检验不同代际模型是否存在种族和性别歧视，以及偏见方向是否随时间/模型更新而发生系统性变化。

Method: 采用Kline, Rose & Walters (2022)的配对简历方法，对14个主流LLM进行审计，每个模型生成24,024组成对岗位申请，观察不同种族的回调率差异。

Result: 唯一的2023年模型再现了劳动力市场实地实验中的亲白人回调差距（+2.12个百分点，1%水平显著）；而所有2024年及之后发布的模型要么无显著差距，要么呈现显著的亲黑人反转（最高达−3.01个百分点）；性别维度呈现相同模式。

Conclusion: 算法招聘偏见的方向在模型代际之间发生了显著反转，表明模型更新（可能源于价值对齐、去偏处理等）已系统性地改变了LLM的歧视行为模式。

Abstract: We audit fourteen mainstream large language models (LLMs) for hiring discrimination using the paired-resume methodology of Kline, Rose, and Walters (2022). The sole 2023-vintage model reproduces the pro-White callback gap documented in field experiments on labor market discrimination ($+2.12$ pp, significant at the 1\% level). Every model released in 2024 or after shows either a null gap or a significant pro-Black reversal (up to $-3.01$ pp). The same pattern holds on the gender axis. Based on 24,024 paired postings per model across 14 models, our results document a reversal in the direction of algorithmic hiring bias across model generations.

</details>


### [70] [Fine-Tuning General-Purpose Large Language Models for Agricultural Applications:A Reproducible Framework and Evaluation Protocol Based on Qwen3-8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28992)
*Zhaoyang Li,Ruijie Zhang,Jiaqi Liu,Zhaoji Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgriTune-R框架，一个可复现、可审计的农业大语言模型适配方案，强调数据治理、专家评估和安全控制，而非报告未经实际训练的模型性能声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在开放领域表现强大，但农业应用具有领域特异性、区域依赖性、时效性和安全关键性。缺乏数据治理、专家评估和证据约束会导致模型在作物病害、农药使用、施肥和政策解读等方面产生不可靠建议。因此需要一套结构化、可复现的农业LLM适配框架。

Method: 提出AgriTune-R框架，以公开可验证的Qwen3-8B为基础模型，整合以下组件：(1)农业数据治理；(2)指令构建；(3)LoRA/QLoRA参数高效微调；(4)检索增强生成(RAG)；(5)专家评估；(6)高风险问题的安全控制。框架明确分离协议设计与实证结论。

Result: 本文未报告任何未经实际训练运行和专家评估的模型性能声明，而是提供了：(1)结构化的农业LLM适配工作流；(2)覆盖农业知识问答、病虫害咨询、栽培管理和政策解释的评估协议；(3)结合事实性、安全性、证据一致性和不确定性表达的专家评审量规；(4)为未来实证研究提供可执行基线。

Conclusion: AgriTune-R为农业领域LLM适配提供了一个透明、可审计的方法论框架，强调在安全关键场景下必须将协议设计与实证结论明确分离，为后续的实证训练与评估研究奠定了可复现的基线。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong abilities in opendomain question answering, information extraction, and text generation. Agricultural applications, however, are domain-specific, region-dependent, time-sensitive, and safety-critical. Without data governance, expert evaluation, and evidence constraints, an agricultural assistant mayproduce unreliable advice on crop diseases, pesticide use, fertilization, or policy interpretation.To avoid presenting unverified simulated numbers as real experimental findings, this paper doesnot report any model-performance claims that have not been produced by an actual training runand expert evaluation. Instead, we propose AgriTune-R, a reproducible and auditable frameworkfor adapting general-purpose LLMs to agricultural tasks. The framework selects the publiclyverifiable Qwen3-8B model as the recommended base model and integrates agricultural datagovernance, instruction construction, LoRA/QLoRA parameter-efficient fine-tuning, retrievalaugmented generation, expert evaluation, and safety control for high-risk questions. The contributions are: (1) a structured workflow for agricultural LLM adaptation; (2) an evaluationprotocol for agricultural knowledge QA, pest and disease consultation, cultivation management,and policy explanation; (3) an expert-review rubric combining factuality, safety, evidence consistency, and uncertainty expression; and (4) a clear separation between protocol design andempirical conclusions, providing an executable baseline for future empirical studies.

</details>


### [71] [BERTomelo: Your Portuguese Encoder Best Friend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28999)
*Rennê Ruan Alves Oliveira,Gustavo Cordeiro Galvão Van Erven,Luís Paulo Faina Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BERTomelo 是一个基于 ModernBERT 架构、从零预训练的葡萄牙语单语编码器，提供 Base 和 Large 两个版本，具备 1024 token 上下文窗口和 FlashAttention 等硬件级优化，在 STS 和 NER 等下游任务上超越了以往的葡萄牙语模型及大规模多语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言模型虽覆盖广泛，但无法充分捕捉葡萄牙语的词汇和句法特性；而已有的葡萄牙语单语模型（如 BERTimbau、Albertina）未能跟上最新的架构突破，在可扩展性和效率上落后于英语基准。

Method: 采用 ModernBERT 架构，融入 FlashAttention 和交替注意力机制等硬件级优化，支持 1024 token 上下文窗口；使用包含 1.06 亿文档的大规模高质量葡萄牙语语料库 ClassiCC-PT 进行从零预训练，提供 Base 和 Large 两种规模。
