Motivation: 当前推理系统普遍采用多次采样（test-time scaling）来提升难题解答能力，但最终必须返回单一答案。覆盖率随采样数持续攀升，而选择准确率却停滞不前，二者之间的"可辨识性鸿沟"（identifiability gap）尚未被系统量化研究，导致实践中存在大量无效计算浪费。

Method: 作者通过分析多次采样中投票行为的收敛特性，定义了"模态天花板"（modal ceiling）和"相关性天花板"（correlation ceiling）两个概念，并提出了一个可由任意采样运行过程直接得出的统一度量——"有效样本数"（effective number of samples），用以确定何时应停止采样以避免无效计算。

Result: 研究发现：对于答案选择，投票结果在数十次采样内即已稳定（模态天花板）；对于基准评分，收敛更快（相关性天花板）。超过该临界点后，额外采样仅增加计算成本而毫无收益，甚至因模型对错误答案过度自信而降低回答质量。

Conclusion: 系统的瓶颈不在于生成正确答案的能力，而在于识别正确答案的能力。部署推理系统时应依据"有效样本数"设定采样上限，避免过度采样的陷阱。

Abstract: People overthink; language models over-sample, and the extra effort can talk both into a worse answer. Reasoning systems answer a hard question by sampling it many times (test-time scaling), and the more they draw, the more often a correct answer turns up somewhere, so coverage, the fraction of problems with at least one correct try, climbs and appears to be progress. But a deployed system must return one answer, and choosing it, not knowing which try is right, is selection; selection is capped, and past a point extra samples only make the model surer of a confident mistake, even as every draw adds cost. The gap between climbing coverage and stalled selection, the identifiability gap, is the answer a model can produce but not pick. So the real question is not whether to sample but how far, and the answer is: not far. For picking an answer, the vote has already settled within a few dozen draws, the modal ceiling; for scoring a benchmark, sooner still, the correlation ceiling. Beyond that, extra draws cost compute and add nothing, and can even make the answer worse. This paper turns the cutoff into a single number, the effective number of samples, that any sampling run already reveals. The bottleneck is recognizing a right answer, not generating one.

</details>


### [106] [Closed-Form Steepest Descent Direction toward Flat Minima: Reducing Upper Bounds on the Loss Hessian Eigenspectrum in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28662)
*Yuto Omae,Kazuki Sakai,Yohei Kakimoto,Makoto Sasaki,Yusuke Sakai,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对平坦性假设，推导了交叉熵损失Hessian矩阵最大特征值的Wolkowicz-Styan上界的梯度闭式解，并据此提出HSR正则化方法，通过沿该上界最陡下降方向更新参数，使网络收敛到平坦极小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦性假设认为损失景观的平坦度与泛化能力相关，但现有方法多侧重算法设计，缺乏对数据分布和网络参数如何结构化地指向平坦极小值方向的理论理解。直接解析刻画这些方向在数学上难以处理。

Method: 基于三层神经网络交叉熵损失Hessian的Wolkowicz-Styan上界（此前已有工作推导该可微上界但未求其梯度），作者首次解析推导出该上界的梯度闭式解，并据此设计Hessian Spectral Range (HSR) 正则化，沿WS上界的最陡下降方向更新参数，从而缩小Hessian特征值谱范围。

Result: 实验表明，HSR正则化能有效缩小Hessian特征值谱，避免尖锐极小值点和鞍点，促使网络收敛到平坦极小值。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个无需数值近似、以闭式梯度引导收敛至平坦极小值的研究，尽管目前仅适用于交叉熵损失和三层架构，但其梯度理论分析对神经网络的进一步发展具有贡献潜力。

Abstract: The flatness hypothesis suggests that flatness of the loss landscape, as measured by the eigenvalues of the loss Hessian, correlates with better neural network generalization. While various algorithms reduce these eigenvalues, most focus on procedural design, leaving it unclear how data distributions and NN parameters structurally determine directions toward flat minima. Characterizing these directions analytically is generally intractable. To overcome this mathematical difficulty, recent studies derived the Wolkowicz-Styan (WS) upper bound on the maximum eigenvalue of the cross-entropy loss Hessian in three-layer NNs. Although this upper bound is differentiable, its gradient was not derived. Therefore, we analytically derive the gradient of the WS upper bound to characterize directions leading to flat minima. Based on this, we propose Hessian Spectral Range (HSR) Regularization, which updates parameters along the steepest descent direction of the WS bound. Experiments demonstrate that HSR Regularization narrows the Hessian eigenvalue spectrum, avoids sharp minima and saddle points, and promotes convergence to flat minima. Although the applicability of this method is currently limited to cross-entropy loss and three-layer architectures, to the best of the authors' knowledge, this is the first study to report a closed-form gradient that promotes convergence to flat minima without numerical approximations. Therefore, the theoretical analysis of this gradient is expected to contribute to the further development of NNs.

</details>


### [107] [Entropy Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Zero-Sum Stochastic Differential Games in a Regime-Switching Jump-Diffusion Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28669)
*Congde Hu,Zhuo Jin,Danping Li,Lin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统随机微分博弈中参数误设和突发结构变化问题，本文提出一种分布控制方法，将最优策略刻画为动作上的概率分布，并在状态切换跳扩散过程中建立熵正则化零和随机微分博弈的强化学习框架，推导HJBI方程，给出线性二次情形的半解析解，并开发Actor-Critic算法处理一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDG框架难以应对参数误设（parameter misspecification）和环境的突然结构性变化（sudden structural environmental changes）。作者旨在通过引入分布控制（distributional control）和熵正则化，构建一个能同时处理连续状态、离散状态切换（regime-switching）和跳扩散（jump-diffusion）动态的零和博弈强化学习框架，以提高策略鲁棒性和适应性。

Method: 首先将最优策略重新定义为以连续状态、离散状态和参数为条件的动作概率分布，形成熵正则化零和随机微分博弈（ERRL-ZSSDGs）的强化学习框架。利用动态规划原理（DPP）推导耦合的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs（HJBI）方程组，将均衡策略表示为值函数梯度的函数。针对线性二次（LQ）问题，通过求解耦合常微分方程组获得值函数与均衡策略的半解析解。对于一般情形，提出跨不同状态的Actor-Critic策略改进算法来逼近值函数和均衡策略。

Result: 线性二次情形下获得了半解析解；一般情形下通过Actor-Critic算法实现值函数与策略的近似。投资博弈应用中的数值实验展示了温度参数（temperature parameter）和状态切换（regime transitions）对最优策略和值函数的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵正则化分布控制方法能够有效处理状态切换跳扩散环境下的零和随机微分博弈，兼具鲁棒性和计算可行性。温度参数和状态切换机制是影响均衡行为的关键因素，该框架为复杂环境下的博弈决策提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: To address parameter misspecification and sudden structural environmental changes in conventional stochastic differential game (SDG) frameworks, this paper introduces a distributional control approach that characterizes optimal strategies as probability distributions over actions, conditioned on the continuous state, the discrete regime state, and parameters. This forms a reinforcement learning framework for entropy-regularized zero-sum stochastic differential games (ERRL-ZSSDGs) in a regime-switching jump-diffusion process. Using the dynamic programming principle (DPP), we derive the associated coupled systems of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) equations, from which equilibrium strategies are expressed via gradients of the value function. For linear-quadratic problems, semi-analytical solutions for both value function and equilibrium strategies are obtained by solving a system of coupled ordinary differential equations (ODEs). In more general settings, an Actor-Critic policy improvement algorithm is developed to approximate the value functions and equilibrium policies across different regimes. The method is applied to an investment game, and numerical examples illustrate the effect of the temperature parameter and regime transitions on optimal policies and values.

</details>


### [108] [Entropy-Regularized Reinforcement Learning for Linear-Quadratic Stackelberg Differential Games in Regime-Switching Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28671)
*Congde Hu,Danping Li,Lin Xu,Wenying Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种熵正则化强化学习方法，用于求解带马尔可夫机制切换的线性二次Stackelberg微分博弈，通过探索性弱耦合HJBI方程和神经网络近似，有效避免次优均衡陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SDG求解依赖动态规划和HJBI方法，在高维系统中计算复杂且易陷入次优均衡，需要一种能主动探索并规避次优解的层次决策框架。

Method: 在连续时间扩散框架下，推导熵正则化的探索性弱耦合HJBI方程；利用神经网络近似机制依赖的值函数并求解高维PDE；提出新型采样技术提升计算可处理性。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提框架相比传统方法能有效逃离次优陷阱，通过探索性策略获得鲁棒解，验证了熵正则化与神经网络近似的关键作用。

Conclusion: 熵正则化与神经网络近似相结合，为马尔可夫机制切换环境下的层次决策问题提供了高效鲁棒的求解路径，尤其擅长应对突发环境变化和规避次优均衡。

Abstract: Stackelberg differential games (SDGs) provide a powerful framework for hierarchical decision-making in stochastic and continuous-time environments, yet their solution remains computationally challenging due to the complexity of traditional dynamic programming and Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs (HJBI) methods, especially in high-dimensional systems. This paper proposes an entropy-regularized reinforcement learning (ERRL) approach for linear-quadratic SDGs (LQ-SDGs) within a continuous-time diffusion framework governed by Markovian regime switching. The key innovation lies in deriving exploratory weakly-coupled HJBI equations with entropy regularization, which promotes stochastic policies that actively avoid suboptimal equilibria -- a limitation of classical SDG methods. Neural networks are integrated to approximate regime-dependent value functions and solve high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs) efficiently, while a novel sampling technique enhances computational tractability. Numerical results demonstrate the effectiveness of the framework compared to conventional approaches, particularly in escaping suboptimal traps through exploratory policies. The study highlights the critical role of entropy regularization and neural network approximations in achieving robust solutions for hierarchical decision-making problems under abrupt environmental shifts.

</details>


### [109] [Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28674)
*Michael Cardei,Jose M Munoz,Oscar Barrera,Shreyas K Chandrahas,Partha Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CTDF提出一种在采样阶段嵌入可行性算子的约束型表格扩散模型，无需额外训练即可在金融数据生成中实现硬约束的零违反，同时提升稀缺数据的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域的生成模型需要在生成逼真数据的同时满足严格的监管和经济目标约束，而标准表格扩散模型无法提供这种硬约束保证。

Method: 将无需训练的可行性算子（feasibility operator）集成到混合类型表格扩散模型的逆向扩散采样循环中，在采样时强制执行硬约束，支持仿真、合规性和外推等应用场景。

Result: 在大规模金融数据集上的实验表明，约束违反率为零，且在稀缺数据场景下的数据效用得到显著提升。

Conclusion: CTDF为生成可信且合规的合成金融数据提供了稳健方法，为金融领域严格的生成建模与分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Generative models in finance face the dual challenge of producing realistic data while satisfying strict regulatory and economic objectives, a requirement that standard tabular diffusion models cannot provide. To address this difficulty, we introduce Constrained Tabular Diffusion for Finance (CTDF), a novel integration of sampling-time feasibility operations with mixed-type tabular diffusion in financial applications. By incorporating a training-free feasibility operator into the reverse-diffusion sampling loop, CTDF enforces hard constraints for applications such as simulation, legal compliance, and extrapolation. Extensive experiments on large-scale financial datasets demonstrate zero constraint violations and improvement in scarce data utility. CTDF establishes a robust method for generating trustworthy and compliant synthetic data, opening new avenues for rigorous generative modeling and analysis in the financial domain.

</details>


### [110] [When Prices Double in a Week: Forecasting of Agricultural Volatility in Import-Isolated Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29248)
*Ranuga Weerasekara,Heshan Nethmina,Manuja Ranathunga,Vinma Wettasinghe,Dinithi Navodya,Subavarshana Arumugam,Nirasha Munasinghe,Nisansa de Silva,Sandareka Wickramanayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个融合供应链特征的机器学习框架，利用梯度提升集成模型（XGBoost和LightGBM）预测斯里兰卡蔬菜价格波动，统一模型在2024年恶性通胀期的未见数据上仍保持85.96%的预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯里兰卡蔬菜市场因进口隔离导致价格高度波动，供应中断会迅速推高价格。现有研究仅局限于单一市场的ARIMA和GARCH模型，缺乏供应链特征、季节性分割和跨机制验证，因此需要一种更精准的预测方法。

Method: 构建整合零售价、产地价、产地对齐天气变量、柴油成本和汇率的2013-2019年数据集（覆盖12种蔬菜、14个市场中心）。以Maha（10-4月）和Yala（5-9月）两个耕作季为基础，使用Optuna优化XGBoost和LightGBM梯度提升集成模型，对比统一配置与季节特定配置的预测性能。

Result: 季节特定模型改善了季内拟合：Yala专用模型R²最高达0.9420（95% CI [0.690, 1.000]）。统一模型整体预测准确率达90.84%（95% CI [88.34%, 91.52%]），R²为0.9281（95% CI [0.760, 1.000]），且在未经再训练的2024年恶性通胀期数据上保持85.96%准确率，成功追踪了主要价格飙升。

Conclusion: 在进口受限市场中，当模型捕捉供应链动态时，农产品价格波动具有显著可预测性，可为农民、贸易商和政策制定者提供早期预警和决策支持。该研究突破了斯里兰卡蔬菜价格预测仅使用ARIMA/GARCH的局限，首次引入供应链特征、季节性分割和跨机制验证。

Abstract: Vegetable prices in Sri Lanka are highly volatile because the market is largely import-isolated, so supply disruptions quickly drive prices up. This study develops a machine learning framework to forecast such volatility by incorporating supply-chain-aware features and explicitly modelling the country's two cultivation seasons, Maha (October-April) and Yala (May-September). An integrated dataset was constructed by combining retail and farmer-gate prices with origin-aligned weather variables, diesel costs, and exchange rates across 12 vegetable varieties and 14 market centres from 2013 to 2019. A gradient-boosted ensemble model (XGBoost and LightGBM) was trained and optimised using Optuna, and unified and season-specific configurations were compared. Results show that season-specific models improve within-season fit, with the Yala-specific model achieving the highest R2 of 0.9420 (95% CI [0.690, 1.000]), while the unified model delivers the best overall predictive accuracy of 90.84% (95% CI [88.34%, 91.52%]) and an R2 of 0.9281 (95% CI [0.760, 1.000]). Notably, the unified model maintains 85.96% accuracy on a completely unseen 2024 hyperinflationary period without retraining, successfully tracking major price surges. These findings suggest that agricultural price movements in import-constrained markets are meaningfully predictable when models capture supply-chain dynamics, offering practical value for early warning and decision making by farmers, traders, and policymakers. Existing studies on Sri Lankan vegetable prices are confined to Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA) and Generalized Autoregressive Conditional Heteroskedasticity (GARCH) applied to single markets, with no supply-chain features, seasonal segmentation, or cross-regime validation.

</details>


### [111] [A Path-Space Formulation of Prediction in World Models: From a Single Action to Prediction, Planning, and Irreversibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28751)
*Gunn Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AI世界模型中预测的路径空间表述，将世界模型视为对未来轨迹的概率测度，而非单步条件分布序列。在局部马尔可夫条件下，路径测度呈Onsager-Machlup形式。作者将潜动力学分解为可逆与不可逆分量，发现注意力不对称性与数据的不可逆性成正比，对称化注意力会抑制熵产生并损害不可逆动力学的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI世界模型将预测框定为状态序列上的单步条件分布，忽视了全局路径视角。作者希望从统计物理（尤其是Onsager-Machlup路径积分理论）出发，重新形式化预测、规划与不确定性，揭示不可逆性作为一种计算资源的角色。

Method: （1）将世界模型的潜动力学投影为路径空间上的概率测度，在局部马尔可夫条件下导出Onsager-Machlup作用泛函；（2）将动力学分解为可逆（梯度型）与不可逆（旋转型）分量；（3）从模型rollout中引入熵产生操作度量；（4）在小规模注意力模型中，通过对称化学习到的注意力权重，操控不可逆性，观察其对长期预测的影响。

Result: （1）注意力不对称性在训练过程中被习得，且与数据中的不可逆性程度成正比；（2）对称化已学习的注意力会抑制熵产生；（3）这种对称化选择性地损害不可逆动力学的长期预测能力，但保留松弛型（可逆）预测能力。

Conclusion: 不可逆性（时间反演不对称性）可能是预测性世界模型的一种计算资源；预测的根本对象应是未来路径的分布，而不仅仅是状态的分布。路径空间视角为统一预测、规划与不确定性分析提供了数学框架。

Abstract: We propose a path-space formulation of prediction in AI world models. Rather than sequences of one-step conditional distributions, we argue that a world model implicitly defines a probability measure over future trajectories. In the local regime where latent dynamics admit an effective Markovian description, this path measure takes the Onsager-Machlup form. Within this framework, prediction (most probable trajectory), planning (constrained optimization), and uncertainty (fluctuations) emerge as operations on a single action functional. We decompose the latent dynamics into reversible and irreversible components and introduce operational measures of entropy production from model rollouts. In controlled small-scale attention-based models, we find that attention asymmetry is acquired during training in proportion to the irreversibility of the data. Symmetrizing the learned attention suppresses entropy production and selectively degrades long-horizon prediction of irreversible dynamics while preserving relaxational prediction. These results suggest that irreversibility may serve as a computational resource for predictive world models. More generally, the fundamental predictive object is a distribution over future paths rather than states.

</details>


### [112] [Hierarchical Decision Making with Structured Policies: A Principled Design via Inverse Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28764)
*Yuexuan Wang,Jingyuan Zhou,Kaidi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个将上层目标抽象与下层结构化决策系统整合的分层框架，通过逆优化方法从专家演示中学习下层优化问题的结构，确保下层目标与长期任务目标对齐，在资源分配和碰撞避免任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层决策框架存在两方面不足：基于强化学习的方法难以保证严格的约束满足，而基于最优控制的方法通常依赖短视且计算代价高昂的公式。分层RL-OC架构虽有前景，但下层优化问题的公式化设计尚未被充分探索，常依赖启发式或短视目标。

Method: 提出一个原则性框架，系统整合上层目标抽象与下层结构化决策。核心创新是采用逆优化方法从专家演示中推断下层优化问题的结构（目标函数与约束），使得下层策略的目标与整体长期任务目标保持一致。

Result: 在两个不同的决策任务上进行了验证：基于网络的资源分配和连续碰撞避免。实验结果表明，该方法在效率和决策质量上一致优于基于端到端RL、学习增强最优控制以及现有分层RL方法的强基线。

Conclusion: 通过逆优化将专家知识结构性地注入下层优化问题，是弥合分层RL-OC架构中上下层目标不一致问题的有效途径。该框架为分层决策提供了一种更原则性的下层问题设计范式，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Hierarchical decision-making frameworks are pivotal for addressing complex control tasks, enabling agents to decompose intricate problems into manageable subgoals. Despite their promise, existing hierarchical policies face critical limitations: (i) reinforcement learning (RL)-based methods struggle to guarantee strict constraint satisfaction, and (ii) optimal control (OC)-based approaches often rely on myopic and computationally prohibitive formulations. To reconcile these trade-offs, hierarchical RL-OC architectures have emerged as a promising paradigm. However, the formulation of the lower-level optimization within these frameworks remains underexplored, often relying on heuristic or myopic objectives. In this work, we propose a principled framework that systematically integrates upper-level goal abstraction with structured lower-level decision making. We adopt an inverse optimization approach to inform the structure of the lower-level problem from expert demonstrations, ensuring that the objective of the lower-level policy remains aligned with the overall long-term task goal. To validate the approach, our framework is evaluated on distinct decision making tasks: network-based resource allocation and continuous collision avoidance. Empirical results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms strong baselines based on end-to-end RL, learning-augmented optimal control, and existing hierarchical RL approaches in both efficiency and decision quality.

</details>


### [113] [Few-Step Boltzmann Generators via Scalable Likelihood Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29110)
*RuiKang OuYang,Hanlin Yu,Xinyue Ai,Yutong He,Nicholas M. Boffi,Pradeep Ravikumar,Jose Miguel Hernandez-Lobato,Max Simchowitz,Benjamin Kurt Miller,Omar Chehab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCALLOP 提出了一种无 Hutchinson 估计器、可向量化的可扩展似然蒸馏目标，用于训练流图模型，在分子科学与图像数据集上显著降低了训练方差和时间，同时保持竞争力并实现高达 10 倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于流的生成模型在高效采样上进展显著，但在模型似然估计方面仍存在瓶颈：现有方法要么依赖限制性架构以进行精确计算，要么使用 Hutchinson 迹估计器等随机近似方法，引入高方差。亟需一种可扩展、低方差的似然蒸馏方法。

Method: SCALLOP 基于 F2D2（likelihood flow map）模型框架，提出一种 Hutchinson-free 的似然蒸馏目标函数，该目标具有可向量化特性，可替代训练中使用的 Hutchinson 迹估计器，从而降低梯度方差并提升可扩展性。

Result: 在分子科学（Boltzmann 生成器）和图像数据集上，SCALLOP 相比 F2D2 显著降低了训练方差和训练时间，性能持续提升；与最先进基线相比具有竞争力，且推理速度比最快基线快达 10 倍。

Conclusion: SCALLOP 通过 Hutchinson-free 似然蒸馏，有效解决了流图模型训练中高方差和可扩展性不足的问题，在保证似然估计质量的同时大幅提升训练与推理效率，为生成建模在科学计算与图像生成领域的应用提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Recent progress in flow-based generative modeling has led to models that output high-quality samples while using only a small number of function evaluations. However, at present, there is a lack of similar advances in estimating the model likelihood. In particular, most existing methods either rely on restrictive architectures that enable exact calculations, or use stochastic approximations such as Hutchinson's trace estimator that introduce substantial variance. In this work, we introduce SCAlable LikeLihood distillation of flOw maPs (SCALLOP). SCALLOP builds on the recently proposed F2D2, a likelihood flow map model that can generate samples and their densities in a small number of function evaluations. While F2D2 uses Hutchinson's estimator during training, we introduce an alternative and more scalable likelihood distillation objective that is Hutchinson-free and admits a vectorized formulation. Empirically, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SCALLOP as a Boltzmann generator in molecular science, and further validate its benefit on image datasets. SCALLOP significantly reduces both training variance and training time while consistently improving performance compared to F2D2, and is competitive with the state-of-the-art while achieving up to 10x inference speedup over the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [114] [Generative Learning as a Tool to Improve Perception of Emotional Body Motion Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28769)
*Huakun Liu,Miao Cheng,Xin Wei,Felix Dollack,Victor Schneider,Hideaki Uchiyama,Chia-huei Tseng,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Monica Perusquia-Hernandez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究基于Transformer的生成模型是否能从日本演员的动作捕捉数据中隐式学习情绪化身体动作，并在13种离散情绪标签条件下生成富有表现力的动作。通过机器分类器和人类感知研究分别评估生成动作的可识别性（准确率22.80%和24.91%），并展示了在情绪识别数据增强、情绪动作模式提取及情绪强度平滑过渡三个任务中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情绪身体动作表达是非语言交流的核心要素，但在VR化身和社交机器人中有效传达情绪仍面临挑战。情绪线索的微妙性、个体差异和文化差异使得精确生成情绪表达动作十分困难。现有研究缺乏对生成模型是否能直接从文化情境化的动捕数据中隐式学习情绪动作的探索，即不依赖显式的情绪-动作映射指导。

Method: 使用49名日本演员的情绪表演动捕数据集，训练一个基于Transformer的生成模型，条件为13种离散情绪标签。评估分为两个维度：(1) 训练LSTM分类器评估机器观察者对生成动作的识别能力；(2) 招募日本评分者进行人类感知研究，评估动作与人类情感解读的一致性。此外，还验证了生成模型在三个实际任务中的效用：增强情绪识别模型、提取代表性的情绪特异性动作模式、合成情绪强度之间的平滑过渡。

Result: LSTM分类器对生成动作的识别准确率为22.80%（随机基线约7.69%）；日本评分者的人类感知研究识别准确率为24.91%，两者均显著高于随机水平。三个应用任务均展示了生成建模在情感计算中的实际价值。

Conclusion: 隐式的、数据驱动的生成建模在增强情感计算应用和加深对情绪表达理解方面具有显著潜力。即使没有显式的情绪-动作映射指导，Transformer生成模型也能从文化情境化的动捕数据中学习到可被机器和人类识别的情绪身体动作表达。

Abstract: Emotional body motion expressions are an essential element of non-verbal communication. Effectively conveying these expressions through technology is of utmost importance, for example, with virtual reality avatars and in social robotics. Recent advances in generative models have opened new opportunities for advancing research on emotional body motion learning. However, generating accurate emotional expression representations is challenging, given the subtlety of emotional cues, individual variability, and cultural differences. We investigate whether a generative model can implicitly learn emotional body motions directly from culturally grounded motion-capture data, without explicit emotion-motion guidance. Using a dataset of emotional performances by 49 Japanese actors, we trained a Transformer-based generative model to generate expressive motions conditioned on 13 discrete emotion labels. We evaluate the generated motions from two perspectives: (1) an LSTM-based classifier to assess recognizability by machine observers, achieving a recognition accuracy of 22.80%, and (2) a human perception study with Japanese raters to assess alignment with human affective interpretations, yielding a recognition accuracy of 24.91%. Beyond these, we evaluate the utility of generative modeling for three practical tasks: augmenting emotion recognition models, extracting representative emotion-specific motion patterns, and synthesizing smooth transitions between emotion intensities. Our findings highlight the potential of implicit, data-driven generative modeling to enhance affective computing applications and our understanding of emotion expressions.

</details>


### [115] [On design-unbiased algorithmic Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28795)
*Li-Chun Zhang,Siu-Ming Tam,Luis Sanguiao-Sande,Wesley Yung,Anders Holmberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究机器学习算法（如kNN、随机森林）在有限总体中实现无偏预测/分类的条件，基于已知抽样设计而非假设模型，探讨训练数据抽样方式、算法调参及样本外表现的无偏评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习算法通常以最小化MSE或F-score等预测性能为目标，但这不能直接保证无偏性。在官方统计等许多场景中，无偏性至关重要。现有ML方法缺乏基于抽样设计的无偏性理论框架。

Method: 基于已知概率抽样设计（而非假设分布或模型）进行推断，系统研究三个条件：(1) 训练数据如何从有限总体中抽样；(2) 如何调优训练好的预测算法以实现无偏预测/分类；(3) 如何无偏地评估样本外预测/分类的性能。

Result: （摘要中未给出具体实验结果，侧重于理论条件的阐述。）

Conclusion: 表明在已知抽样设计下，无需依赖真实数据模型的存在与知识，亦可使ML算法在有限总体中实现无偏预测与分类，并给出无偏评估样本外表现的方法。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, such as k-Nearest Neighbours (kNN) or random forest, eschew the ideal of true data models in favour of predictive performance. However, minimising the MSE or F-score cannot lead to unbiasedness directly, which is important in many situations such as official statistics. We study the conditions of algorithmic ML, other than the existence and knowledge of true data models, which lead to unbiased prediction or classification for a given finite population, including how the training data may be sampled from the population, how a trained prediction algorithm can be tuned to achieve unbiased prediction or classification for that population, and how the performance of out-of-sample prediction or classification can be assessed unbiasedly. The inference is based on the known probability design of samples and training sets, rather than any assumed distributions or models.

</details>


### [116] [Dead-Direction Conditioners: Gauge-Equivariant Preconditioning for Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29176)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDC（Dead-Direction Conditioner），一种将基础优化器提升为规范群等变的优化方法，通过在G-不变度规的轨道分解中条件化优化器状态，使轨迹保持在商空间上的预条件梯度流，从而防止Adam预条件子沿对称轨道漂移导致的优化偏离和过拟合崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络的损失函数对参数的连续对称性（logit平移、ReLU缩放、LayerNorm尺度、注意力旋转）具有不变性，但Adam的逐坐标预条件子会沿对称轨道漂移，将轨迹拉离商空间，模糊奇异学习率。需设计一种严格保持群等变的优化器，使优化轨迹保持在商流形上。

Method: 构建DDC框架：(1)在G-不变度规下对参数空间进行轨道分解，将优化器状态条件化到商空间Θ̄=Θ/G；(2)嵌入四种架构规范：交叉熵平移、ReLU/SwiGLU缩放、LayerNorm/RMSNorm尺度、与RoPE匹配的逐头O(d_head)注意力旋转；(3)在Adam基础上证明精确等变性；(4)通过规范等变正交化器与Muon基础优化器组合，得到DDCMuon。

Result: 在语言模型上的实验显示：DDCAdam抵抗AdamW的过训练崩溃，验证-训练损失差距仅0.67（AdamW为5.88）；在65个逐层可观测单元中的32个可读取死方向率，而AdamW仅7个。视觉Transformer从头训练，DDCAdam验证损失更低（1.71 vs 2.12），同时压缩了空闲前馈容量。DDCMuon在深度24的11个种子中grok了10个，而普通Muon完全无法达到。

Conclusion: 将网络规范对称性内建到优化器中，不仅锐化了优化器找到的极小值，还将极小值的几何结构转化为轨迹可测量的信息，为解决过训练崩溃和提升泛化性能提供了新的原理性路径。

Abstract: A deep network's loss is invariant to continuous symmetries of its parameters: the logit shift, the ReLU rescaling, the LayerNorm scale, the per-head attention rotation. Adam's per-coordinate preconditioner drifts along each symmetry orbit, which pulls the trajectory off the symmetry quotient where the optimization lives and blurs the singular-learning rate the quotient makes readable. We build DDC, a Dead-Direction Conditioner that lifts a base optimizer into a $G$-equivariant one: it conditions the optimizer's state in the orbit decomposition of a $G$-invariant metric, so the trajectory stays a preconditioned gradient flow on the quotient $\barΘ= Θ/G$. The construction carries four architectural gauges (cross-entropy shift, ReLU and SwiGLU rescaling, LayerNorm and RMSNorm scale, and a per-head $O(d_{\rm head})$ attention rotation matched to RoPE), proves exactly equivariant on an Adam base, and composes with a Muon base through a gauge-equivariant orthogonaliser. Respecting the symmetry changes both the minimum the optimizer reaches and what it leaves measurable there. On a language model trained past the point of fit, DDCAdam resists the over-training collapse AdamW falls into, holding a validation-train loss gap of 0.67 against 5.88, and reads the dead-direction rate in 32 of 65 layer-by-observable cells where AdamW reads it in 7. A vision transformer trained from scratch reaches lower validation loss (1.71 against 2.12) while compressing spare feed-forward capacity a matched AdamW leaves intact. On a Muon base, where the rotation gauge composes exactly, DDCMuon groks ten of eleven seeds at depth 24 that a plain Muon never reaches. Built into the optimizer, a network's gauge symmetry sharpens the minimum it finds and turns that minimum's geometry into something the trajectory can measure.

</details>


### [117] [HARD-KV: Head-Adaptive Regularization for Decoding-time KV Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28831)
*Yuxuan Yang,Feiyang Ren,Bowen Zeng,Dalin Zhang,Jinpeng Chen,Gang Chen,Huan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARD-KV 提出了一个统一框架，通过 Cascade Cache 层级结构和 Logits Calibration 机制，解决了动态压缩算法与静态推理引擎之间的"静-动"失配问题，在长上下文 LLM 推理中实现了高达 2 倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文 LLM 推理面临核心矛盾：以 Top-p 核采样为代表的头自适应压缩算法能够通过动态调整内存预算来获得更高的精度，但现代推理引擎（如 vLLM）需要刚性的静态内存模式才能高效利用 CUDA Graphs 和 PagedAttention。现有方案无法同时兼顾动态选择带来的精度优势和静态系统对性能的要求。

Method: 提出 HARD-KV 统一框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) Cascade Cache 层级结构，将 token 生命周期管理划分为 dense、sparse 和 condensed 三个层级；(2) Logits Calibration 机制，将异构的注意力头重要性度量统一归一化到同一个概率空间，实现跨头一致的 Top-p 预算分配；(3) 系统级索引重写方案，将碎片化的动态索引转换为连续物理布局，以兼容高性能推理引擎。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试（AIME、U-Math）上的大量实验表明，HARD-KV 在 10k+ token 场景下，相比静态基线实现了高达 2 倍的吞吐量提升，同时保持了高保真的生成质量。

Conclusion: HARD-KV 成功弥合了动态 KV 缓存压缩与静态推理引擎系统约束之间的鸿沟，为长上下文 LLM 的高效部署提供了兼具精度与性能的实用方案。

Abstract: Long-context LLM inference faces a fundamental conflict: head-adaptive compression algorithms (e.g., Top-$p$ nucleus sampling) offer superior accuracy by dynamically fluctuating memory budgets, yet modern inference engines (e.g., vLLM) demand rigid, static memory patterns to leverage CUDA Graphs and PagedAttention. We resolve this ``Static-Dynamic'' mismatch with HARD-KV, a unified framework that that bridges dynamic selection with rigid system constraints. HARD-KV introduces a Cascade Cache hierarchy, managing the token lifecycle across dense, sparse, and condensed tiers. Crucially, we propose a Logits Calibration mechanism that normalizes diverse importance metrics into a unified probability space, enabling consistent Top-$p$ budgeting across heterogeneous heads. To bridge the efficiency gap, we offer a system-level solution, which rewrites fragmented, dynamic indices into contiguous physical layouts compatible with high-performance inference engine. Extensive experiments on math-reasoning benchmarks (AIME, U-Math) verify that HARD-KV achieves up to 2$\times$ throughput improvement over static baselines while maintaining high-fidelity generation in 10k+ token scenarios. Code is available at https://github.com/SuDIS-ZJU/HARDInfer.

</details>


### [118] [Bayesian Best-Arm Identification with Abstention: A Polynomial-to-Exponential Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29203)
*Yuqi Huang,Yunlong Hou,Vincent Y. F. Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了带弃权机制的贝叶斯固定预算最佳臂识别问题。核心发现是：引入任意小的弃权预算会导致错误概率从多项式衰减转变为指数衰减，即发生相变。论文给出了高斯先验下最优错误指数的精确匹配上下界 $\exp(-\frac{\alpha^{2}T}{8\kappa_{\nu}^{2}})$，并提出了达到该最优指数的自适应算法PGWS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在序贯决策中，学习者往往需要在有限预算下识别最佳选项，但有时可能面临统计上难以区分的困难实例。传统固定预算最佳臂识别强制要求做出推荐，导致不可避免的错误。本文旨在研究允许"弃权"（abstention）这一机制如何改变错误概率的衰减速率，从而在困难实例上规避错误。

Method: 在贝叶斯框架下建模固定预算最佳臂识别问题，引入弃权预算α。通过信息论方法推导错误概率的下界，利用大偏差理论和后验分布分析建立匹配的上下界。提出PGWS（Posterior-based Gap-weighted Sampling）自适应算法，通过对后验分布中top-two差距的加权采样策略，将弃权预算集中用于统计上模糊的实例。进一步将贝叶斯结果与频率学派设定进行对比分析。

Result: 1. 弃权引发相变：无弃权时错误概率随采样预算T多项式衰减；引入任意正弃权预算α后变为指数衰减。2. 在T→∞后α↓0的渐近区域，最优错误指数为$\exp(-\frac{\alpha^{2}T}{8\kappa_{\nu}^{2}})$，其中$\kappa_{\nu}$是先验分布在top-two差距为零处的密度，反映了近平局实例的主导作用。3. PGWS算法在上下界上达到最优匹配。4. 多项式到指数的提升是贝叶斯独有的现象，在频率学派设定中弃权仅影响低阶指数项。5. 结果可推广至非高斯模型。

Conclusion: 弃权机制在贝叶斯固定预算最佳臂识别中引发了从多项式到指数衰减的相变，从根本上改变了错误概率的行为。困难参数$\kappa_{\nu}$揭示了近乎平局的实例是根本瓶颈。PGWS算法通过智能分配弃权预算实现了信息论最优。该发现对设计具有风险控制的序贯决策系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study the Bayesian fixed-budget best-arm identification problem in which a learner can abstain from making a terminal recommendation. Subject to an abstention budget $α$, we analyze the probability of undetected error--the risk of recommending a suboptimal arm without abstaining. Our central finding is that abstention induces a phase transition: without abstention, the error probability decays polynomially in the sampling budget $T$; in contrast, introducing any small positive abstention budget shifts this to an exponential decay. For Gaussian priors and rewards, in the regime $T\to\infty$ followed by $α\downarrow0$, we establish exact matching information-theoretic lower bounds and algorithmic upper bounds on the optimal error exponent, which takes the form $\exp(-\frac{α^{2}T}{8κ_ν^{2}})$. The hardness parameter $κ_ν$ represents the prior density of the top-two gap at zero, highlighting that nearly tied instances drive the fundamental error. We introduce an adaptive algorithm, PGWS, that successfully achieves this optimal exponent by expending its abstention budget on statistically ambiguous instances. We further demonstrate that this polynomial-to-exponential improvement is exclusively a Bayesian phenomenon--in the frequentist setting, abstention only affects lower-order exponent terms. We also extend our results beyond the Gaussian model.

</details>


### [119] [Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition for Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28833)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将量子核估计中的非均匀采样分配策略从分类扩展到高斯过程回归，推导了三类闭合形式的成对灵敏度指标，并设计了带均匀覆盖下限的Neyman最优分配规则。在多个基准测试中，该方法在中低采样预算下实现了10–21%的测试RMSE改善，并成功迁移到真实量子核及多种下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明，在量子核分类中按下游任务灵敏度非均匀分配采样预算可降低达到目标精度所需的测量次数。然而，高斯过程回归的下游量（全谱后验方差、对数行列式、边际似然）与核误差的耦合远比分类的符号输出更紧密，亟需将非均匀分配策略扩展至回归场景。

Method: (1) 推导了三类闭合形式的成对灵敏度指标：预测耦合度 |α_iα_j|、留一残差和边际似然梯度；(2) 将这些灵敏度嵌入Neyman型最小方差分配规则；(3) 为防止预热阶段灵敏度估计噪声导致的灾难性过度集中，引入基于缺失项扰动的Frobenius下界作为高均匀覆盖下限；(4) 在四个UCI数据集和两个合成受控实验上进行验证，并测试真实ZZ和Pauli-Z量子核及四种下游任务。

Result: 在中度采样预算区间内，所提分配器相比均匀分配实现10–21%的测试RMSE提升。该增益成功迁移到量子-自然数据上的真实ZZ和Pauli-Z量子核（低预算下-13–-15%改善，配对检验p<0.05），以及贝叶斯求积、异方差回归、超参数学习和多输出Cokriging四种下游任务。但在UCI特征嵌入ZZ核时增益消失，符合指数集中机制下无分配优化空间的预期。

Conclusion: 基于成对灵敏度的非均匀采样分配策略可有效扩展至高斯过程回归，在中低采样预算下显著优于均匀分配，并具有跨任务和跨核类型的迁移能力。该方法在指数集中区域失效的现象与理论预期一致，为量子核方法在实际NISQ硬件上的高效部署提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Quantum kernel estimation on near-term hardware is shot-budgeted: every entry of the kernel Gram matrix is a Bernoulli expectation that must be sampled with a finite number of circuit executions. Recent work on quantum kernel classification has shown that allocating shots non-uniformly across kernel entries, weighted by their downstream task sensitivity, can reduce the shot budget required to reach a target accuracy. We extend this idea to Gaussian process (GP) regression, a setting whose downstream quantities (full-spectrum posterior variance, log-determinant, marginal likelihood) couple to kernel error more tightly than the sign-only outputs of classification. We derive three closed-form pair-level sensitivities predictive coupling $|α_iα_j|$, leave-one-out residual, and marginal-likelihood gradient and plug them into a Neyman-style minimum-variance allocation rule. To prevent catastrophic over-concentration when the warm-up sensitivity estimate is itself noisy, we add a high uniform coverage floor justified by a Frobenius lower bound on the missing-entry perturbation. On four UCI benchmarks and two synthetic RBF + Bernoulli controlled studies, the resulting allocator delivers $10$--$21\%$ test-RMSE improvement over uniform allocation across the moderate-budget regime. The gain transfers (i) to genuine ZZ and Pauli-Z quantum kernels on quantum-natural data ($-13$--$15\%$ at low budget, $p<0.05$ paired) and (ii) to four downstream tasks (Bayesian quadrature, heteroscedastic regression, hyperparameter learning, multi-output Cokriging). On UCI features embedded into a ZZ kernel the gain disappears, consistent with the exponential-concentration regime where shot allocation has nothing to exploit.

</details>


### [120] [Sample Complexity of Scientific Discovery: PAC Learnability of Compositional Function Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29331)
*Şuayp Talha Kocabay,Talha Rüzgar Akkuş,Kerem Yalçın*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从PAC学习角度重新审视符号回归的统计可行性，证明由光滑算子组合而成的函数树的Rademacher复杂度并非随符号结构数量指数爆炸，而是受深度和算子Lipschitz常数控制。理论给出了形如$\mathcal{O}(L^{d}/\sqrt{n})$的泛化界，并通过可微算子树实验验证了复杂度项与泛化差距的正相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归的假设空间随深度呈组合爆炸，传统观点认为其在统计和计算上均不可行。本文旨在从统计学习理论角度澄清：组合函数树的泛化能力是否必然随符号结构数量指数恶化，并给出可控的泛化界。

Method: 基于PAC学习框架，对由有限词汇表（如$\{+,\times,\sin,\exp\}$及仿射映射）构成的组合函数树进行分析。利用有限联合界（union bound）和Maurer型向量收缩引理，推导出深度$d$下假设类$\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{d}$的Rademacher复杂度上界：$\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{d}) \leq (Kb\sqrt{2}L)^{d-1}\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{1})$，进而得到$\mathcal{O}(L^{d}/\sqrt{n})$的高概率风险界。实验部分构建了可微算子树（非MLP），在可控深度的合成物理类目标上验证理论预测。

Result: 理论上证明了当基词汇表大小$K$和元数界$b$为常数时，泛化风险界为$\mathcal{O}(L^{d}/\sqrt{n})$，其中$L$为算子Lipschitz常数、$d$为深度、$n$为样本量。实验表明，经验泛化差距与预测的复杂度项$(\widehat{L}^{d})/\sqrt{n}$呈正相关，验证了理论的实用性。

Conclusion: 符号回归的统计可行性并非如传统认知般悲观：组合函数树的泛化能力主要受深度$d$和算子光滑性（Lipschitz常数$L$）控制，而非候选符号结构的绝对数量。该结果为深度符号回归提供了理论保障，并指出控制深度和算子光滑性是实现良好泛化的关键。

Abstract: Scientific discovery via symbolic regression is often viewed as statistically and computationally intractable because the hypothesis space of expressions grows combinatorially with depth. This paper revisits the statistical side through the lens of PAC learning, focusing on compositional function trees built from a finite vocabulary of smooth operators (e.g., $\{+,\times,\sin,\exp\}$ and affine maps). We prove that the relevant generalization quantity, Rademacher complexity, hence the excess risk, does not necessarily blow up exponentially with the number of distinct symbolic structures, but is controlled by (i) the depth $d$ and (ii) the Lipschitz constants of the base operators along the composed computation graph. Concretely, under mild Lipschitz conditions on operators and bounded affine leaves, a finite-union bound over a vocabulary of size $K=|\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{base}}|$ together with Maurer-type vector contraction yields $\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{d}) \leq (Kb\sqrt{2}L)^{d-1}\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{1})$ with arity bound $b$; corresponding high-probability risk bounds scale as $\mathcal{O}(L^{d}/\sqrt{n})$ when $K,b=O(1)$ and $\mathfrak{R}_n(\mathcal{H}_{\mathrm{comp}}^{1})=O(n^{-1/2})$. We complement the theory with a modular codebase that trains differentiable operator trees (not MLPs) on synthetic "physics-like" targets of controlled depth and shows that the empirical generalization gap correlates positively with the predicted complexity term $(\widehat{L}^{d})/\sqrt{n}$.

</details>


### [121] [Fisher-Routed Mixture of Experts for Federated Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28835)
*Wenhao Yuan,Chenchen Lin,Jian Chen,Jinfeng Xu,Zewei Liu,Edith Cheuk Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedFMX框架，通过Fisher信息路由的混合专家机制，解决联邦类增量学习中的容量冲突、灾难性遗忘、数据异质性和类别错位问题，实现自适应专家分配，并给出理论收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习扩展到类增量学习场景面临三大核心挑战：(1) 共享模型过载导致的容量冲突与灾难性遗忘；(2) 非独立同分布数据带来的客户端异质性；(3) 同步过程中的类别错位问题。现有方法难以同时应对这些挑战，需要一种能在各客户端自适应进行专家特化的新框架。

Method: 提出FedFMX框架，核心思路是将每个样本路由到最优专家子集。具体包含三个关键模块：(1) FRES模块，基于Fisher信息的稳定性代价和梯度的可塑性增益来估计专家重要性；(2) AES模块，通过量化边际贡献自适应确定专家子集；(3) RAR路由感知正则化，实现负载均衡与高效联邦训练。理论上证明了O(T⁻¹)的收敛速率。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，与现有最先进方法对比，FedFMX展现出显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: FedFMX通过Fisher路由的混合专家机制，有效解决了联邦类增量学习中的容量冲突、遗忘、异质性和类别错位等关键难题，理论与实验均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) emerged as a promising distributed machine learning paradigm. However, extending FL to the class incremental learning scenarios introduces unique challenges: 1) Capacity conflict and catastrophic forgetting from the shared model overloading, 2) Heterogeneity from Non-Independent and Identically Distributed (Non-IID) data, and 3) Synchronized class misalignment. In this paper, we propose \textbf{F}isher-Routed \textbf{M}i\textbf{X}ture of Experts for \textbf{Fed}erated Class-Incremental Learning (\textsc{FedFMX}), a novel framework to address these challenges via adaptive expert specialization across clients. The crucial insight is to route each sample to an expert subset that jointly optimizes knowledge acquisition and retention. Specifically, we introduce a Fisher-Routed Expert Scoring (FRES) module to estimate expert importance via Fisher-based stability cost and gradient-based plasticity gain. Then, we design an Adaptive Expert Selection (AES) module by quantifying marginal contributions for adaptive expert subset determination. Finally, by the routing-aware regularization (RAR), we achieve load balance and efficient FL training. We theoretically prove the $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1})$ convergence rate. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art methods demonstrate the superiority of \textsc{FedFMX}.

</details>


### [122] [A Mathematical Optimization Approach for Expert-Informed Bayesian Best Subset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29516)
*Nolan Alexander,Henning Mortveit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出专家隐含贝叶斯最优子集（EBBS）方法，通过最大后验（MAP）框架将领域专家的概率评估整合到混合整数优化（MIO）最优子集选择中，利用泊松二项分布等聚合多专家意见，以对数几率惩罚项引导特征选择，理论推导完备，实证结果待发表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优子集选择仅利用观测数据，未纳入领域专家对预测变量相关性的先验知识；现有框架缺少将专家概率评估直接融入最优子集目标的机制。

Method: 采用最大后验（MAP）框架，通过泊松二项分布（边际概率估计）、成对胜率（成对比较）或归一化平均秩（序数排名）将多专家意见聚合为每个特征的单先验概率，再以对数几率惩罚项嵌入MIO最优子集目标函数中。

Result: 给出MAP公式的解析推导及其理论性质刻画；当所有专家无先验观点时，模型退化为经典最优子集选择。合成与真实数据的实证结果即将完成。

Conclusion: EBBS成功将专家领域知识以概率形式系统融入最优子集选择，填补了现有方法的空白，具有扎实的理论基础和广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: A central challenge in statistical modeling is identifying the subset of features that belong in the true regression model. The classical best subset selection problem, recently made tractable via mixed-integer optimization (MIO), finds the globally optimal sparse solution. It does not, however, make use of any information beyond the observed data. In many applied settings, domain experts can meaningfully rank or score the relevance of candidate predictors, yet no existing framework integrates such probabilistic expert assessments directly into the best-subsets objective. This paper presents Expert-Implied Bayesian Best Subsets (EBBS), a method that incorporates domain-expert probability estimates of feature relevance into the MIO best-subsets problem through a maximum a posteriori (MAP) framework. Expert views from multiple respondents are aggregated into a single prior probability per feature using the Poisson binomial distribution for marginal probability estimates, the pairwise win rate for pairwise comparisons, or the normalized mean rank for ordinal rankings. This probability enters the objective function as a log-odds penalty term that smoothly encourages or discourages the selection of each feature consistent with the expert consensus. This paper provides analytic derivations of the MAP formulation and characterizes its theoretical properties. The proposed model reduces to Best Subsets when experts all have no views. Empirical results on synthetic and real datasets are forthcoming.

</details>


### [123] [The Contagion Tensor: A Framework for Measuring Output-Distribution Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems -- and Auditing the Claims It Enables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28839)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Contagion Tensor测量框架与耦合放大因子(CAF)指标族，通过仿真消融和真实API实验，量化LLM输出分布在模态、智能体和时间步间的耦合效应，揭示图像条件下的超线性效应实为设计伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏定量、可证伪的测量工具来评估大语言模型输出分布在多模态、多智能体场景下的耦合/传染效应，需要建立基线参照的无量纲指标体系以区分真实耦合与实验设计引入的伪影。

Method: 构建Contagion Tensor（传染张量）作为多维测量框架，从中导出CAF（耦合放大因子），定义为条件期望与基线期望之比（CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline]），并配以Bootstrap置信区间。实例化四种CAF变体，在完整的2×2×2区组正交仿真设计中进行模态特异性消融实验，同时辅以DeepSeek-Chat和GPT-4o-mini的真实API实验验证。

Result: 消融实验发现：图像条件下表观超线性效应（CAF=1.40）在禁用图像扰动模块后坍缩为亚线性（CAF=0.87），降幅-0.53，而文本条件未受影响。真实API实验中：均匀角色设定下文本通信CAF接近1.0；多样化角色促使收敛（CAF=0.88）；GPT-4o-mini内C3（文本）CAF=1.02 vs. C5（真实视觉）CAF=1.72，Δ=+0.70，验证了仿真的超线性预测。11个条件中5个经真实API测试，6个待验证。

Conclusion: 贡献分两层：（1）提供使输出分布耦合可定量证伪的测量工具；（2）提出可迁移的消融协议，供任意模块化多智能体模拟器区分真实耦合与设计伪影。

Abstract: We introduce the Contagion Tensor, a measurement framework for quantifying how large language model (LLM) output distributions couple across modalities, agents, and time steps. From the tensor we derive the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF), a family of ratio-based metrics sharing the form CAF = E[T_condition] / E[T_baseline], providing unitless, baseline-referenced measurement with bootstrap confidence intervals. We instantiate CAF in four variants and evaluate the strongest in a complete 2x2x2 block-orthogonal simulation design with modality-specific ablation. The ablation reveals that an apparent image-condition super-linear effect (CAF = 1.40) collapses to sub-linear (CAF = 0.87) when the image perturbation module is disabled, a shift of -0.53 with zero effect on text conditions. We supplement with real-API experiments across two model families: DeepSeek-Chat (R=30) and GPT-4o-mini (R=15, real vision). Under uniform personas, text-only communication produces CAF approx 1.0 in both models. Diverse personas drive convergence (CAF = 0.88). A within-model comparison on GPT-4o-mini reveals: C3 (text) CAF = 1.02 vs. C5 (real vision, R=30) CAF = 1.72 [1.700, 1.733], delta = +0.70, validating the simulation's super-linear image-condition prediction. Of 11 conditions, 5 have been tested on real APIs and 6 remain unverified. Our contribution is two-layered: (1) a measurement instrument that makes output-distribution coupling quantitatively falsifiable; and (2) a transferable ablation protocol that any modular multi-agent simulator can adopt to distinguish genuine coupling from design artifacts.

</details>


### [124] [Not All Objectives Are Born Equal: Priority-Constrained Descent for Hierarchical Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29521)
*Dara Varam,Mohamed I. Alhajri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD)的梯度优化框架，通过单一参数τ显式利用目标间的层次结构，在保持主目标下降方向的同时对次要目标进行最小必要扭曲，实现目标缩放不变性，并在结构化网络压缩、非结构化稀疏和低秩等问题上展现出优于现有多目标方法的帕累托支配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多目标优化方法存在对称性问题，忽略了深度学习问题中目标之间固有的层次结构——即主目标定义核心目标，而稀疏性、压缩、鲁棒性等次要目标仅作为约束存在。

Method: 提出Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD)，一种基于梯度的优化框架。核心思想是：保持主目标下降方向不变，同时引入由单一参数τ∈[0,1]控制的最小必要扭曲，以保证次要目标取得进展。该方法具有目标缩放不变性，对二目标和三目标问题可推导出精确的闭式解。

Result: 在结构化网络压缩、非结构化稀疏、低秩以及多种合成实验中，PCD展现出帕累托支配能力，相比现有方法在每个目标上均取得更优表现，并提供次要目标进展的保证；参数τ提供了可解释的权衡机制。

Conclusion: PCD通过显式建模目标层次结构，克服了现有多目标方法的对称性缺陷，在理论（闭式解、缩放不变性）和实践（压缩、稀疏、低秩任务）层面均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Deep learning problems rarely involve objectives that are equal in importance. A primary objective defines the goal, whilst secondary objectives, such as sparsity, compression, or robustness constrain the solution. While existing multi-objective methods have proven effective in practice, they have a clear symmetry problem and neglect the inherent objective hierarchy built into these objective spaces. We introduce Priority-Constrained Descent (PCD), a gradient-based optimization framework designed to explicitly exploit hierarchical objective structures. PCD preserves the direction of primary descent whilst allowing for the minimal distortion necessary to guarantee progress on secondary objectives, controlled by a single $τ\in [0, 1]$ that dictates the strength of the distortion. The resulting formulation is invariant to objective scaling and admits exact closed-form solutions for problems with two and three objectives. We evaluate PCD within structured network compression settings, unstructured sparsity and low-rankness, and across a variety of synthetic experiments, showing Pareto dominance and better per-objective performance with secondary progress guarantees over existing methods, further exhibiting the interpretable trade-off that $τ$ provides.

</details>


### [125] [Analysis of Adam Algorithms for Stochastic Dynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28879)
*Xin Zheng,Yifei Jin,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为Adam优化器在非平稳、时变随机系统中的性能建立了通用理论框架，突破了现有分析依赖i.i.d.假设的局限。通过引入非平稳依赖随机矩阵乘积分析技术和新的随机Lyapunov函数，导出了参数跟踪和输出预测误差的显式界，并通过合成与真实数据实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Adam算法的理论分析几乎全部建立在时不变模型参数和i.i.d.数据假设之上，而实际应用（如在线学习、自适应信号处理、强化学习等）中系统往往是时变非平稳的。理论基础的缺失导致对此类场景下Adam行为的理解不足，亟需突破i.i.d.框架建立普适理论。

Method: (1) 引入新分析技术处理Adam耦合的一阶/二阶矩递推所导致的非平稳依赖随机矩阵乘积；(2) 构造融合一阶矩和二阶矩动态的新型随机Lyapunov函数；(3) 在允许非平稳依赖数据的随机激励条件下，推导参数跟踪误差界和输出预测误差界。

Result: 推导出参数跟踪误差和输出预测误差的显式界，这些界量化了步长、一阶/二阶动量参数、梯度噪声和参数漂移对性能的影响，为超参数选择提供了理论指导。合成数据与真实数据实验验证了理论分析和设计准则的有效性。

Conclusion: 论文成功将Adam的理论分析从i.i.d.静态优化框架推广到时变非平稳系统，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。所得误差界不仅提供了性能保证，还为超参数调优提供了原理性指导，对自适应在线学习等领域具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: The adaptive moment estimation algorithm, known as Adam, is widely used in modern machine learning, owing to its low per-iteration complexity and strong empirical performance. Despite its prevalent use, the theoretical foundation of Adam remains largely unexplored for time-varying and nonstationary systems. In fact, the existing theoretical analyses of Adam-type algorithms are primarily concerned with time-invariant model parameters and explicitly or implicitly rely on independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data assumptions, under which the learning taskcan be formulated as minimizing a fixed expected objective with a static minimizer. However, such assumptions are often violated in time-varying and nonstationary systems, thereby calling for a theoretical investigation beyond the conventional yet idealized i.i.d. setting. The main objective of this paper is to solve this challenging problem by establishing a general theory of Adam for time-varying and nonstationary stochastic systems. We will introduce some new techniques for analyzing the products of nonstationary and dependent random matrices induced by Adam's coupled first- and second-moment recursions, and will construct a new stochastic Lyapunov function that blends these two moment dynamics. Under a stochastic excitation condition that allows nonstationary and dependent data, we will derive both parameter tracking and output prediction error bounds explicitly, quantifying the effects of stepsize, first- and second-momentum parameters, gradient noise and parameter drift. These bounds not only provide guarantees for Adam performance, but also provide guidelines for hyperparameter selection. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world data validate our theory and design guidelines.

</details>


### [126] [Optimizer Memory Makes Shuffle Order a First-Order Source of Fine-Tuning Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29554)
*John Sweeney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了固定时钟优化器（如AdamW）中，shuffle顺序会产生一阶（O(η)）而非传统认为的二阶（O(η²)）微调噪声。根源在于优化器的记忆状态按步索引而非按学习率缩放的时间τ=ηk推进，导致同一梯度获得位置依赖的端点权重。作者通过升维状态展开和一阶回放系数刻画了该机制，并给出了无需拟合的噪声量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，对于无记忆优化器，重排等值多重集（equal multiset）不会产生一阶效应，shuffle顺序对微调的影响仅为二阶小量（O(η²)）。但实践中AdamW等现代优化器对shuffle种子极为敏感，单一种子即可翻转A/B对比结论。这种理论与实践的鸿沟需要从优化器记忆机制层面加以解释和量化。

Method: 作者通过以下步骤揭示并量化该噪声机制：(1) 区分“固定时钟”（步索引驱动）与“记忆无关”（τ=ηk驱动）两类优化器更新规则；(2) 对固定时钟优化器进行升维状态展开（lifted-state expansion），推导出一阶回放系数，证明只要该系数非零即产生O(η)等值多重集对比；(3) 设计逐位确定性回放实验，从单一预热状态出发隔离该机制；(4) 对AdamW冻结预条件子时，利用脉冲权重核（impulse-weight kernel）给出闭式渐近阶方差下界。

Result: 实验测得的阶方差斜率分别为：SGD 4.00（二阶）、固定β动量 2.00（一阶）、AdamW 1.83（一阶）。将记忆时钟匹配到τ后，指数恢复为正规值。对冻结预条件子的AdamW，在测得局部势能后，脉冲权重核可给出无拟合系数的闭式渐近方差下界。该方法具有窗口局部性（跨窗口独立重排可平均化通道），并衍生出阶噪声误差棒、位置归因权重及微调对比的种子预算准则。

Conclusion: 固定时钟优化器的记忆结构使得shuffle顺序成为一阶噪声源，量级足以影响A/B微调对比。该机制可通过一阶回放系数隔离，并通过脉冲权重核实现无拟合量化。这为实践中shuffle种子的敏感性问题提供了理论解释，并给出了误差控制与种子预算的具体工具。

Abstract: Shuffle order can be a larger source of fine-tuning noise than a memoryless analysis predicts: fixed-clock optimizer memory makes local equal-multiset contrasts first order in the learning rate rather than second order, and the resulting order channel can be large enough for a single seed to flip a close A/B comparison. We isolate this mechanism and derive a fit-free way to size the noise it produces. For a memoryless optimizer, reordering an equal multiset has no first-order endpoint term; the leading local contrast is the $O(η^2)$ gradient bracket. Fixed-clock optimizers such as AdamW are different. Their moment buffers, preconditioner state, and de-biasing counters advance with the step index rather than with the learning-rate-scaled time $τ=ηk$, so the same gradient can receive a position-dependent endpoint weight. For any fixed finite measurement window, a lifted-state expansion gives an $O(η)$ equal-multiset contrast whenever the first-order replay coefficient is nonzero, while regular and clock-matched controls remain $O(η^2)$; a bare fixed-$β$ momentum buffer is already enough. A bitwise-deterministic replay from one warmed optimizer state isolates the mechanism, giving order-variance slopes 1.83 for AdamW, 2.00 for fixed-$β$ momentum, and 4.00 for SGD; matching the memory clock to $τ$ restores the regular exponent. For AdamW with a frozen preconditioner, the same impulse-weight kernel gives a closed-form asymptotic order-variance floor after the local potentials are measured, with no fitted coefficients. The result is local to the measurement window (independent reshuffling can average the channel across windows), but it yields order-noise error bars, positional attribution weights, and a seed-budget criterion for fine-tuning comparisons.

</details>


### [127] [An Integrated Machine Learning and Hierarchical Variance Decomposition Pipeline for Student Performance Prediction and Metacognitive Calibration on Multi-Signal Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28881)
*Gurdeep Singh Virdee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了UBP-CAP统一框架，将学生行为预测、校准分析与方差分解整合为单一流程，并通过PEDI指标量化预测与解释特征间的结构差异，在1195条交互记录上验证发现校准偏差主要是情境性的而非倾向性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将学生表现预测、校准误差计算和方差分解作为三个独立流程处理，导致无法对智能导学系统中的元认知校准进行统一解释。作者旨在通过一个整合框架消除这种割裂。

Method: 提出UBP-CAP框架，包含三个模块：(1) LightGBM分类器配合SHAP进行二元正确性预测，并与Logistic Regression对比；(2) 使用ECE、MCE和Brier分数分解等形式校准指标评估元认知对齐；(3) 交叉GLMM分解校准偏差。此外，创新性地提出PEDI（预测-解释分歧指数），通过余弦相似度等形式量化预测模型与解释模型在共享行为特征上的结构性分歧。

Result: 在1195条交互记录（27名学生、45个任务）上：Logistic Regression的AUC-ROC达0.903，优于LightGBM（0.878）；学生朴素ECE（0.109）显著高于模型ECE（0.068），证实系统性误校准；交叉GLMM的ICC_student仅为0.123，表明校准偏差是情境性而非倾向性；PEDI_cos=0.081（p=0.327），表明预测与解释在共享行为特征上结构对齐。

Conclusion: UBP-CAP框架成功统一了预测与校准分析流程。核心发现是：学生元认知校准偏差主要由情境因素驱动（而非稳定的个体倾向），且预测模型和解释模型在行为特征层面结构一致。这为智能导学系统的个性化干预设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: Predicting student performance and characterizing metacognitive calibration are essential for personalization in intelligent tutoring systems. Prior research treats performance prediction, calibration error calculation, and variance decomposition as separate pipelines, preventing unified interpretation. I propose the Unified Behavioral Prediction and Calibration Analysis Pipeline (UBP-CAP), an integrated framework processing student pre-execution behavioral telemetry through three linked modules: (1) a LightGBM classifier with SHAP for binary correctness prediction, (2) formal calibration metrics (ECE, MCE, and Brier score decomposition) to evaluate metacognitive alignment, and (3) a crossed Generalized Linear Mixed-Effects Model (GLMM) for decomposing calibration deviations. I introduce the Predictive-Explanatory Divergence Index (PEDI), which quantifies structural divergence between predictive and explanatory feature profiles. Evaluated on 1,195 interaction records (27 students, 45 tasks), Logistic Regression achieves AUC-ROC = 0.903, outperforming LightGBM (0.878). Student naive ECE (0.109) significantly exceeds model ECE (0.068), confirming systematic miscalibration. The crossed GLMM yields ICCStudent = 0.123, showing calibration is situational rather than dispositional. PEDIcos = 0.081 (p = 0.327) indicates structural alignment between prediction and explanation on shared behavioral features.

</details>


### [128] [How AI settled the complexity of the oldest SGD algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29593)
*Michał Dereziński,Xiaoyu Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文讲述了Kaczmarz算法（随机梯度下降的最早实例）与ChatGPT、Gemini等现代AI模型协作，发现该算法最坏情况复杂度的故事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaczmarz算法（1937年提出）是随机梯度下降的最早范例，而SGD是训练现代大语言模型（如ChatGPT、Gemini）的核心范式。作者旨在展示AI模型如何反向帮助揭示这一经典算法的理论性质——最坏情况复杂度，体现AI辅助数学研究的新范式。

Method: 利用ChatGPT、Gemini等大语言模型作为研究辅助工具，与人类研究者协同探索Kaczmarz算法求解线性方程组的最坏情况复杂度，通过人机协作的方式推进理论分析。

Result: 在AI模型的辅助下，成功发现了Kaczmarz算法的最坏情况复杂度。

Conclusion: 该论文以叙事方式呈现了一段独特的研究历程：Kaczmarz算法作为SGD先驱启发了现代AI训练，而这些AI模型又反过来帮助揭示该算法的复杂度下界，形成了数学与AI之间双向促进的闭环。

Abstract: In 1937, Stefan Kaczmarz proposed a simple algorithm for solving systems of linear equations. This algorithm turned out to be the earliest known example of stochastic gradient descent, a ubiquitous computing paradigm that drives the training of modern AI models such as ChatGPT and Gemini. Now, those AI models have joined forces to discover the worst-case complexity of the Kaczmarz algorithm. This paper tells the story of how it happened.

</details>


### [129] [MALOQ: Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28911)
*Manasa Kaniselvan,Alexander Maeder,Denghui Lu,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Mathieu Luisier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MALOQ 是一个基于 SO(2)-等变架构的大规模量子输运算符学习框架，通过自定义数据处理核与可扩展的边级图分布策略，实现了对包含数万原子的体系进行哈密顿量/密度矩阵的高效训练与推理，在 Alps 超算上展现出优异的扩展性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 DFT 电子结构计算成本高昂，难以扩展至大规模体系。现有的机器学习算子模型虽能加速预测，但在处理高秩哈密顿矩阵数据、大规模原子体系（尤其是大基组、多元素）时面临数据吞吐与分布式训练瓶颈。

Method: 提出 MALOQ 框架：采用 SO(2)-等变主干网络架构；设计自定义数据处理核以高效处理高秩哈密顿矩阵数据；引入可扩展的边级（edge-wise）原子图分布策略替代传统的分子级（molecule-wise）分布；在 Alps 超算上部署，支持多达 192 GPU 训练和 256 GPU 推理。

Result: 在目前最大的分子哈密顿量数据集上训练，相比分子级分布框架，每 epoch 时间减少超过 30%；支持任意尺寸材料图的推理；成功在 3,000–12,000 原子的体系上实现可扩展训练与推理。

Conclusion: MALOQ 通过算法与系统层面的协同优化，大幅提升了 ML 算子模型处理大规模电子结构数据的效率与可扩展性，为将电子结构计算推向此前不可行的尺度提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Machine-learned (ML) operator models can be trained to predict density functional theory (DFT) Hamiltonian/density matrices at significantly reduced computational cost, thus extending electronic-structure calculations to previously unfeasible scales. Here, we introduce MALOQ (Massively Accelerated Learning of Operators for Quantum Transport), an application built to train on and predict electronic-structure matrices for systems made of few to 100k atoms, described by large basis sets, and covering a wide range of atomic elements. Based on a state-of-the-art, SO(2)-equivariant backbone architecture, MALOQ provides (i) custom data-processing kernels to handle high-rank Hamiltonian matrix data and (ii) a scalable edge-wise distribution of atomic graph(s). Trained on the largest molecular Hamiltonian datasets available today, it reduces time-per-epoch by over 30% compared to a molecule-wise-distributed framework, and enables inference on material graphs of arbitrary size. We demonstrate scalable training and inference for 3,000-12,000 atoms on the Alps supercomputer, up to 192 GPUs and 256 GPUs, respectively.

</details>


### [130] [ML-Powered LDAP Reconnaissance Detection using Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28917)
*Shaefer Drew,Edward Raff,Michael Brautbar,Yaron Zinar,Benjamin Malmberg,Dor Agron,Sagi Sheinfeld,Avraham Kama,Asaf Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种机器学习框架用于检测LDAP侦察行为：一个基于弱监督的LDAP查询分类器，以及一个基于统计假设检验的恶意LDAP签名挖掘方法。分类器在保留集上达到65%真正率且控制误报，挖掘的签名经CrowdStrike MDR团队验证达到81.48%的字段精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LDAP协议默认允许所有用户读取AD数据，使其成为攻击者在初始入侵后最常用的侦察工具。在侦察阶段早期捕获威胁行为者对于阻止后续攻击至关重要，但传统人工标注方式成本高、耗时长，因此需要一种可大规模自动化构建语料库的实用检测方案。

Method: 方法一：利用弱监督学习，通过将LDAP查询与终端检测结果关联，自动标注大规模数据集，训练二分类器区分恶意与良性LDAP查询。方法二：在分类器基础上，采用严格的统计假设检验框架，自动挖掘新型恶意LDAP查询签名，实现即时部署。

Result: LDAP分类器在保留集上达到最高65%的TPR，同时控制假阳性率；ML驱动的签名挖掘方法产出的签名经CrowdStrike托管检测与响应（MDR）团队验证，字段精度达81.48%。

Conclusion: 尽管弱监督方法相比人工标注存在一定局限性，但其大规模自动化语料构建能力使其在实际部署中更具可行性，两种方法均达到了可用的性能基准，能够有效在侦察阶段早期检测威胁行为者。

Abstract: Lightweight Directory Access Protocol (LDAP) is a protocol that allows users to query and modify Active Directory (AD) data. By default, all users have read access to all AD data through LDAP, making it a common initial tool for reconnaissance when a threat actor first compromises an identity. To capture threat actors early in the reconnaissance phase, we developed two machine learning frameworks to detect LDAP reconnaissance: an ML classifier to predict malicious LDAP queries and an ML-based data-mining method to extract malicious query signatures. By correlating LDAP queries with endpoint detections, the first framework uses weak supervision to label a massive dataset and classify LDAP queries as malicious or benign. For immediate deployment, a second technique was developed on top of this approach to employ a rigorous statistical hypothesis-testing framework for mining novel, malicious LDAP signatures. While this weakly supervised approach is limited compared with manual human labeling, it is more practical for this use case because it leverages large-scale automated corpus construction, reducing costs and time. Ultimately, both the LDAP classifier and the ML-based LDAP signature mining method achieved performance benchmarks, with the classifier achieving up to a 65\% True Positive Rate (TPR) on the holdout set while limiting false positives, and mined signatures demonstrating 81.48\% field precision with CrowdStrike's Managed Detection and Response team.

</details>


### [131] [I-BBS: Coordinate-Free Inference of Latent Sub-Manifolds Using Random Distance Matrix Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29675)
*Igor Halperin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出I-BBS方法，一种无需坐标的推断框架，仅从距离矩阵中识别嵌入高维空间的低维潜在子流形。该方法通过两个整数稳定的谱特征（顶部非Perron多重态的重数及其随噪声收缩的参数无关规律）来恢复潜在几何结构，在合成球面上表现出远超连续谱斜率的噪声鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBS发现距离矩阵的谱编码了底层几何信息，但现有方法依赖高维向量空间的可观测性。然而在实际场景中，环境空间可能仅部分可观测或根本未定义，因此需要一种仅从距离矩阵出发、不依赖坐标的推断方法，且该方法需对噪声具有鲁棒性。

Method: 提出I-BBS框架：首先通过两类生成式噪声（基于模型的和无模型的）对环境嵌入进行建模，噪声将潜在信号与流形外成分混合。然后利用两个整数稳定的谱特征进行推断：(1) 顶部非Perron多重态的重数，用于确定流形维数d；(2) 描述多重态位置随噪声增长而收缩的参数无关规律。这两个特征在噪声下保持稳定，使得从单张距离矩阵即可同时恢复流形结构和噪声模型。

Result: 在合成球面S¹、S²和S³上的实验表明，整数签名在噪声下的稳定性远超连续谱斜率指标。盲测实验成功从单张距离矩阵中同时恢复了流形和噪声模型。

Conclusion: I-BBS是一种仅依赖距离矩阵的坐标自由流形推断方法，其整数稳定签名对噪声具有强鲁棒性，适用于环境空间不可观测或未定义的场景。该方法在神经网络表征和动态训练机制中的应用将在两篇配套论文中展开。

Abstract: Bogomolny, Bohigas and Schmit (BBS) found that the spectrum of the pairwise distance matrix on N points sampled from a smooth d-dimensional manifold encodes a signature of the underlying geometry. We develop I-BBS (Inference-BBS), a coordinate-free method that identifies a low-dimensional latent sub-manifold embedded in a high-dimensional ambient distance matrix alone, without accessing an ambient high-dimensional vector space. It therefore applies even when that space is only partly observable or undefined. We model the ambient embedding by two classes of generative noise, model-based and model-free. The noise mixes the latent signal with off-manifold components, so the eigenvalues reorganise collectively and the latent geometry cannot be read off eigenvalue by eigenvalue. We recover it instead from two integer-stable signatures that survive the noise: the multiplicity of the top non-Perron multiplet, which fixes $d$, and a parameter-free law for how the multiplet positions shrink as the noise grows. On synthetic spheres $S^1$, $S^2$ and $S^3$ these integer signatures are far more stable under noise than the continuous spectral slope, and a blind test recovers both the manifold and the noise model from a single distance matrix. Applications to neural-network representations and to the dynamic training regime are developed in two companion papers.

</details>


### [132] [Towards Improved Anomaly Detection for Cloud Cybersecurity via Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28923)
*Manu Nandan,TJ Jaymes,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图神经网络的自监督学习方法，应用于AWS CloudTrail日志，以检测云环境中的安全威胁。该方法为每个事件生成异常分数，并能动态适应组织变化而无需周期性重训练。在五个组织的实验中，该方法将告警量从传统方法的数千条降至约每小时1条。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于启发式规则或异常检测的云威胁检测方法误报率高，且相对静态，难以适应动态变化的云环境。需要一种能够自动、动态检测可疑事件并大幅降低告警量的方法，以减轻安全分析人员的审查负担。

Method: 采用自监督学习的图神经网络方法，对AWS CloudTrail日志进行建模。模型为每个事件输出一个异常分数，用于识别可疑活动。该方法无需周期性重训练即可动态适应组织环境的变化。

Result: 在五个组织的实验中，该模型几乎在所有情况下都比基于领域专家规则的基线方法产生显著更少的告警，告警量从传统方法的数千条降至约每小时1条。

Conclusion: 该方法在降低告警量方面效果显著，但研究仅评估了被标记的事件，无法估计假阴性率。因此，该研究应被理解为一篇提供实际部署洞察的实践研究，而非经过完整验证的检测系统。未来工作需将该方法扩展到其他云环境并解决当前局限。

Abstract: Detecting security threats in an organization's cloud computing environment has become necessary due to the increased reliance on cloud infrastructure. Logging of all cloud computing events enables investigation into any incidents after they are detected. Automated detection of threats using the logs based on heuristics or anomaly detection could result in a high false positive rate due to its relatively static nature. In this article, we present an industrial case study of a self-supervised learning method using graph neural networks applied to AWS CloudTrail logs to surface suspicious events for analyst review. The model produces an anomaly score for each event and dynamically adapts to changes in the organization without requiring periodic retraining. Based on our experiments across five organizations, the proposed model produced substantially fewer alerts than a domain expert rule-based baseline in almost all cases, reducing alert volumes to approximately 1 per hour from thousands generated by traditional methods. We note that this evaluation covers only flagged events, and false negatives cannot be estimated from the current data; findings should therefore be interpreted as a practical deployment study offering insights into real-world constraints rather than a fully validated detection system. We discuss these limitations and the requirements for extending the approach to other cloud environments as future work.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Agent Routing as Set-Valued Prediction: A WildChat Benchmark and Cost-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28925)
*Ananto Nayan Bala,Faisal Muhammad Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入了一个基于WildChat的多智能体路由基准，包含3000条提示和12个固定智能体目录，采用AI辅助启发式标注和多标签评估协议。研究发现监督路由器显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由，微调编码器在无约束集合精度上最强，而线性多标签模型是最强实用基线；在成本约束下，加权路由层（WAR）叠加监督评分器可进一步提升效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言提示的工具与智能体路由本质上是一个集合值预测问题：单个查询可能需要多个智能体，但过度选择会增加执行成本。现有工作缺乏标准化的多标签评估基准来系统研究准确性与成本的权衡。

Method: 构建了包含3000条提示、12个固定智能体的基准数据集，源自WildChat，使用AI辅助启发式标注和受控重平衡。评估协议包括：集合级指标（Precision/Recall/F1/Jaccard/Exact Match）、延迟、能力覆盖模拟以及基于序数智能体成本分层的约束加权路由设置。比较了最近邻匹配、线性多标签分类、依赖感知基线、微调编码器、加权智能体路由（WAR）以及零样本LLM基线等方法。

Result: 监督路由器在集合精度上显著优于最近邻和零样本LLM路由。微调编码器在无约束设置下取得最强集合准确率，线性多标签模型提供最强实用基线。在约束设置中，WAR加权路由层叠加在强监督评分器上可改善效用，Encoder+WAR组合增益最大。

Conclusion: 该基准和评估协议支持在固定目录多智能体路由场景下对准确率-成本权衡进行可复现研究，为后续工作提供了标准化的评测框架。

Abstract: Tool and agent routing from natural-language prompts is naturally a set-valued prediction problem: a single query may require multiple agents, while over-selection increases execution cost. The benchmark introduced here is derived from WildChat and contains 3,000 prompts over a fixed 12-agent catalog, with AI-assisted heuristic labels under a fixed schema and controlled rebalancing for multi-label evaluation. The evaluation protocol combines set-level metrics (Precision, Recall, F1, Jaccard, and Exact Match), latency, an execution-oriented capability-coverage simulation, and a constrained weighted-routing setting based on ordinal agent-cost tiers. Compared methods include nearest-neighbor matching, linear multilabel classification, dependency-aware baselines, a fine-tuned encoder, deterministic weighted post-scoring via Weighted Agent Routing (WAR), and a zero-shot LLM baseline. Results show that supervised routers substantially outperform nearest-neighbor and zero-shot LLM routing. The fine-tuned encoder achieves the strongest unconstrained set accuracy, while the linear multilabel model provides the strongest practical baseline. In the constrained setting, the weighted routing layer improves utility when applied on top of strong supervised scorers, with the largest gain observed for Encoder+WAR. Overall, the benchmark and evaluation protocol support reproducible study of accuracy-cost trade-offs in fixed-catalog multi-agent routing.

</details>


### [134] [What Drives the Inlier-Memorization Effect? A Theory of Outlier Detection via Early Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29791)
*Kunwoong Kim,Dongha Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对深度模型中的"内点记忆效应"（IM effect）进行了理论分析，以简单自编码器为研究对象，刻画了该效应的出现、强度和持续性，并揭示了数据分布与参数初始化对这些性质的影响，最终提出增强IM效应的实用指南，在ADBench数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管内点记忆效应已在无监督异常检测中取得实证成功，但其理论理解仍然匮乏。本文旨在填补这一空白，为IM效应提供严格的理论基础。

Method: 以简单自编码器为分析模型，在温和假设下，从理论上推导了训练早期阶段模型对内点的记忆成功与对异常点记忆失败的条件；进一步刻画了IM效应的出现时刻、强度与持续性，并分析这些性质如何依赖于数据分布和参数初始化；基于理论洞见，提出数据预处理与参数初始化等实用增强策略。

Result: 理论分析成功表征了IM效应的出现、强度和持续性，并揭示了其与数据分布及参数初始化的依赖关系；基于此提出的实用指南在ADBench数据集上取得了最先进的异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 本研究为内点记忆效应提供了首个系统的理论支撑，不仅解释了该现象为何有效，还给出了可操作的改进方向，为基于IM效应的异常检测方法奠定了理论基础并指明了优化路径。

Abstract: Outlier detection (OD) aims to identify anomalous instances by learning the underlying structure of normal data (inliers), and is particularly challenging in fully unsupervised settings where no information about anomalies is available during training. Recent advances have leveraged the inlier-memorization (IM) effect, a phenomenon in which deep models memorize inlier patterns earlier than those of outliers, as a powerful signal for distinguishing outliers. However, despite its empirical success, the theoretical understanding of the IM effect remains limited. In this work, we present a theoretical study of the IM effect. Focusing on a simple autoencoder, we show that, under mild assumptions, the model can successfully memorize inliers while failing to memorize outliers during certain stages of early training. In particular, we characterize not only the emergence of the IM effect, but also its strength and persistence, and analyze how these properties depend on the data distribution and parameter initialization. In addition, building on these insights, we derive simple yet practical guidelines for enhancing the IM effect, including data preprocessing and parameter initialization schemes, achieving state-of-the-art performance on the ADBench datasets. Our findings provide a theoretical foundation for the IM effect and offer actionable directions for improving IM-based outlier detection methods.

</details>


### [135] [DLR: Zero-Inference-Cost Latent Residuals for Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28932)
*Dong Wang,Wenwu Tang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DLR 是一种训练专用、无额外参数、可折叠的插件，通过在低秩预训练中给输出添加固定的结构化残差，在不增加部署成本的前提下提升模型质量，并在 LLaMA 60M–7B 上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练成本极高，低秩预训练虽能减少参数量和计算量但通常质量不及全秩训练。需要一种方法在保持低秩部署优势的同时缩小这一质量差距。

Method: 提出 Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR)：在低秩输出 Bz 上加一个固定的结构化残差项 α/√K · Expand_K(z)，将每个潜在坐标复制 K=ceil(d_out/r) 次。α 固定，训练时不引入可学习参数；训练后通过闭式解 B* = B + α/√K R^T 将残差吸收到上投影矩阵中，部署时参数、FLOPs 和内存与底层低秩骨干网络完全一致。

Result: 在 LLaMA 60M 到 7B 参数规模的模型上，DLR 在 C4 验证困惑度上多数设置下提升了低秩预训练质量，130M 及以上规模收益最明显；折叠后的 checkpoint 可直接用于标准基准的监督微调，迁移效果良好。

Conclusion: DLR 是一种零额外部署成本的低秩预训练增强方案，能有效缩小低秩与全秩训练之间的质量差距，尤其适用于中等及以上规模的模型，且对下游微调友好。

Abstract: Large language models have driven recent progress in language and multimodal AI, yet pre-training them at scale is prohibitively expensive. Low-rank pre-training, which factorizes each weight matrix into a rank-r product to reduce both parameters and FLOPs, is a promising response but typically lags full-rank training in quality. We propose Duplicated Latent Residual (DLR), a training-only, parameter-free, foldable plug-in for low-rank pre-training. DLR augments the standard low-rank output Bz with a fixed structured residual alpha/sqrt(K) * Expand_K(z) that replicates each latent coordinate K = ceil(d_out/r) times across the output. With alpha fixed, DLR adds zero learnable parameters per layer; after training, it is absorbed into the up-projection in closed form, B* = B + alpha/sqrt(K) R^T, so deployment parameter count, FLOPs and memory match the underlying low-rank backbone exactly. Across LLaMA models from 60M to 7B parameters, DLR strengthens low-rank pre-training on C4 validation perplexity in most settings, with the clearest gains at 130M and above; folded checkpoints transfer cleanly to supervised fine-tuning on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [136] [Decision-Value Attribution in Predict-then-Optimize Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29878)
*Konstantinos Ziliaskopoulos,Alexander Vinel,Alice E. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出决策价值归因（DVA），一个基于Shapley值的框架，用于在"预测-优化"流程中将下游决策价值归因于特征、优化配置或二者交互。通过区分事后DVA与事前DVA，将归因转化为诊断模型运行信念与实现绩效是否对齐的工具。案例研究表明，预测解释无法替代运行价值归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释方法解释的是预测本身，而非预测所引发的决策。在"先预测后优化"系统中，预测的大幅变化可能不改变决策，而微小变化却可能显著改变决策及其价值。因此需要一种直接归因决策价值的方法。

Method: 提出DVA框架，定义以决策价值为支付函数的合作博弈，参与者可以是特征（InfoDVA）、运行/设计参数（DesignDVA）或二者的交互（DVI）。采用Shapley值进行归因，区分事后DVA（基于实现结果评估）和事前DVA（基于模型预测评估）。

Result: 通过电力储能套利和急救医疗服务覆盖两个案例表明：(1)预测解释是运行价值的劣质代理；(2)DVA可指导针对性的信息-控制干预；(3)优化配置决定预测信息何时对决策具有相关性。

Conclusion: DVA提供了一种以运行目标为单位、可分解为相对基线的增益或损失的归因方法，能够将归因从预测层面推进到决策层面，为预测-优化流水线提供更精准的诊断和改进指引。

Abstract: Predictive models are increasingly embedded in operational decision-making, yet standard explanation methods typically explain forecasts rather than the decisions those forecasts induce. This distinction is important in predict-then-optimize systems: large forecast changes may leave the optimizer's action unchanged, while small changes can alter the selected decision and its realized value. We propose Decision Value Attribution (DVA), a Shapley-based framework for attributing the value of a fixed prediction--optimization pipeline. The framework defines cooperative games whose payoff is the downstream decision value, allowing the players to be information sources, optimization or design parameters, or both. We present three variants: InfoDVA attributes value to features, DesignDVA attributes value to operational configurations, and Decision-Value Interactions (DVI) quantifies how information and design jointly create value. We further distinguish post-DVA, which evaluates decisions using realized outcomes, from pre-DVA, which evaluates decisions under the model's full prediction. This separation turns attribution into a decision-level diagnostic of whether the model's operational beliefs align with realized performance. The resulting attributions are expressed in the units of the operational objective and decompose the gain or loss relative to a baseline. Case studies in electricity storage arbitrage and emergency medical service coverage show that predictive explanations can be poor proxies for operational value, that DVA can guide targeted information-control interventions, and that optimization configurations determine when predictive information is decision-relevant.

</details>


### [137] [ReGuide: From Test-Time Guidance to Self-Improving Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28939)
*Tzu-Hsiang Lin,Srinivas Shakkottai,Dileep Kalathil,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGuide 是一种自改进框架，将测试时的引导 rollout 转化为可复用的在线策略恢复数据。它通过阶段条件引导（PCG）生成修正性轨迹，再通过微调或从头重训练将成功经验吸收回策略中，在 Robomimic 多个任务上将成功率提升 1.3–7.7 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆扩散策略虽表现力强，但存在协变量偏移问题：与演示状态的微小偏离会级联为任务失败。现有方案或依赖昂贵的专家修正/合成数据增广，或在测试时引导后丢弃修正轨迹，造成了数据浪费。因此需要一种能将引导 rollout 作为可复用恢复数据反馈至策略训练的自改进方法。

Method: 提出 ReGuide 框架：1）Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG)：构建阶段特定的潜在目标，仅在"已偏离但可恢复"的区域施加引导，并通过估计的干净动作进行引导以匹配动力学模型的训练分布；2）策略吸收：ReGuide-FT 在当前检查点上微调，ReGuide-FS 在增广数据集上从头重训练，两者可组合迭代。

Result: 在 Robomimic 的 Can、Square、Transport 和 Tool Hang 四个任务上，ReGuide 将基础策略成功率提升 1.3–7.7 倍；在仅测试时设置下超越 LPB；匹配数据消融实验表明，性能增益源于引导恢复数据而非单纯增加 rollout 数量。

Conclusion: ReGuide 有效解决了扩散策略的协变量偏移问题，通过将测试时引导与策略再训练形成闭环，实现了数据高效的自改进，在多个复杂操作任务上显著提升了策略的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Behavior-cloned diffusion policies are expressive but remain vulnerable to covariate shift: small deviations from demonstrated states can compound into task failure. Existing methods address this either by expanding the training distribution through expert corrections or synthetic augmentation, or by steering a frozen policy at test time with guidance from a learned model. The former can be expensive or assumption-dependent, while the latter discards the corrected trajectories after execution. We introduce ReGuide, a self-improving framework that treats guided rollouts as reusable on-policy recovery data. ReGuide first uses Phase-Conditioned Guidance (PCG) to generate corrective rollouts: it constructs phase-specific latent targets, applies guidance only in the drifted-but-recoverable regime, and guides through the estimated clean action to match the dynamics model's training distribution. Successful guided rollouts are then absorbed back into the policy through ReGuide-FT, which fine-tunes the current checkpoint, or ReGuide-FS, which retrains from scratch on the augmented dataset; the two can also be composed and iterated. On Robomimic Can, Square, Transport, and Tool Hang, ReGuide improves base-policy success by $1.3$--$7.7\times$, outperforms LPB in the test-time-only setting, and matched-data ablations show that the gains come from guided recovery data rather than additional rollouts alone.

</details>


### [138] [When Is a Draft Accepted? A Theory of Acceptance in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30265)
*Aaryam Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为投机解码中的贪婪解码、松弛接受规则和树候选集等实际常用场景建立了理论分析框架，通过将拒绝区域刻画为目标分布的下水平集，推导出精确的KL散度认证和基于margin的严格界限，并在Qwen3模型上验证了松弛和树方法能显著扩大认证接受区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机解码理论主要关注保持分布（distribution-preserving）的随机采样设置，而实际系统广泛使用贪婪解码、松弛接受规则和树候选集，这些场景的成功取决于局部排序和阈值事件，而非精确的分布匹配，缺乏相应的理论分析。

Method: 1. 识别常见接受准则的拒绝区域可刻画为目标分布的下水平集（lower level sets）；2. 刻画拒绝所需的确切KL散度，推导严格贪婪解码、加性和乘性松弛接受、top-m松弛以及熵阈值接受的精确认证和基于margin的锐利界限；3. 将框架扩展到贪婪树解码，推导目标贪婪token被drafter的top-m候选覆盖时的精确和仅margin认证。

Result: 理论结果表明，拒绝所需KL散度可由目标分布的margin精确刻画；在Qwen3模型上的实验评估显示，松弛和树基接受准则能显著扩大认证接受区域，尤其在目标模型分布margin较低的解码步骤上效果更为明显。

Conclusion: 该理论框架补充了现有分布保持的投机解码分析，刻画了实际推理系统中常见的确定性局部接受事件，为理解和改进实际投机解码系统提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language model inference by using a fast drafter to propose candidate tokens that are then verified by a larger target model. Existing theory largely studies the stochastic, distribution-preserving setting, where the goal is to exactly sample from the target distribution. In contrast, many practical systems use greedy decoding, relaxed acceptance rules, or tree-based candidate sets, where success is governed by local ranking and threshold events rather than exact distributional equality. We develop a theory for these regimes. We identify that many common acceptance criteria have rejection regions that can be characterized as lower level sets of the target distribution. For these, we characterize the exact KL divergence required for rejection yielding exact certificates and sharp margin-based bounds for strict greedy decoding, additive and multiplicative relaxed acceptance, top-(m) relaxed criteria, and entropy-thresholded acceptance. We then extend the framework to greedy tree decoding, deriving exact and margin-only certificates for when the target greedy token remains covered by the drafter's top-(m) candidates. Finally, we evaluate the resulting certificates on Qwen3 models, showing that relaxed and tree-based criteria substantially enlarge the region of certified acceptance, especially on decoding steps with low target model distribution margin. These results complement existing distribution-preserving analyses of speculative decoding by characterizing the deterministic local acceptance events common in practical inference systems.

</details>


### [139] [Machine-learnable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28947)
*Veit Elser,Manish Krishan Lal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了大离散集合的正式定义，这些集合具有元素易识别、易生成且易从样例学习的特性。作者将该形式化专门用于二进制串集合，并基于有界复杂度布尔自编码器的存在性定义了“机器可学习性”。实验通过布尔阈值函数网络实现自编码器，在罗夏克图案及更“狂野”的集合上展示了机器可学习性，并演示了一种简单迭代方法可将狂野集合演化为真正机器可学习的集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于形式化地刻画一类大离散集合——其元素应具备易于识别、易于生成、且这些任务易于从样例中学习这三个直观属性，并探讨基于有界复杂度自编码器的机器可学习性定义，从而为理解什么样的集合结构能够被神经网络有效学习提供理论基础。

Method: 作者提出了基于有界复杂度布尔自编码器的机器可学习性形式化定义：若存在一个布尔自编码器能够将集合中的元素映射到自身（即“固定”这些元素），且该自编码器的复杂度有界，则称该集合是机器可学习的。实验中使用布尔阈值函数网络作为自编码器的实现形式，分别在罗夏克图案（允许镜像半侧对比度反转）和结构更不规则的“狂野”集合上进行验证。对于狂野集合，还引入了一种简单迭代演化过程，使其逐步变得可被有界复杂度自编码器近似固定，最终成为严格机器可学习的集合。

Result: 实验成功展示了罗夏克图案集合的机器可学习性；对于更狂野的集合，虽然元素仅能被可容许的自编码器近似固定，但通过所提出的简单迭代演化方法，这些狂野集合能够转化为真正满足机器可学习性定义的集合。

Conclusion: 本文的结论是：通过有界复杂度布尔自编码器的形式化框架，可以严格定义和验证离散集合的机器可学习性；即使初始结构不规则的集合，也可以通过迭代演化过程变得可学习，这为理解学习的内在条件和集合的结构属性之间的关联提供了新视角。

Abstract: In this study we present a formal definition of large discrete sets having, informally, three properties: their elements are easily recognized, easily generated, and the latter tasks are easily learned from examples.
