Result: （1）自主多智能体系统部署因系统生成决策（而非人类决策）产生伤害，无法满足"一步因果关系"标准；（2）"整体组成部分"测试预设下游人类行为者，无法涵盖自主系统；（3）因此此类平民部署在法律默认归类为"间接参与"，与该框架旨在捕获亲自参与敌对行动的平民的规范目的相悖；（4）现有技术性AI治理工具未记录或报告"目标规格粒度"这一关键属性。

Conclusion: 国际人道法"直接参与敌对行动"框架在AI介导网络行动面前存在结构性不匹配。要弥补这一规制缺口，需关注"目标规格粒度"作为法律—技术桥接属性，并考虑对现有AI治理工具进行相应改造，以实现对平民AI部署行为的更精确法律定性。

Abstract: International humanitarian law protects civilians from direct attack unless and for such time as they take direct part in hostilities, with the ICRC's 2009 Interpretive Guidance operationalising this rule through a three-criterion cumulative test. This paper argues that AI-mediated civilian cyber operations challenge the direct causation element of this test in a structurally specific way: when a civilian deploys an autonomous multi-agent cyber system of the kind recently demonstrated in offensive AI research, the "one causal step" standard fails because harm is produced by system-generated decisions made after human disengagement, and the integral-part requirement does not extend because it presupposes downstream human contributors whose conduct can be independently classified. The framework therefore defaults to treating such deployments as indirect participation, in tension with its purpose of capturing civilians who personally take part in hostilities. Beyond the doctrinal analysis, this paper identifies goal-specification granularity as the property on which the integral-part test's concreteness component implicitly turns, classifies AI-mediated operations along a five-level spectrum, and argues that existing technical AI governance instruments do not log or report this property.

</details>


### [173] [Selective Memory Retention for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29178)
*Pranath Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TraceRetain 是一个为冻结LLM智能体设计的轻量级有界外部记忆框架，通过可解释特征评分并驱逐低分条目。研究表明，在干净环境中不同保留策略差异不大，但在噪声注入场景下，有界保留策略（如TraceRetain-CEM）显著优于无界记忆和FIFO，能有效抵御记忆污染，保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究记忆增强型LLM智能体中"保留策略"何时真正起作用。现有研究多关注记忆的引入，但对记忆容量有限时如何选择保留/驱逐条目、以及不同保留策略在何种条件下产生实质性差异，缺乏系统分析。

Method: 提出TraceRetain框架：基于七个可解释特征（成功与否、年龄、访问频率、冗余度、特异性、相似度、下游效用）对记忆条目打分，容量满时驱逐最低分条目。在ALFWorld环境上，使用gpt-5-mini，对比无记忆、无界记忆、FIFO-K50、TraceRetain-CEM等策略。设计干净环境和受控噪声写入压力（75%合成干扰项）两种条件进行测试。

Result: 干净ALFWorld（T=100到T=200）：外部记忆一致优于无记忆，但不同有界保留策略间差异落在Wilson 95%置信区间内。噪声条件下：无界记忆和FIFO-K50的Precision@5分别从20.2%降至12.4%、15.8%降至3.8%，而TraceRetain-CEM基本不变（16.9%到16.6%），并保持97/100任务成功。无界记忆具有最高平均相似度（0.87）但最低精度，表明失败的干扰项在嵌入空间中与查询接近。保留集外分布评估中，记忆增强策略解决47-49/50任务，无记忆仅39/50。

Conclusion: 有界保留策略在饱和的干净基准上可提升记忆和步骤效率而不牺牲任务成功率，但仅在输入流含噪声时，智能保留策略才与简单缓存启发式（如FIFO）产生实质性分化。干净环境本身并不自然展现保留策略旨在解决的记忆污染问题。

Abstract: When does retention matter for memory-augmented LLM agents? We study this with TraceRetain, a lightweight framework for bounded external memory in frozen LLM agents that scores entries by interpretable features (success, age, access frequency, redundancy, specificity, similarity, downstream utility) and evicts the lowest-scoring ones at capacity. On clean ALFWorld with gpt-5-mini, external memory robustly improves over no memory across two seeds, but differences among bounded retention policies fall within Wilson 95% CIs: clean ALFWorld at T=100 to T=200 does not naturally exhibit the memory pollution retention is designed to address. Under a controlled noisy-write stress (75% synthetic distractors), unbounded memory and FIFO-K50 degrade on Precision@5 (20.2% to 12.4% and 15.8% to 3.8%) while TraceRetain-CEM is essentially unchanged (16.9% to 16.6%) and preserves 97/100 task success. The mechanism: unbounded memory has the highest mean similarity (0.87) but lowest precision, indicating failed distractors close to the query in embedding space. Held-out in-distribution evaluation shows memory-augmented policies solving 47 to 49 of 50 tasks vs. 39/50 for no memory. Bounded retention buys memory and step efficiency on saturated clean benchmarks at no task-success cost, and only differentiates from cache heuristics when streams contain noise.

</details>


### [174] [Measuring Graph-to-Graph Semantic Similarity in Knowledge Graphs: An Empirical Evaluation of Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29180)
*Seungryeol Baek,Wooseok Sim,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了知识图谱（KG）的图级别语义相似度问题，构建了基于文档修改的语义匹配数据集，并提出两种基于KG嵌入的评分方法（EmbPairSim和AvgEmbSim），实验表明EmbPairSim在参数更少的情况下比Sentence-BERT的MRR高出最多5.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KG嵌入方法主要关注实体、关系和三元组级别，忽略了图级别的语义信息；而传统的图结构对比方法仅依赖结构模式，无法保证语义相似性。因此需要系统评估不同方法捕捉图级别语义信息的能力。

Method: 1. 数据集构建：通过修改文本文档，分别从原始和修改后的文档中提取KG，将文档间的已知对应关系迁移到KG对上，构建语义匹配数据集。2. 对比方法：包括基于文本的方法（如Sentence-BERT）、基于结构的方法和基于KG嵌入的方法。3. 提出两种KG嵌入评分函数：EmbPairSim（基于最大成对实体相似度）和AvgEmbSim（基于频率加权质心）。在WikiText-2和CC-News数据集上进行实验评估。

Result: EmbPairSim在WikiText-2和CC-News数据集上相比Sentence-BERT，MRR最高提升5.3个百分点，且使用的参数量显著更少。

Conclusion: KG嵌入表示可以作为图到图语义相似度的紧凑且有效的信号，EmbPairSim方法在性能和效率上均具有优势。

Abstract: A Knowledge Graph (KG) represents facts as structured triples and is widely used to organize relational knowledge across diverse domains. Just as textual information ranges from words and sentences to complete documents, KG information can be interpreted at multiple levels, from entities, relations, and triples to subgraphs and entire KGs. However, existing KG embedding methods mainly focus on entities, relations, and triples, leaving graph-level semantics largely unaddressed. Conventional graph-level methods, which typically compare graphs based on structural patterns, are also insufficient because structural similarity alone cannot guarantee semantic similarity between KGs. To evaluate how well different methods capture such graph-level semantic information, we study graph-to-graph semantic similarity, which determines whether a pair of KGs represents semantically corresponding underlying information. To obtain reliable ground-truth correspondences, we construct a semantic matching dataset by modifying text documents, extracting KGs from both original and modified documents, and transferring their known correspondences to KG pairs. We compare text-based, structure-based, and KG embedding-based approaches on each dataset. For the KG embedding-based approach, we introduce two scoring functions: \textit{EmbPairSim}, which uses maximal pairwise entity similarity, and \textit{AvgEmbSim}, which uses a frequency-weighted centroid. Experiments on WikiText-2 and CC-News show that \textit{EmbPairSim} achieves up to 5.3 pp higher MRR than Sentence-BERT while using substantially fewer parameters. These results suggest that KGE representations can serve as compact and effective signals for graph-to-graph semantic similarity in KGs. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungRyeolBaek/KG-to-KG-Semantic-Similarity.

</details>


### [175] [Evidence-Informed LLM Beliefs for Continual Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29182)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Reece Adamson,Andrew McCallum,Peter Clark,Ashish Sabharwal,Bodhisattwa Prasad Majumder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM科学发现中"贝叶斯惊奇"应是非稳态的（随信念更新而变化），而非静态量。作者提出基于证据的信念更新机制，通过检索增强生成修正惊奇度量，并引入信念更新过滤和多样性最大化两种搜索改进，在五个领域中将累积非稳态惊奇平均提升30.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作（如AutoDiscovery）将"贝叶斯惊奇"视为静态量——即LLM对假设证据的信念偏移在搜索过程中保持不变。然而，人类推理中的惊奇是非稳态的：它相对于随经验不断演化的信念而定义，这是持续科学发现的前提。作者识别出这一根本性不匹配，并致力于修复它。

Method: 首先，提出"证据知情LLM信念"：利用先前假设的证据来更新先验，从而为后续新假设计算非稳态惊奇。对比了多种上下文信念更新机制，发现基于嵌入的检索增强生成（RAG）能最好地预测最终后验，并识别出37.5%的静态惊奇为虚假信号。然后，从两个互补角度改进搜索过程：(1) 信念更新过滤——筛除在非稳态信念下不再令人惊奇的假设；(2) 多样性最大化——优先探索与已有发现不同的假设方向。

Result: 在五个发现领域（涵盖多个科学学科）上，所提方法相比原始搜索过程，累积非稳态惊奇平均提升30.62%。基于嵌入的RAG信念更新机制成功识别了37.5%的静态惊奇为虚假惊奇。

Conclusion: 持续的科学发现不仅需要更好的信念度量（非稳态惊奇），还需要避免冗余、鼓励多样性的搜索过程。将信念更新机制与改进的搜索策略相结合，是LLM实现真正持续科学发现的关键路径。

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery with large language models (LLMs) increasingly operates as a long-horizon loop of hypothesis search and verification, where a reward signal guides which hypotheses to test next. A notable recent example is AutoDiscovery, which uses "Bayesian surprise" - the belief shift an LLM undergoes after observing evidence for a hypothesis - as both a discovery metric and a reward for search. We first observe that AutoDiscovery treats surprisal as a static quantity, while surprisal in human reasoning is non-stationary - it is defined relative to beliefs that evolve with experience, a prerequisite for continual scientific discovery. We address this mismatch with evidence-informed LLM beliefs: priors updated with evidence from previous hypotheses to compute non-stationary surprisal for new hypotheses. We compare in-context belief-updating mechanisms and find that embedding-based retrieval-augmented generation over prior discoveries best anticipates eventual posteriors, identifying 37.5% of static surprisals as spurious. We then modify search to avoid these spurious rewards and prioritize hypotheses that remain surprising under non-stationary beliefs. Concretely, we introduce two complementary changes to the original search procedure: belief-update filtering and diversity maximization. Across five discovery domains, our method increases accumulated non-stationary surprisal by 30.62% on average compared to the original search procedure, demonstrating that continual scientific discovery with LLMs requires not only better belief measurement but also search procedures that avoid redundancy and encourage diversity.

</details>


### [176] [AI Trading's Alpha Singularity: Emergent Market Reasoning through Agent-to-Agent Self-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29194)
*Yuqi Li,Siyuan Liu,Bingjun Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出密封联合搜索（SJS）框架，通过结构性信息流约束防止自主发现系统中的自证偏差。在Agora多智能体系统中验证：LLM智能体通过受限通信进化alpha库，100轮后实现样本外夏普比率+1.87，显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化alpha挖掘将评分函数固定、仅变化搜索算法，导致搜索过度拟合评分器无法惩罚的因素，造成样本外泛化差距。核心问题在于：当评分函数本身也是搜索产物时，联合搜索容易退化为自我确认（self-confirmation）。需要建立一套结构性条件来规范自主发现系统中的信息流，使联合搜索具有可容许性。

Method: 提出SJS框架，包含五项结构性条件：角色分解（role decomposition）、类型化角色间通信（typed inter-role communication）、来源密封读取（provenance-sealed reads）、版本化存储（versioned stores）和基板局部提升（substrate-local promotion）。Agora系统实现：五类LLM智能体通过三个通道通信，进化八个技能库，基于AlphaGen算子构建alpha库。三名评估者分别撰写报告后汇总为一份简报，保留分歧而非投票。在CSI 1000上运行100轮，91天2026年样本外数据对所有LLM输入密封。消融实验：冻结库中预加载两项指标、以及无库进化的PPO。

Result: Agora实现样本外夏普比率+1.87。最佳基线在有利种子下+1.334，跨种子均值-0.755。冻结库消融仅恢复+2.25夏普差距中的+0.40；添加PPO但无库进化则使差距恶化。两项关键指标自发涌现而非人为设计。

Conclusion: SJS框架通过结构性信息约束有效防止联合搜索中的自证偏差，Agora验证了其有效性。自发涌现的评估指标表明系统发现了人类未预设的信号。局限性包括：单种子运行、做空端信号集中、设计为多空组合。

Abstract: Automated alpha mining holds the scoring function fixed and varies the search algorithm over it. A search that converges against a fixed scorer overfits whatever the scorer cannot penalize, a primary cause of the out-of-sample generalization gap. We treat the scoring function as a search artifact alongside the alpha factors and study what conditions make this joint search admissible. Sealed Joint Search (SJS) is a framework: a set of structural conditions on information flow in an autonomous-discovery system that prevent joint search from collapsing into self-confirmation while keeping the evaluator sealed. Conditions cover role decomposition, typed inter-role communication, provenance-sealed reads, versioned stores, and substrate-local promotion. Agora tests SJS empirically: five LLM agent classes communicate via three channels, evolving eight skill libraries, with alpha libraries built on AlphaGen operators. Three evaluators write reports aggregated into one brief, carrying forward disagreement instead of voting. We run Agora for 100 rounds on CSI 1000 and evaluate on a 91-day 2026 holdout sealed from all LLM inputs. Agora achieves holdout Sharpe +1.87; best baseline +1.334 at favorable seed and -0.755 cross-seed mean. Pre-loading Agora's two metrics into a frozen-library ablation recovers only +0.40 of the +2.25 Sharpe gap, and adding PPO without library evolution worsens the gap. The two metrics emerge rather than being designed. Caveats: single-seed run, short-side concentrated signal, intended for long-short.

</details>


### [177] [A Cognition-Emotion-Personality Framework for Modeling Human-Like Awareness and Behavior in Emergency Evacuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29212)
*Zoi Lygizou,Michalis Zervas,Helena G. Theodoropoulou,Vasilis Zafeiropoulos,Dimitris Kalles,Chairi Kiourt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个整合认知、情感、社会与人格机制的智能体疏散仿真框架，通过动态事件感知、记忆型出口知识、连续恐惧/恐慌模型及OCEAN人格特质，更真实地再现紧急疏散中的人群行为，揭示了这些心理因素对疏散效率的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的疏散仿真多依赖完美事件感知、完整出口知识和完全理性决策等理想化假设，忽视了人类在紧急情境下的认知局限、情感反应和人格差异，导致模拟结果与现实人群行为存在偏差，亟需一个更统一、更真实的人类行为模型。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的扩展疏散框架，包含四个核心机制：(1) 基于连续"事件确定水平"的动态事件感知机制；(2) 涵盖获取、遗忘与回忆过程的记忆型出口知识表征；(3) 将恐慌视为高强度状态的连续恐惧模型；(4) 基于OCEAN的人格表征，其中神经质被显式集成到情绪模型中，影响恐惧生成、升级、社会传染与恢复。此外，通过个体化决策阈值捕捉行为异质性。通过仿真实验系统考察空间熟悉度、记忆鲁棒性、决策敏感性、情绪动态及人格变异对疏散的影响。

Result: 仿真结果表明，认知、情感和人格驱动的过程显著影响疏散动态，降低了疏散效率，并生成了延迟、混乱、受伤以及社会影响行为等现实人群现象。

Conclusion: 该框架为紧急疏散中的人类行为提供了更真实的表征，支持对认知、情感、人格与人群动态之间交互作用的系统研究，克服了传统模型过度简化的局限。

Abstract: Agent-based evacuation simulations are widely used to study crowd behavior during emergencies, but many models rely on assumptions such as perfect event awareness, complete exit knowledge, and fully rational decision-making. This paper presents an extended evacuation framework that integrates cognitive, emotional, social, and personality-related mechanisms into a unified model of human behavior under uncertainty. The framework incorporates a dynamic event-awareness mechanism based on a continuous Event Certainty Level, a memory-based representation of exit knowledge subject to acquisition, forgetting, and recall, a continuous fear model in which panic emerges as a high-intensity state, and an OCEAN-based personality representation. Neuroticism is explicitly integrated into the emotional model, influencing fear generation, escalation, social contagion, and recovery. Behavioral heterogeneity is further captured through individualized decision thresholds that affect responses to perceived risk. The framework is evaluated through simulation experiments examining the effects of spatial familiarity, memory robustness, decision sensitivity, emotional dynamics, and personality variation. Results show that cognitive, emotional, and personality-driven processes substantially influence evacuation dynamics, reducing evacuation efficiency and generating realistic crowd phenomena such as delays, confusion, injuries, and socially influenced behaviors. The proposed framework provides a more realistic representation of human behavior in emergency evacuations and supports systematic investigation of the interactions between cognition, emotion, personality, and crowd dynamics.

</details>


### [178] [PolicyGuard: A Dialogue-Grounded Sub-Agent Verifier for Policy Adherence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29225)
*Seongjae Kang,Taehyung Yu,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: POLICYGUARD 是一种子代理验证器，通过利用完整对话上下文、基于公司策略进行自我推理，并提供可操作的补救反馈来引导代理的下一轮行为，从而显著提升LLM代理在航空公司客服场景中的策略合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将LLM代理的策略遵循问题视为安全防护（safeguarding）问题，仅在外部阻断违规行为。但真实工作流跨越多轮对话，需要显式用户确认和前置读取操作，且合规性取决于对话内容而非单一参数值。因此需要一种能够利用完整对话上下文、进行策略推理并提供对话级补救的方案。

Method: 提出POLICYGUARD，一个子代理验证器（sub-agent verifier），与主代理共享对话视图，在上下文中对策略进行推理，并为主代理的下一轮行为生成可操作的反馈。在tau^2-BENCH航空场景下，跨三种大模型（GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Pro）进行四轮重复实验评估。

Result: POLICYGUARD在三种模型上的PASS4指标分别提升+12.0、+6.0、+12.0个百分点。逐调用分析表明，POLICYGUARD在实现更高策略违规召回率的同时，其阻断频率仅为基于参数级别防护方法的大约一半。

Conclusion: 策略遵循是一个比单纯安全防护更广泛的问题。POLICYGUARD通过整合完整上下文、策略推理和对话级补救三种能力，在策略违规检测召回率和误阻断率方面均优于传统的参数级别防护方法。

Abstract: LLM agents handle user requests on behalf of organizations through tool calls and must follow the company policies stated in their system prompts. Prior work approaches this as a safeguarding problem -- external checks that block non-compliant agent actions. We argue that policy adherence is a broader problem: real workflows unfold across many turns, require explicit user confirmation and prerequisite reads, and hinge on the content of the dialogue rather than on any single argument value. Meeting this bar requires (i) full conversation context, (ii) self-reasoning over the policy and the current dialogue, and (iii) conversation-specific remediation that guides the agent's next turn -- three capabilities that prior safeguard work has often underestimated. We introduce POLICYGUARD, a sub-agent verifier that shares the agent's view of the dialogue, reasons over the policy in context, and provides actionable feedback for the agent's next turn. On tau^2-BENCH airline across three vendors (GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Pro) with four trials per setting, POLICYGUARD improves PASS4 by +12.0 / +6.0 / +12.0 pp. Per-call analyses show POLICYGUARD achieves higher policy-violation recall while blocking roughly half as often as argument-level guards.

</details>


### [179] [SurgVLA-Bench: Towards Evaluating Vision-Language-Action Models for Laparoscopic Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29247)
*Jiashuo Sun,Yue He,Wenxuan Liu,Tao Mao,Jiazheng Wang,Xiang Chen,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SurgVLA-Bench，首个面向腹腔镜手术机器人的VLA模型标准化评测基准，基于SurRoL构建层级化任务分类与多维度评估框架，系统对比自回归与流匹配两类范式，揭示当前模型在语义理解与任务精度间的权衡及物理瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在外科具身智能中前景广阔，但缺乏针对手术场景的标准化评测平台，现有基准均为通用机器人设计，无法有效评估手术环境下的特殊挑战（如受限内窥镜视野、遮挡等）。

Method: 基于SurRoL腹腔镜仿真平台，构建从原子动作到完整手术过程的层级化任务分类体系；设计多维度评估框架（动作准确性与语义一致性）；系统评估两类代表性范式——自回归模型（OpenVLA）与流匹配模型（π₀、π₀.₅、SmolVLA）。

Result: 自回归模型在语义理解方面表现更优，流匹配模型任务精度更高但面临泛化权衡；即使最优模型也远未达到满意水平，受限的内窥镜视野、有限视角及频繁遮挡构成根本性物理瓶颈。

Conclusion: SurgVLA-Bench填补了手术VLA评估空白，揭示了当前模型在手术场景中的能力差异与共性瓶颈，为后续外科VLA研究提供了标准化评测基础与改进方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models represent a promising direction for embodied intelligence in surgical robotics. Despite the prevalence of VLA benchmarks for general robotics, standardized evaluation platforms specifically designed for surgical contexts remain absent. To address this limitation, we present SurgVLA-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating VLA models in laparoscopic surgical robotics. Leveraging the SurRoL simulation platform, we construct a hierarchical task taxonomy ranging from atomic actions to complete surgical procedures, complemented by a multi-dimensional evaluation framework assessing action accuracy and semantic consistency. We then systematically evaluate two representative paradigms, including autoregressive models such as OpenVLA, and flow matching models such as $π_{0}$, $π_{0.5}$, and SmolVLA. Our experiments show that autoregressive models tend to excel in semantic understanding, while flow matching models often achieve higher task precision but may face generalization trade-offs. However, even the best-performing models remain far from satisfactory, as the constrained endoscopic field of view, restricted viewing angles, and frequent occlusions persist as fundamental physical bottlenecks. The code and data are available at https://github.com/VCL-HNU/SurgVLA

</details>


### [180] [When Summaries Distort Decisions: Information Fidelity in LLM-Compressed Financial Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29251)
*Hoyoung Lee,Suhwan Park,Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,CheolWon Na,Zhangyang Wang,Zach Golkhou,Minkyu Kim,Sotirios Sabanis,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Dhagash Mehta,Soonyoung Lee,Chanyeol Choi,Wonbin Ahn,Yongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 金融信息压缩中，LLM虽能生成流畅准确的摘要，但可能改变原始材料所支持的投资决策。本文将此问题定义为信息保真度损失，识别出"去语境化"和"模型依赖性"两种诊断模式，并提出通过生成多个候选压缩并审计其分歧的代理式上下文压缩方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融决策者面临信息过载，需要上下文压缩。但LLM压缩金融材料时，即使摘要流畅且事实正确，仍可能改变原始材料所支持的投资判断。这一问题在代理系统中可能逐步放大，而现有评估仅关注效率和事实准确性，忽视了决策保真度。

Method: 1. 将问题框架化为"信息保真度"：压缩后文本是否改变了原始材料引导的决策；2. 在金融申报文件和财报电话会议记录上测试LLM压缩效果；3. 识别两种保真度损失的诊断模式：去语境化（保留显著证据但剥离警示和语境限定词）和模型依赖性（不同压缩器呈现同一源材料的不同视角）；4. 提出代理式上下文压缩（Agentic Context Compression），生成多个候选压缩版本，并与原始材料交叉审计其分歧。

Result: LLM压缩能生成流畅且事实合理的压缩文本，但仍会改变下游决策。去语境化导致关键证据因缺少限定语境而被误读；模型依赖性表明不同压缩模型对同一材料的选择性呈现差异显著。代理式压缩方法通过多候选审计机制可缓解上述问题。

Conclusion: 金融场景下的上下文压缩不应仅以效率和事实准确性为评估标准，还必须衡量其保留决策相关语境的能力，即信息保真度。代理式上下文压缩为提升保真度提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Financial decision-makers face more information than they can directly inspect, making context compression necessary. Yet when large language models (LLMs) compress financial source material, they can alter the investment judgment supported by the original source. We frame this problem as information fidelity: compression loses fidelity when it changes the decision induced by the source. In agentic systems, such losses may recur across intermediate steps and amplify throughout the decision process. Across financial filings and earnings-call transcripts, we find that LLM-based compression can produce fluent and factually plausible compressed contexts that nevertheless alter downstream decisions. We analyze two diagnostic patterns associated with fidelity loss: decontextualization, where salient evidence is retained but separated from the caveats and contextual qualifiers needed for correct interpretation, and model dependency, where different compressors expose different views of the same source. We then propose Agentic Context Compression, which generates multiple candidate compressions and audits their disagreements against the original source. Our results suggest that financial compression should be evaluated not only by efficiency or factuality, but also by its ability to preserve decision-relevant context.

</details>


### [181] [The Complexity Ceiling Benchmark: A Multi-Domain Evaluation of Sequential Reasoning Under Depth Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29278)
*Shubh Chapra,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Yash Sinha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出复杂度上限基准(CCB)，在三个结构不同的推理域中固定语义内容、仅改变所需推理步数(N=5~50)，测量语言模型的推理衰退规律。发现模型呈几何级逐步衰退，不同域之间存在巨大上限差异：前两个域最强模型在N=50仍保持>0.92的步均准确率，而第三域所有模型在N=5时已崩溃。14.5%的正确答案经由错误中间推理得出；强制详细状态追踪不提升上限；首次发散步数k*比参数量更能预测域内准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型推理能力评估缺乏对长序列推理链中"深度"维度的系统控制。现有基准混淆了语义复杂度与结构深度，无法测量推理能力随步数增加的衰退模式，也难以定位模型在何种推理深度下失效。

Method: 设计CCB基准，固定语义内容，仅在三个结构不同的推理域（空间状态追踪、抽象符号指针操作、传递关系推理）中系统变化深度N∈{5,...,50}。在5个前沿及开源LLM上进行6000次试验。引入TFBC（最终答案正确但推理过程错误的比例）和k*（推理首次发散的平均步数）等过程级指标；采用几何衰退模型拟合步均准确率pd，将长程推理能力压缩为每任务族一个可解释参数。

Result: 三个域呈现截然不同的上限：(1)空间状态追踪和(2)符号指针操作中，最强模型pd>0.92持续至N=50；(3)传递关系推理中所有模型在N=5时已崩溃，最佳模型50%成功率仅约4.7步。全基准14.5%正确答案经错误中间推理得出。强制详细状态追踪无显著改善(McNemar p=1.000)。首次发散步数k*比参数量更能预测域内准确率。

Conclusion: CCB和几何衰退模型可将模型的长期推理能力简化为每个任务族的一个可解释数字。语言模型的推理衰退遵循几何规律，且不同推理域的上限差距悬殊，表明当前模型在某些基本推理结构上存在根本性脆弱性。过程级分析揭示表面正确率掩盖了推理路径中的系统性错误。

Abstract: We introduce the Complexity Ceiling Benchmark (CCB), a controlled evaluation of how language-model reasoning decays as the number of required sequential steps grows. CCB fixes the semantic content of a task and varies only its depth N in {5,...,50} across three structurally distinct regimes: grounded spatial state-tracking, abstract symbolic pointer manipulation, and transitive relational inference. Across 6,000 trials over five frontier and open-weight LLMs we find a consistent pattern of geometric per-step decay with widely separated domain ceilings: on the first two regimes the strongest models retain pd>0.92 across N=50; on the third every model collapses by N=5, with the best model's 50%-success horizon at H0.5~4.7 steps despite pd=0.863. A trace-level metric (TFBC) shows that 14.5% of correct answers across the benchmark are reached via incorrect intermediate reasoning. Forced verbose state-tracking does not move the ceiling (McNemar p=1.000), and the mean step at which reasoning first diverges, k*, predicts within-domain accuracy better than parameter count. CCB and the geometric decay model together reduce a model's long-horizon reasoning profile to one interpretable number per task family.

</details>


### [182] [Process Advantage Signal Shaping: A Paradigm-Agnostic Middleware for Process-Supervised RL in LLM Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29296)
*Chao Wang,Hongtao Tian,Tao Yang,Yunsheng Shi,Ting Yao,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PASS（Process Advantage Signal Shaping）是一种插入GRPO与过程信号之间的中间件，通过优势融合、按值分块和除以长度三项技术，解决了GRPO在引入稠密过程监督时产生的通道污染、分辨率不匹配和累积陷阱三大结构性问题，在数学推理和多跳问答任务上均取得一致性的pass@1提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO是LLM推理器过程监督强化学习的默认方案，但引入稠密的过程奖励信号（如学习到的PRM或在策略蒸馏KL信号）会暴露三个结构性问题：通道污染（池化时过程、结果和格式信号相互干扰）、分辨率不匹配（过程信号的粒度与逻辑决策粒度不一致）、累积陷阱（return-to-go求和导致长度膨胀或探索截断）。需要一种系统性的解决方案来消除这些病态。

Method: 提出PASS中间件，包含三项技术：（1）Advantage Fusion：在每组内对过程、结果、格式三个信号流独立标准化，消除通道污染；（2）Chunk-by-Value：从标量过程信号本身推导值同质化分块，在块内广播信用以对齐分辨率；（3）Divide-Length：将累积目标转换为平均价值密度得分，避免长度膨胀和探索截断。在两个领域（数学推理、多跳问答）、两种过程信号范式（学习PRM、在策略蒸馏KL信号）以及两种分组标准化算子下验证。

Result: 在所有实验设置下（涵盖不同领域、过程信号范式和分组标准化算子），PASS均相对于对应的GRPO基线取得了一致性的pass@1性能提升。

Conclusion: PASS作为一种轻量级中间件，有效修复了GRPO在引入稠密过程监督时的三项结构病态，是一种通用、鲁棒的改进方案，无需改动GRPO核心算法即可稳定提升推理性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a default recipe for process-supervised reinforcement learning of LLM reasoners, and dense process supervision -- via learned process reward models (PRMs) or on-policy-distillation KL signals -- is a common way to densify its otherwise weak outcome reward. Layering such a step-level signal on top of GRPO's group-standardized advantage, however, exposes three structural pathologies: \emph{channel contamination} between the pooled process, outcome, and format streams at group standardization; \emph{resolution mismatch} between the granularity of the process signal and the granularity of the logical decisions being credited; and a \emph{cumulative trap} by which GRPO's return-to-go sum surfaces either length inflation or truncated exploration depending on the sign regime of the signal. We propose \textbf{PASS} (\emph{Process Advantage Signal Shaping}), a compact middleware that sits between any scalar step-level process signal and GRPO's clipped surrogate and addresses the three pathologies in turn: \emph{Advantage Fusion} standardizes the three streams independently within each group, \emph{Chunk-by-Value} derives value-homogeneous chunks from the signal itself and broadcasts credit within each chunk, and \emph{Divide-Length} converts the cumulative objective into an average-value-density score. We validate PASS across two domains and two process-signal paradigms -- a learned PRM on mathematical reasoning and an on-policy-distillation KL signal (with a generalized variant) on multi-hop question answering -- and under two group-standardization operators. In every regime PASS delivers a consistent pass@1 gain over the corresponding GRPO baseline.

</details>


### [183] [Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29315)
*Abhranil Chandra,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Utsav Dhanuka,Varun Gandhi,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HExA 是一种免训练、与黑箱模型兼容的智能体框架，通过在上下文中主动实验来自我提升。它迭代设计实验、学习可复用的技能库，并整合实验证据来回答查询或执行动作。在 Interphyre 基准上，HExA 将 Claude Sonnet 4.6 的成功率从 2% 提升至 77%，且仅靠技能迁移（无主动实验）即可达到 44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 智能体主要依赖参数化知识、固定数据、检索或搜索，在全新领域和需要复杂推理的长时程任务中表现不佳。仅知道物理定律无法让 LLM 在复杂物理系统中完成查询或任务，因此需要一种能通过主动实验从经验中学习的新范式。

Method: 提出 HExA（Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents），一种层次化实验主义智能体框架，包含三个核心机制：(1) 迭代设计与优化与查询相关的实验；(2) 从实验经验中学习可组合、可复用的技能库；(3) 整合实验证据来回答查询或执行动作。该框架免训练、兼容任意黑箱模型、无需外部监督或离线数据。同时，基于 PHYRE 2D 过程物理环境构建了 Interphyre 工具调用基准，智能体可通过模拟 API 提出干预并测试假设。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.6 在 Interphyre 上仅获 2% 成功率，HExA 将同一模型提升至 77%。HExA 同样提升了开源权重模型的表现，并超越了 ReAct、Reflexion 等基线。仅使用从简单关卡学到的技能进行迁移（无主动实验）即达到 44% 成功率，验证了技能的复用性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 主动实验学习能够帮助智能体发现有用知识、习得可复用技能，并在全新的长时程任务上取得高效进展。HExA 框架展示了这一范式的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to take actions in the real world and support human decision-making, yet most agents rely on parametric knowledge, fixed post-training data, retrieval, or search. This paradigm breaks down in novel domains and for sophisticated queries that cannot be answered from prior knowledge alone. Knowing the laws of physics, for instance, does not by itself enable LLMs to answer queries or complete long-horizon tasks in a complex physical system. To address this, we introduce Hierarchical Experimentalist Agents (HExA), an in-context self-improvement framework to learn from active experimentation. HExA iteratively designs and refines query-relevant experiments, learns a reusable library of composable skills from experience, and integrates experimental evidence to answer queries or take actions. HExA is training-free, compatible with any black-box model, and does not require external supervision, oracles, or offline data. To evaluate active experimentation, we introduce Interphyre, a tool-calling benchmark built on the PHYRE 2D procedural physics environment, where agents propose interventions and test hypotheses through simulation APIs. Experiments show that current LLM agents struggle in these settings, especially on the hardest levels of Interphyre. Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 2% success, while HExA improves the same model to up to 77% success. HExA also improves open-weight models and outperforms agentic baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion. Moreover, using only skills learned from easier levels and transferred without active experimentation, HExA achieves 44% success, demonstrating the reusability and generalization of its learned skills. Overall, HExA shows that learning through active experimentation can help agents discover useful knowledge, acquire reusable skills, and make efficient progress on novel long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [184] [PHF: Privileged Hidden Flow for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29340)
*Yuhan Li,Mingxu Zhang,Dazhong Shen,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Privileged Hidden Flow (PHF)，在on-policy自蒸馏中额外蒸馏教师隐藏状态的 token 间转移方向与轨迹几何，避免逐点隐藏态对齐，在Qwen3系列模型上显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPSD仅监督输出分布，教师拥有的特权上下文（verified reference solutions）通过token级散度间接影响训练，缺乏对产生该分布的内部计算过程的直接监督。作者希望利用教师的隐藏状态流动信息，更直接地传递推理过程中的内部计算模式。

Method: PHF在OPSD基础上增加两个隐藏流蒸馏损失：(1) Token-to-token转移方向对齐——在选定的生成位置上，对齐学生与教师隐藏状态的前向转移方向（相邻时间步的差分向量）；(2) 轨迹几何保持——利用这些转移方向构造相邻层关系项，约束轨迹的整体几何结构。该方法具有共享轨迹偏移不变性和转移方向正交变换不变性，且避免逐点隐藏态匹配。

Result: 在相同100步训练设置下，PHF在Qwen3-1.7B、4B、8B上相比复现的OPSD基线，Average@12分别提升约+2.2、+1.5、+1.7个点。消融实验区分了PHF与逐点隐藏态匹配、单通道转移损失和层子集选择的差异。

Conclusion: PHF作为一种紧凑的隐藏流扩展，有效补充了OPSD，通过蒸馏转移方向和轨迹几何来传递教师内部计算过程的特权信息，实验验证了其在多规模模型上的一致增益，且设计具有良好的几何不变性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a reasoning model on rollouts sampled from its own policy by matching a privileged teacher that also sees verified reference solutions. Existing OPSD objectives supervise only the output distribution, so privileged context affects training through a token-level divergence without directly supervising the internal computation that produced that distribution. We propose Privileged Hidden Flow (PHF), which additionally distills how a privileged teacher's hidden states move along the same rollout. Rather than forcing each student hidden vector to match the teacher vector at the same token position, PHF aligns token-to-token transition directions and trajectory geometry over selected generated positions. The all-layer recipe also includes an adjacent-layer relation computed from these same transitions, without pointwise hidden-state imitation. Under the same 100-step training schedule, PHF improves the Average@12 aggregate over our reproduced OPSD baseline on Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 8B, with observed gains of about +2.2, +1.5, and +1.7 points. The transport objective is exactly invariant to shared trajectory offsets; its local geometry term is also invariant to orthogonal transformations of transition directions. Ablations distinguish the fixed PHF recipe from pointwise hidden-state matching, single-channel transition losses, and layer-subset choices, supporting PHF as a compact hidden-flow extension to OPSD.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Spectral Perturbation of the Empirical Fisher Information Matrix under Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28432)
*Rahid Zahid Alekberli,Hikmat Karimov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了经验Fisher信息矩阵(FIM)在两种结构扰动下的谱扰动：输入偏离参考分布集合，以及模型参数量化。作者证明偏离参考流形会提升FIM的最大特征值λ_max；核心结果是利用Weyl不等式给出了量化噪声下λ_max的方向性扰动界，并在温和条件下证明其严格超过未扰动值。基于此，作者提出将σ_t = λ_max(F_t)/λ_base作为部署语言模型的运行时监控统计量，并报告了12个模型、1080条轨迹的实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为部署中的语言模型设计运行时监控统计量，需要理解FIM在分布偏移和参数量化两种扰动下的谱行为。作者观察到4-bit量化模型上的校准阈值约为全精度估计的244倍，希望从理论上解释这一现象并形式化监控方法。

Method: 在局部曲率单调性假设下分析输入偏离对λ_max的影响(Proposition 3.2)；利用Weyl不等式推导量化噪声下λ_max的扰动下界，并在温和泛型条件下证明严格不等式(Theorem 4.3)；给出λ_max的两个可计算近似(一个启发式、一个带严格双边界)；构造基于阈值的增广状态空间划分的完备性结果。

Result: Proposition 3.2表明偏离参考流形可证明地提升λ_max；Theorem 4.3建立了量化噪声下λ_max的下界(至三阶余项)，并在泛型条件下证明其严格超过未扰动值；实验覆盖12个模型、1080条轨迹，结果与理论预测广泛一致，但量化膨胀幅度的闭式预测仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: σ_t = λ_max(F_t)/λ_base可作为部署语言模型的有效运行时监控统计量。量化扰动理论为4-bit模型上观测到的244倍阈值膨胀提供了机制性解释，但闭式预测该膨胀幅度仍待解决。

Abstract: We study the spectral perturbation of the empirical Fisher Information Matrix (FIM) of a parametric statistical model under two structured perturbations: departure of the input from a reference (in-distribution) ensemble, and finite-precision (quantized) perturbation of the model's parameters. For the first, under an explicit local curvature-monotonicity hypothesis on the dominant eigenvalue lambda_max of the FIM, we show departure from a reference manifold provably elevates lambda_max relative to a calibration baseline (Proposition 3.2), and discuss why this hypothesis is required, since curvature need not increase monotonically under every perturbation. Our principal result is a directional eigenvalue perturbation bound, via Weyl's inequality, showing lambda_max under a quantization noise perturbation is lower bounded by its unperturbed value up to a third-order remainder, and, under a mild genericity condition, strictly exceeds it at leading order (Theorem 4.3). We give two tractable approximations to lambda_max -- one heuristic, one with a rigorous two-sided bound -- and a completeness result for a threshold-based partition of an augmented state space. These results motivate using sigma_t = lambda_max(F_t)/lambda_base as a runtime monitoring statistic for deployed language models: the quantization result offers a mechanism for an empirical observation of our own, where a calibration threshold for this statistic was approximately 244 times larger than a preliminary full-precision estimate on a 4-bit quantized model, a single measurement rather than a value derived in closed form. We report supporting measurements (twelve models, n=1,080 trajectories) broadly consistent with our predictions, discuss the scope and limitations of every result, and state as an open problem the closed-form prediction of the quantization inflation magnitude our bound does not supply.

</details>


### [186] [Adaptive Iterative Hard Thresholding for Online High-dimensional Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28652)
*Zitian Zhou,Nan Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AIHT 是一种在线稀疏回归框架，通过自适应调度硬阈值步骤与随机次梯度更新交替执行，将支持集发现与局部精化分离，在高维在线分位数回归中实现对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线高维回归需要既能序贯更新又能保持结构稀疏性的算法。现有方法难以在非光滑损失（如分位数回归）和异质性/重尾噪声下有效平衡支持集发现与局部收敛。

Method: 提出 AIHT 框架：早期延迟阈值操作，让弱信号坐标积累信息；后期提高投影频率以稳定稀疏估计并利用局部曲率。理论分析针对高维在线分位数回归，在受限曲率和梯度泄漏条件下证明算法保持在膨胀稀疏锥内，呈现两阶段收敛行为。

Result: 在受限曲率和梯度泄漏条件下，AIHT 保持在膨胀稀疏锥内，呈现两阶段收敛行为，并在滑动窗口目标上实现对数遗憾。仿真实验（含阈值调度消融实验）验证了该机制并展示了其相对于标准在线稀疏学习基线的优势。

Conclusion: AIHT 通过自适应阈值调度有效解决了在线高维稀疏回归中支持集发现与局部精化的权衡问题，尤其在非光滑、异质性场景下表现优越。

Abstract: Online high-dimensional regression requires algorithms that can update sequentially while preserving structural sparsity. We propose \textit{Adaptive Iterative Hard Thresholding (AIHT)}, an online sparse-regression framework that alternates stochastic subgradient updates with adaptively scheduled hard-thresholding steps. The key idea is to separate support discovery from local refinement: early in the learning process, AIHT delays thresholding so that weak but informative coordinates have time to accumulate signal, while later it increases the projection frequency to stabilize the sparse estimator and exploit local curvature. We develop the theory for high-dimensional online quantile regression, a challenging setting in which the loss is nonsmooth and the data may exhibit heterogeneity or heavy-tailed noise. Under restricted curvature and gradient-leakage conditions, AIHT remains in an inflated sparse cone, exhibits a two-phase convergence behavior, and attains logarithmic regret for the sliding-window objective. Simulations for online quantile regression, together with threshold-scheduling ablations, support the proposed mechanism and illustrate its advantage over standard online sparse-learning baselines.

</details>


### [187] [Variance Reduction for Stochastic Gradient Generalized Non-reversible Langevin Monte Carlo Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28808)
*Bingye Ni,Xiaoyu Wang,Yingli Wang,Lingjiong Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了非可逆Langevin动力学下随机梯度Euler-Maruyama估计量的前导阶波动，在小步长中心极限定理框架下证明了经验平均满足中心极限定理，并证明反对称扰动可严格减小前导阶波动常数，数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非可逆Langevin动力学中随机梯度方法的渐近波动行为，旨在从理论上理解反对称扰动是否能系统性地减小估计量的渐近方差，从而为非可逆采样方案提供严格的统计效率保证。

Method: 在小步长中心极限定理的结构假设和无偏随机梯度谕示下，证明经验平均在消失步长机制下满足中心极限定理；通过Poisson方程刻画极限方差；利用算子形式将方差常数与连续时间渐近方差关联；导出反对称扰动严格减小前导阶波动的充分条件；对二次哈密顿量和线性观测量给出闭式公式。

Result: 理论证明非可逆方案的前导阶波动常数严格小于可逆基线；框架覆盖非可逆Langevin动力学和增广状态示例（包括Hessian-free高分辨率动力学和梯度调整欠阻尼Langevin动力学）；数值实验在合成数据的贝叶斯线性回归和真实数据的贝叶斯逻辑回归上均显示非可逆方案一致降低RMSE。

Conclusion: 非可逆Langevin动力学中的反对称扰动能在小步长渐近意义下严格减小随机梯度估计量的前导阶波动，这一理论优势在多个实际贝叶斯推断任务中得到数值验证。

Abstract: We study the leading-order fluctuation of stochastic gradient Euler-Maruyama estimators for generalized non-reversible Langevin dynamics. Under structural assumptions tailored to the small-stepsize central limit theorem and under an unbiased stochastic gradient oracle, we prove that the empirical average over a horizon of order the inverse squared stepsize satisfies a central limit theorem in the vanishing-stepsize regime. The limiting variance is characterized through the Poisson equation of the limiting full-gradient diffusion. We then rewrite this constant in an operator form that links it to the continuous-time asymptotic variance and, under standard operator-theoretic assumptions, derive a sufficient condition under which an anti-symmetric perturbation strictly reduces the leading-order fluctuation constant relative to the reversible baseline. We also identify bounded smooth predictive observables that re directly covered by the main theorem. As a separate Gaussian calculation beyond the bounded-test-function regime, we obtain closed-form formulas for quadratic Hamiltonians and linear observables. The framework covers non-reversible Langevin dynamics and augmented-state examples including Hessian-free high-resolution dynamics and a positive-definite subclass of gradient-adjusted underdamped Langevin dynamics that allow stochastic gradients. Numerical experiments on basic examples and Bayesian linear regression using synthetic data, and Bayesian logistic regression using real data support the predicted Gaussian fluctuations and show that the non-reversible schemes consistently reduce the root mean squared error (RMSE) relative to their reversible baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Perspectives on Latent Factor Indeterminacy and its Implications for Data Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28854)
*Carel F. W. Peeters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了因子分析模型中潜在因子投影的固有不定性问题，揭示其与变分自编码器中潜在变量坍缩的深层联系，并证明当特征维度趋于无穷时，潜在因子可实现完全确定性——这一涌现特性使得因子模型特别适用于高维数据的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子分析模型作为生成式表示学习的基础模型，其潜在因子投影存在根本性的不定性问题——即使已知潜在维度、满足正则条件且解决了旋转不定性，因果潜在源的检索仍然是不确定、分布偏离和非唯一的。这一问题在心理测量学中经典但常被忽视，且与现代深度生成模型中VAE的潜在变量坍缩问题密切相关，亟需系统性的跨学科审视。

Method: 作者从多角度理论评估了潜在因子不定性：首先厘清因子模型与Helmholtz机、Boltzmann机、线性自编码器及单隐层生成神经网络的等价关系；其次系统分析不定性的多个面向（不确定性、分布偏离性、非唯一性）；然后建立经典心理测量学不定性问题与现代VAE潜在坍缩之间的数学与概念关联；最后通过渐近分析证明特征维度趋于无穷时因子确定性的涌现。

Result: 当特征维度增长至无穷时，潜在因子在所有方面均达到确定性，这一结果催生了一种在大特征数样本情形下本质上无分布的估计方法。这些确定性属性是大规模条件下的涌现特性。

Conclusion: 由于潜在因子确定性是规模涌现属性，因子模型天然适用于超高维数据的表示学习，这一发现对心理测量学、统计学和人工智能社区均有重要启示。

Abstract: The common factor analytic model is related to Helmholtz and Boltzmann machines, can be conceived as a linear autoencoder, or can be thought of as a single-hidden-layer generative neural network. We thus consider it a basal generative representation learner that can be used as a minimal model for studying the foundational characteristics of (deep) generative model architectures. We focus on the fundamental problem of indeterminacy in latent factor projections. This indeterminacy implies that, even when the intrinsic dimension of the latent vector is known, regularity conditions are met, and rotational indeterminacy is resolved, an inherent indefiniteness in the retrieval of causative latent sources remains: they will be uncertain, distributionally deviant, and non-unique. This can have major implications for data representation but remains an elusive issue, even to practitioners and theorists well-versed in the factor model. Moreover, this classic psychometric problem is intricately related to the modern issue of latent variable collapse in the variational autoencoder framework for deep generative modeling. Here, we assess this indeterminacy from various perspectives and show how these are mathematically and conceptually related and we discuss subsequent implications for the Psychometrics, Statistics, and Artificial Intelligence communities. We show that one has latent factor determinacy across all its facets when the feature-dimension grows to infinity. This feeds into an essentially distribution-free estimation approach in the sample case when the number of features grows very large. We conclude, as these are emergent properties at scale, that the factor model is suited for representation learning of very-high-dimensional data.

</details>


### [189] [A Bayesian latent Gaussian process framework for aerodynamic uncertainty quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28871)
*Geoffrey Davis,Ashwin Renganathan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高斯过程代理模型与Kennedy-O'Hagan校准框架的方法，用于融合大量低保真计算数据与稀疏、含噪声的实验测量数据。核心创新在于开发了贝叶斯潜高斯过程方法，可边缘化输入不确定性并匹配输出不确定性的边缘均值与方差，从而实现对气动系数的高精度不确定性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞行器气动性能（升力、阻力、力矩系数）预测面临两难：计算模型存在偏差，直接高保真模拟计算成本过高。虽然可用实验测量校准低保真计算预测，但测量数据往往稀疏且同时受控制输入和响应输出两端不确定性的污染。现有方法难以有效处理这种双重不确定性下的校准问题。

Method: 作者构建了基于高斯过程（GP）代理模型与Kennedy-O'Hagan校准框架的方法论：(1) 在丰富且廉价的低保真数据上训练GP代理模型；(2) 用稀疏测量数据对其进行校准；(3) 关键创新在于开发了贝叶斯潜高斯过程方法，对输入不确定性进行边缘化处理，同时匹配输出测量不确定性的边缘均值与方差，使校准后的代理模型能准确量化预测不确定性。

Result: 校准后的代理模型在包括外推输入条件下均能以极高精度预测气动系数的不确定性。验证结果表明，模型将94.2%-95.8%的预测样本置于公布的95%真值区间内，端点累积概率非常接近标称的0.025和0.975水平，证明其不确定性量化高度可靠。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功解决了在输入与输出双重不确定性下，利用稀疏实验数据校准低保真计算模型的问题。校准后的GP代理模型不仅在插值区域表现优异，在外推条件下同样具有良好校准性，为飞行器气动性能预测提供了一种实用且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting the aerodynamic performance (e.g. lift, drag, and moment coefficients) of an aircraft is challenging -- computational models are biased and direct simulations are prohibitive. A pragmatic way to overcome this limitation is by calibrating low-fidelity computational predictions with experimental measurements. This, however, requires calibrating against \emph{sparse} measurements contaminated with \emph{uncertainty} in both the control inputs and the measured aerodynamic response. We develop a methodology to address this problem based on Gaussian process surrogates and the classical Kennedy-O'Hagan calibration. A surrogate model learned on abundant-but-cheap low-fidelity data is calibrated with a sparse set of measurement data. Crucialy, we develop a Bayesian latent Gaussian process based approach that marginalizes the calibrated surrogate model over the input uncertainty, while also matching the marginal mean and variance of the measured output uncertainty. Once calibrated, our surrogate model predicts the uncertainty in aerodynamic coefficients with very high accuracy, including at extrapolative input settings. We validate our calibrated surrogate model predictions against measurement data with \emph{true} uncertainty intervals to demonstrate that the model places $94.2-95.8\%$ of its predictive samples inside the released $95\%$ truth intervals, with endpoint cumulative probabilities very close to the nominal 0.025 and 0.975 levels.

</details>


### [190] [Connectivity Estimation using Stochastic Graph Heat Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29098)
*Stephan Goerttler,Min Wu,Fei He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的噪声驱动热建模框架，用于神经生理连接性估计。作者放宽了早期噪声假设并引入正则化以提升鲁棒性，开发了仿真评估流程，并在两个实验、四个真实数据集上验证了该方法捕捉空间结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多技术利用数据集的空间结构，但空间结构的估计及其在这些技术中的作用常被忽视。在神经生理数据分析中，现有脑连接估计方法大多缺乏显式模型基础，且很少同时具备动态性、多变量性和有向性。为此，作者旨在改进此前提出的热建模框架，使其更鲁棒、更灵活。

Method: 作者扩展了先前提出的图上噪声驱动热建模框架：(1) 放宽早期对噪声的假设限制；(2) 引入正则化技术以增强估计的鲁棒性；(3) 设计仿真流程，在受控环境中系统评估方法性能；(4) 在两个实验中使用四个真实数据集验证方法的有效性。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法在受控环境下表现良好；在两个实验、四个真实数据集上的应用证明，该方法能够有效捕捉有意义的空间连接结构。

Conclusion: 该连接估计器的显式模型公式有望提升基于图的技术在广泛应用中的可解释性。代码已开源。

Abstract: A growing number of techniques leverage the spatial structures that underlie many real-world datasets. Despite these advances, the complementary task of estimating spatial structures and understanding their role within these techniques has often been overlooked. In neurophysiological data analysis specifically, numerous methods exist to estimate brain connectivity, but most are not explicitly model-based, dynamic, multivariate, or directed. To address these limitations, we previously introduced noise-driven heat modelling on graphs for neurophysiological connectivity estimation. In this study, we extend this framework by relaxing earlier noise assumptions and adding regularisation to improve robustness. We also develop a simulation procedure to characterise and evaluate our technique in a controlled setting. Finally, we demonstrate that the technique is able to capture meaningful spatial structure across two experiments, each using two real-world datasets. The explicit model formulation of our connectivity estimator has the potential to improve the interpretability of graph-based techniques across a wide range of applications. The code implementing our method is available at https://github.com/sgoerttler/Heat_Connectivity.

</details>


### [191] [Generalization Analysis of Transformers in Distribution Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29256)
*Peilin Liu,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于分布回归的Transformer学习框架,将注意力机制严格数学化为"注意力算子",证明Transformer能够无损地将分布压缩为函数表示,并在学习复杂泛函方面优于CNN和全连接网络。基于该框架,作者为提示调优、参数高效微调、高效缩放等LLM技术提供了理论洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Transformer架构及其相关成功技术(如参数高效微调、高效缩放等)缺乏严格的数学理论基础,其背后的工作机制尚不明晰。作者旨在建立一个严谨的数学框架来揭示Transformer及其相关技术的底层机制。

Method: 作者以分布回归为动机,提出了一个Transformer学习框架:(1)将输入建模为分布,并构建一个两阶段采样过程与自然语言处理相联系;(2)提出"注意力算子"这一数学形式化工具来描述注意力机制;(3)在该框架下推导泛化界;(4)利用该理论框架分析LLM中的提示调优、参数高效微调和高效缩放等技术。

Result: (1)通过注意力算子,Transformer能够将分布无损压缩为函数表示;(2)相比CNN和全连接网络,Transformer在学习结构更复杂的泛函方面具有更强的能力;(3)在分布回归框架内得到了泛化界;(4)该框架为提示调优、参数高效微调和高效缩放等LLM技术提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 该工作通过分布回归视角为Transformer建立了严谨的数学理论框架,不仅揭示了注意力机制的本质——将分布无损压缩为函数表示,还解释了Transformer优于其他架构的原因,并为LLM中的多项经验性技术提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: In recent years, models based on the Transformer architecture have seen widespread applications and have become one of the core tools in the field of deep learning. Numerous successful techniques, such as parameter-efficient fine-tuning and efficient scaling, have been proposed surrounding their applications to further enhance performance. However, the success of these strategies has always lacked the support of rigorous mathematical theory. To study the underlying mechanisms behind Transformers and related techniques, we first propose a Transformer learning framework motivated by distribution regression, with distributions being inputs, connect a two-stage sampling process with natural language processing, and present a mathematical formulation of the attention mechanism called attention operator. We demonstrate that by the attention operator, Transformers can compress distributions into function representations without loss of information. Moreover, with the advantages of our novel attention operator, Transformers exhibit a stronger capability to learn functionals with more complex structures than convolutional neural networks and fully connected networks. Finally, we obtain a generalization bound within the distribution regression framework. Through the aforementioned theoretical results, we further discuss some successful techniques emerging with large language models (LLMs), such as prompt tuning, parameter-efficient fine-tuning, and efficient scaling. We also provide theoretical insights behind these techniques within our novel analysis framework.

</details>


### [192] [Gradient boosting with vector-valued leafs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29326)
*David Cortes*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将梯度提升框架从标量目标函数扩展到向量输入的目标函数，并提出一种可与基于直方图的决策树高效配合的简单算法，克服了现有框架逐元素更新或使用对角上界近似的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的梯度提升框架在处理向量目标函数（如多类分类中的多项逻辑对数似然）时，要么逐元素更新输入向量，要么使用二阶导数的对角上界近似，这两种方法均非最优。作者希望将梯度提升自然地扩展到向量值目标函数上。

Method: 将标准梯度提升框架扩展以支持向量输入函数，并设计了一个可与基于直方图的决策树高效协同使用的简单算法，利用向量化的梯度和二阶导数信息进行联合优化。

Result: 该方法能够以更自然和高效的方式处理多类分类等向量目标函数问题，且与基于直方图的决策树实现良好兼容。

Conclusion: 向量输入的梯度提升扩展是可行的，所提算法简洁高效，为多类分类等需要向量评分的问题提供了优于传统逐元素更新和对角近似方法的解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient boosting in the form of decision tree ensembles has successfully been applied to a variety of problems using simple objective functions based on log-likelihoods of a single variable. The concept extends naturally to objective functions operating on vectors - for example, multinomial logistic log-likelihood for multi-class classification, where observations have a score for each class - but popular frameworks approach these functions by either updating one value of the input vectors at a time, or by using a diagonal upper bound on the second derivative. This work extends the usual gradient boosting framework to functions of vector inputs and sketches a simple algorithm that can be used efficiently with histogram-based decision trees.

</details>


### [193] [Self-Organized Conformal Prediction: Reducing Regional Coverage Gaps with Unsupervised Group Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29403)
*Louis Berthier,Ahmed Shokry,Maxime Moreaud,Guillaume Ramelet,Aymeric Dieuleveut*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SOCP 利用自组织映射（SOM）在输入空间中发现自然分组，为测试点从其最佳匹配单元或邻域提取局部校准缓冲，从而在不改变预测器或非一致性评分的前提下，提供分组级别的共形预测保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的共形预测通过池化校准（pooled calibration）只能保证全局边际覆盖，容易在安全关键的亚群体中出现区域性欠覆盖。现有方法通常依赖监督式的预定义分组或需要重新训练预测器，缺乏一种能够直接从输入几何结构中发现异质性分组的无监督校准方案。

Method: 提出 SOCP：使用自组织映射（SOM）对校准数据进行无监督聚类以发现输入空间分组；测试时，根据查询点的最佳匹配单元（BMU）或固定大小的网格邻域提取局部校准集。BMU 检索提供精确有效性保证，邻域检索提供固定检索集有效性，中心单元有效性附带一个 KS 偏差项的近似保证。进一步提出分流路由（split-routed）扩展，在路由分流条件下恢复固定检索集有效性。方法兼容回归和分类任务、表格数据与图像嵌入。

Result: 在 8 个回归与分类基准上，SO-SCP 在 7/8 数据集上降低了加权区域覆盖差距（平均配对变化 -7.1%），预测集大小平均增加 6.2%，在最大的 6 个数据集上额外开销可忽略；SO-CQR 收益较小，因为分位数回归已吸收了大部分异质性。

Conclusion: SOCP 能够直接从输入几何中学习分组，提供精确的固定分组保证和近似的中心单元保证，无需监督式分区或预测器重新训练，是一种有效的局部校准共形预测方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction guarantees marginal coverage, but pooled calibration averages over heterogeneous regions and can mask regional undercoverage in safety-critical subgroups. We introduce Self-Organized Conformal Prediction (SOCP), a calibration scheme that discovers input-space groups with a Self-Organizing Map (SOM) and, at test time, draws a local calibration buffer from the query's best-matching unit (BMU) cell or a fixed grid neighborhood. The same retrieval rule applies to regression and classification tasks across tabular features and image embeddings, leaving the predictor and nonconformity score untouched. SOCP gives exact validity for BMU-cell retrieval and fixed retrieved-set validity for neighborhood buffers; central-cell validity for neighborhood retrieval holds up to a Kolmogorov-Smirnov (KS) bias term. A split-routed extension recovers fixed retrieved-set validity conditional on the routing split. On eight regression and classification benchmarks, SO-SCP reduces the weighted regional coverage gap on $7/8$ datasets (mean paired change $-7.1\%$) for a mean prediction-set size increase of $6.2\%$, with negligible overhead on the largest six datasets; SO-CQR yields smaller gains, since quantile regression already absorbs much of the heterogeneity. By learning groups directly from the input geometry, SOCP provides group-local calibration with exact fixed-group guarantees and approximate central-cell guarantees, without supervised partitions or predictor retraining.

</details>


### [194] [Bidirectional Autoregressive Latent Diffusion for Forward and Inverse Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29620)
*Alexander Scheinker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种双向自回归潜在扩散模型，用于预测磁流体力学（MHD）多物理场演化，利用双向流动的一致性作为自监督度量进行无真值的测试时不确定性与误差估计，并展示了其在等离子体诊断和自适应反馈中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁流体力学多场演化预测面临高维复杂动力学挑战，传统方法难以量化模型在测试时的不确定性——尤其是在无法获取真实值的情况下。需要一个既能准确预测、又能自我评估预测可靠性的框架。

Method: 提出双向自回归潜在扩散模型：在潜在空间中沿时间正向和反向进行自回归扩散预测；以"从任意时刻出发，先正向再反向（或反之）流动后能否回到相同预测场"作为自监督一致性度量，从而在无真实值时估计逐点不确定性与误差。同时引入自适应反馈机制，利用稀疏诊断或有限观测视图增强模型鲁棒性。

Result: 所提方法能够在无需真实值的情况下有效估计测试时的不确定性和预测误差；双向一致性度量与真实误差之间展现出良好相关性；展示了作为非侵入式等离子体诊断工具的潜力；自适应反馈机制能基于稀疏测量提升模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双向自回归潜在扩散模型为MHD多场预测提供了一种自带不确定性量化的解决方案，其双向一致性自监督度量可在缺乏真值的实际部署场景中实现可靠的误差估计，并有望拓展为实用的等离子体诊断与自适应控制工具。

Abstract: This work presents a new bidirectional autoregressive latent diffusion approach for predicting the evolution of multiple fields (mass density, pressure, velocity, and magnetic field components) for magnetohydrodynamics. We show that this bidirectional flow can be used as a self-supervised consistency metric for uncertainty and error estimation, which enables the model to estimate test-time uncertainty and error without access to ground truth, by comparing how closely flowing forwards and backwards in time returns to the same predicted fields. We also demonstrate this methods's potential to serve as a non-invasive plasma diagnostic, and show how adaptive feedback can be used to make the model more robust based on sparse diagnostics or limited views/measurements.

</details>


### [195] [Adjusted Wasserstein distances for bridging empirical and true distributions with applications to MDS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29665)
*Flor Martinez-Sermeno,Arturo Jaramillo,Johan Van Horebeek*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了对多维缩放（MDS）中使用的距离度量进行改进，提出Max-D-SW距离，通过正交基聚合替代单方向优化，在处理重尾分布时具有数值优势，并给出了样本复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Max-Sliced Wasserstein距离仅优化单一单位方向，限制了其在MDS可视化中的模式识别效果，尤其对重尾分布表现不佳，因此需要一种改进的度量来提升MDS的视觉分析能力。

Method: 提出Max-D-SW距离，在正交基上聚合多个方向的贡献，而非仅在单个方向优化；同时推导了该度量的样本复杂度界，并分析了度量作为MDS输入时复杂度与性能之间的关系。

Result: Max-D-SW在MDS应用中表现出明显的数值优势，尤其适用于重尾分布；样本复杂度界与原始Max-Sliced Wasserstein相当，保持统计可处理性；此外发现更好的样本复杂度并不必然转化为更好的MDS性能。

Conclusion: Max-D-SW通过正交基聚合显著提升了MDS在重尾分布下的模式识别可视化效果，且保持优良的统计性质；样本复杂度与MDS实际性能之间存在脱节，提示评估度量质量需结合具体应用场景。

Abstract: This paper examines how metric adjustments to Multidimensional Scaling (MDS) can enhance its effectiveness as a visual tool for pattern recognition. The distance under consideration, referred to as Max-D-SW, is an adjustment of the Max-Sliced Wasserstein distance. In contrast to the original formulation, which optimizes over single unit directions, Max-D-SW aggregates contributions over orthonormal bases. This modification provides a clear numerical advantage in MDS outcomes, particularly when applied to heavy-tailed distributions. We also establish sample-complexity bounds showing that Max-D-SW remains statistically tractable, with rates comparable to those of its max-sliced counterpart. Moreover, we show that a better sample complexity for a metric does not necessarily translate into better performance when the metric is used as an input for MDS.

</details>


### [196] [Notes on generative modeling: flow matching, diffusion, optimal transport and Schr{ö}dinger bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30053)
*Titouan Vayer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文概述了生成建模中不同技术背后的高层次数学原理，并展示了最优传输与薛定谔桥、流匹配等标准技术之间的内在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模领域存在多种技术（如最优传输、薛定谔桥、流匹配等），它们看似独立但实际上共享深层数学结构。作者旨在厘清这些方法之间的理论关联，为研究者提供统一的数学视角。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性笔记，采用高层次数学推导的方式，从最优传输理论出发，逐步建立其与薛定谔桥（Schrödinger bridge）问题和流匹配（flow matching）方法的联系，揭示它们在概率测度演化框架下的统一性。

Result: 文章阐明了最优传输与薛定谔桥之间的桥梁关系：薛定谔桥可视为引入熵正则化后的最优传输问题的随机版本。同时，流匹配方法被证明与连续归一化流及最优传输中的速度场构造密切相关，统一于相同的数学框架之下。

Conclusion: 不同生成建模技术并非孤立存在，它们在最优传输理论的统一框架下具有深刻的内在联系。理解这些数学联系有助于推动生成模型的理论发展和算法设计。

Abstract: These notes recapitulate the high level mathematical principles behind different techniques for generative modeling. I show the connections between optimal transport and standard techniques such as Schr{ö}dinger bridge and flow matching.

</details>


### [197] [Highly Data Parallelizable Estimation of the Sliced-Wasserstein Distance Using Cumulative Distribution Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30310)
*Christophe Vauthier,Quentin Mérigot,Anna Korba*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于累积分布函数（CDF）而非分位数函数的Sliced Wasserstein距离新估计量，避免了排序操作，支持大规模并行计算，且天然适配联邦学习场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SW距离估计依赖蒙特卡洛平均和一维Wasserstein距离计算，需要排序投影样本并访问完整数据集，计算成本高且不适用于数据隐私受限的联邦学习场景。作者旨在设计无需排序、可并行化、且CDF比分位数函数更易获取的替代估计量。

Method: 提出基于投影测度CDF的SW距离估计量族。利用CDF直接计算一维Wasserstein距离，避免排序操作；部分估计量引入超参数控制方差或平滑性；通过CDF的可加性支持数据的局部分布聚合，无需交换原始样本，实现联邦学习兼容。

Result: 新估计量在混合高斯等CDF解析可得的场景中表现优异，具有良好的并行扩展性（支持大规模数据集并行），且在联邦学习框架下可本地计算并聚合投影数据的CDF，无需共享原始数据。

Conclusion: 基于CDF的SW距离估计量在计算效率、并行性和隐私保护方面相比传统基于分位数的方法具有显著优势，为大规模分布式场景下的最优传输距离计算提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: The Sliced Wasserstein (SW) distance has emerged as a computationally attractive alternative to the Wasserstein distance by leveraging one-dimensional optimal transport along random projections. Standard estimators of the SW distance rely on Monte Carlo averages of one-dimensional Wasserstein distances computed via quantile functions, which require sorting projected samples and access to full datasets. In this work, we introduce a new class of estimators for the Sliced Wasserstein distance based on cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) of projected measures, that avoid sorting and scale via massive dataset parallelism. This class includes several estimators, some of them being indexed by hyperparameters controlling their variance or smoothness. We show that they are especially well suited to scenarios in which CDFs are more tractable than quantile functions, such as mixtures of Gaussians, and moreover that they are also naturally compatible with federated learning, since CDFs of projected data can be computed and aggregated locally without requiring the exchange of raw samples.

</details>


### [198] [Extrapolating from Regularised Solutions for Solving Ill-Conditioned Linear Systems in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30328)
*Disha Hegde,Jon Cockayne,Chris. J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 autonugget，一个基于 JAX 的 Python 软件包，利用 Richardson 外推组合多次线性求解，自动且稳定地解决病态线性方程组，避免了传统 Tikhonov 正则化中 nugget 选择困难及自动微分不兼容的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习算法快速原型开发中，病态线性方程组的数值求解通常依赖 Tikhonov 正则化（引入 nugget 参数）。然而，nugget 的大小选择困难，且数据自适应选择过程会破坏自动微分的兼容性，导致端到端训练不稳定。此外，数据自适应方法执行多次线性求解却仅返回一次结果，存在计算浪费。

Method: 提出 autonugget 软件包，核心思想是用 Richardson 外推（Richardson extrapolation）将多次不同 nugget 参数下的线性求解结果进行组合，以外推得到病态系统在零 nugget 极限下的精确解，而非仅依赖单次 nugget 求解。该方法完全兼容 JAX 的自动微分框架。

Result: autonugget 能够自动、稳定地求解病态线性系统，相比基于单一 nugget 的近似方法具有更高的精度，且保持与 JAX 自动微分的完全兼容性，适合快速原型开发场景。

Conclusion: autonugget 通过 Richardson 外推有效规避了传统 nugget 选择的难题，既提升了数值精度，又保证了端到端可微性，为机器学习中需要求解病态线性系统的快速原型开发提供了一种实用工具。

Abstract: Rapid prototyping of algorithms is a critical step in modern machine learning. Most algorithms exploit linear algebra, creating a need for lightweight numerical routines which -- while potentially sub-optimal for the task at hand -- can be rapidly implemented. For the numerical solution of ill-conditioned linear systems of equations, the standard solution for prototyping is Tikhonov-regularised inversion using a nugget. However, selection of the size of nugget is often difficult, and the use of data-adaptive procedures precludes automatic differentiation, introducing instabilities into end-to-end training. Further, while data-adaptive procedures perform multiple linear solves to select the size of nugget, only the result of one such solve is returned, which we argue is wasteful. This paper aims to circumvent the above difficulties, presenting autonugget; a Python package for automatic and stable numerical solution of linear systems suitable for rapid prototyping, and fully compatible with automatic differentiation using JAX. autonugget combines multiple linear solves using Richardson extrapolation to determine the solution of the ill-conditioned system, improving in accuracy over approximations based on a single nugget.

</details>


### [199] [A Stochastic--Geometric Theory of Scaling Laws in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30388)
*Róisín Luo,Christian Gagné,Jonas Ngnawé,Ihsan Ullah,Karyn Morrissey*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从理论层面揭示了"延迟泛化"(grokking)现象的机制：Adam优化器结合权重收缩正则化会在解空间中诱导出"壳-核"拓扑结构——随机初始化位于外层球壳，记忆解构成中间球壳，泛化解位于核心。作者利用停时理论分析了优化轨迹从记忆流形逃逸并首次到达泛化边界的时间，推导出关于学习率、批量大小和L2正则化系数的grokking缩放律，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管grokking现象（网络先拟合训练数据、经过长时间延迟后才突然泛化）已被大量实证研究，但其底层理论机制仍不清楚。本文旨在从优化动力学和拓扑结构的视角，为grokking提供一个严格的理论解释。

Method: 首先，基于Adam优化动力学与权重收缩正则化，理论刻画并实证验证了可达解空间的"壳-核"拓扑构型（外层初始化壳→中间记忆壳→内核泛化壳）。随后，运用停时(stopping-time)理论分析该拓扑构型的几何性质，计算优化轨迹从记忆流形逃逸并首次抵达泛化流形边界所需的转移时间。

Result: 理论分析成功推导出grokking关于学习率、批量大小和ℓ₂正则化系数的缩放律(scaling laws)，这些缩放律不仅通过实验得到验证，还能复现先前文献中的已有结果。

Conclusion: Grokking源于Adam优化与权重收缩正则化在参数空间中诱导的壳-核拓扑结构；泛化延迟由优化轨迹穿越该拓扑构型所需的逃逸时间决定；grokking缩放律可通过理论严格推导并经验验证。

Abstract: Delayed generalization (\ie~grokking) refers to the phenomenon in which a neural network fits its training data early in training but only begins to generalize after a prolonged delay, often through an abrupt transition. Despite extensive empirical study, its underlying mechanism remains poorly understood. In this work, we first theoretically characterize a shell--core topological configuration of the reachable solution space induced by Adam's optimization dynamics with weight-shrinkage regularization, supported by empirical evidence. This optimization-induced topological configuration gives rise to grokking. In model's parameter space, random initialization solutions concentrate on a thin outer spherical shell, enclosing another spherical shell of memorization solutions, which in turn contains a core corresponding to the generalization solutions. Leveraging stopping-time theory, we then analyze the geometry of this topological configuration and the solution transition time at which optimization trajectories escape the memorization manifold and first reach the boundary of the generalization manifold. Our theoretical analysis derives grokking scaling laws for the learning rate, batch size, and $\ell_2$ regularization coefficient, which are further validated through experiments and shown to recover results from prior literature.

</details>


### [200] [SGD Provably Prioritizes a Shortcut Spurious Feature in the XOR Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30444)
*Tyler LaBonte,Vidya Muthukumar*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次对两层ReLU网络在在线mini-batch SGD下利用虚假（spurious）特征学习XOR信号函数的完整过程进行了理论刻画，揭示了SGD以指数速度优先学习虚假特征、且强虚假分量会抑制信号特征学习的耦合动力学机制，并精确描述了其中的相变现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络容易依赖虚假相关性，但其利用捷径特征（shortcut features）的精确机制尚不明确，现有的缓解算法所依赖的关于学习表征的假设也缺乏理论支撑。因此需要对虚假特征学习进行端到端的理论分析。

Method: 在由布尔超立方体采样的高维数据上建模，信号函数为二次XOR结构，虚假相关为线性。分析两阶段相变：(1) 虚假特征符号与第二层权重的对齐驱动虚假特征快速增长；(2) 大类间隔（majority group margin）增大导致学习放缓，信号特征持续被抑制。进一步分析虚假相关最大强度时与无虚假特征情况下XOR样本复杂度阈值的对比。

Result: SGD以指数速度优先学习虚假特征；优化动力学将虚假特征与信号特征耦合，强虚假分量抑制信号学习；学习过程存在精确相变；当虚假相关性极强时，即使达到孤立情况下XOR可被学习的样本复杂度阈值，虚假特征仍占主导；当相关强度为常数时，初步实验表明模型最终可学到XOR信号，但虚假特征不会被遗忘。

Conclusion: 首次给出了虚假特征学习的端到端理论表征，揭示了SGD优先且指数级快速学习虚假特征、并通过耦合动力学抑制信号学习的机制，为理解和缓解捷径学习提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Neural networks are known to be susceptible to over-reliance on spurious correlations. However, the precise mechanism by which models exploit shortcut features is not fully understood, and algorithms to mitigate this behavior rely on as yet unjustified assumptions about the learned representations. In this work, we provide the first end-to-end theoretical characterization of spurious feature learning for two-layer ReLU neural networks trained by online minibatch SGD on the logistic loss. We consider data drawn from the high-dimensional Boolean hypercube with a quadratic signal function (namely XOR) and a linear spurious correlation. We show that SGD learns the spurious feature first, and exponentially fast. Moreover, the optimization dynamics couple the spurious and signal features, with a stronger spurious component inhibiting signal feature learning. Our analysis reveals precise phase transitions in the learning dynamics. In the first phase, alignment between the signs of the spurious feature and second-layer weight drives rapid growth of the spurious feature. In the second phase, large majority group margin slows learning and the signal feature remains suppressed. When the spurious correlation is maximally strong, we show theoretically that the spurious feature dominates even at the sample complexity threshold where XOR would be learned in isolation (i.e., if the spurious feature was absent). In contrast, when the correlation strength is constant, we provide preliminary empirical evidence that the model can eventually learn the XOR signal, although the spurious feature is not forgotten.

</details>


### [201] [Non-parametric recovery of causal diffusion mechanisms from steady-state observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30467)
*Richard Schwank,Mathias Drton*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究从截面观测数据中恢复连续时间稀疏多变量随机系统的因果转移机制，证明了在均衡分布下漂移函数的非参数可识别性，提出了核估计器及其一致性证明，并通过交叉验证进行超参数调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受基因表达分析等应用场景驱动，其中破坏性实验技术只能记录细胞生命周期中的一次数据。传统方法需要时间序列数据来推断动态系统的因果机制，但在许多实际场景中只能获得截面数据（即系统已达稳态均衡时的单次快照），需要在仅有稳态分布观测的条件下还原系统的动力学转移机制。

Method: 假设系统服从时间齐次扩散过程且在观测时已达均衡分布，因果机制由扩散漂移函数完整描述且具有无环结构（因果图已知）。在此框架下：1）在无非爆炸（non-explosion）弱条件下证明漂移函数的非参数可识别性；2）构建非参数核估计器以求解该反问题；3）证明估计器的一致性；4）提出交叉验证方案用于超参数选择。

Result: 理论上证明了在无非爆炸条件下，仅凭截面数据即可非参数识别完整的因果漂移函数；所提出的核估计器具有一致性；模拟实验验证了估计器的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了从稳态截面数据反演动态因果机制的理论可行性，弥合了因果推断与扩散过程之间的方法论鸿沟，并与不可逆生成扩散模型及低频采样数据建立了联系，为基因表达分析等仅能获取截面数据的场景提供了新的统计推断工具。

Abstract: We consider sparse multivariate stochastic systems that evolve in continuous time according to a causal mechanism and present methodology to recover the system's time-infinitesimal transition mechanism from mere cross-sectional data. This observational paradigm is motivated by applications such as gene expression analysis, where destructive experimental techniques may only allow recording data once over a cell's lifetime. Precisely, we assume the system follows a time-homogeneous diffusion process that has reached an equilibrium distribution at observation time. Further, we assume the causal mechanism is fully described by the diffusion drift, is acyclic, and its causal structure graph is known. In this setting, we prove that the full causal mechanism, i.e., the drift function, can be non-parametrically identified under a weak non-explosion criterion. We derive a non-parametric kernel estimator for this challenging inverse problem and prove its consistency. Moreover, we propose a cross-validation scheme for hyperparameter tuning, illustrate the behavior of our estimator in simulations, and we discuss connections with irreversible generative diffusion models and low-frequency sampled data.

</details>


### [202] [Factorizable Normalizing Flows for parameter-dependent density morphing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30489)
*Davide Valsecchi,Mauro Donegà,Rainer Wallny*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出可分解归一化流（FNFs），通过将参数相关的密度变化分解为各参数独立的多项式变换，避免了对组合爆炸式联合参数空间的采样，实现了随参数数量线性扩展的连续密度形变建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题（如高能物理）需要建模概率密度随连续参数（物理效应强度、校准常数、系统不确定性）的形变。为每个参数配置单独训练一个流模型在计算上不可行，因为联合参数空间随参数数量指数增长。

Method: 提出可分解归一化流（FNFs），将参数依赖密度表示为一个固定的高保真参考配置流，与一个可学习的变换相结合。该变换对参数呈多项式依赖，并在参数维度上分解。每个参数的效应从单独变化该参数的样本中孤立学习，多参数的联合响应通过推理时的求和恢复，无需采样组合爆炸的联合空间。可选地引入交互项以捕捉参数间强变化时的残差相关性。

Result: 在具有两个可解释形变联合作用的受控问题上，学习到的变换复现了真实形变，匹配了最优似然值，且可选的交互项成功捕捉了多参数同时强变化时的残差相关性。

Conclusion: FNFs模型具有可解释性，计算复杂度随参数数量线性扩展，且保持似然可处理，为需要连续密度形变的推理工作流提供了通用工具，可直接赋能高能物理中下一代无分箱似然拟合。

Abstract: Normalizing Flows excel at modeling a single fixed density, yet many problems across the sciences, such as high energy physics, instead require modeling how that density deforms as a function of continuous parameters: the strength of a physical effect, a calibration constant, or a source of systematic uncertainty. Learning a separate flow for every parameter configuration quickly becomes intractable, since the number of joint settings grows exponentially with the number of parameters. We introduce Factorizable Normalizing Flows (FNFs), which represent the parameter-dependent density as a fixed, high-fidelity flow for a reference configuration composed with a learnable transformation that is polynomial in the parameters and factorized over them. This structure has a practical consequence: each parameter's effect is learned in isolation, from samples in which that parameter alone is varied. The combined response of many parameters is then recovered by summation at inference, without ever sampling their combinatorially large joint space. On a controlled problem with two interpretable deformations applied jointly to the data, the learned transformation reproduces the true deformations and matches the optimal likelihood, while optional interaction terms capture residual correlations when several parameters vary strongly at once. The resulting model is interpretable, scales linearly with the number of parameters, and keeps the likelihood tractable. This provides a general tool for any inference workflow requiring continuous density morphing, and directly enables the next generation of unbinned likelihood fits in high energy physics.

</details>


### [203] [Doubly Robust Adaptive Conformal Inference for Causal Effects Under Temporal Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30500)
*Andreas Koukorinis,Ricardo Silva*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文提出了一种双重稳健自适应共形推断方法（DR-ACI），用于在时间相依数据下，基于双重稳健伪结果构造预测区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间相依的观测数据中，现有预测区间构造方法要么依赖正确的模型设定（如结果回归或倾向得分模型），要么在模型误设时覆盖概率失效。双重稳健（DR）估计可在其中一个模型正确时保证一致性，但将其与自适应共形推断（ACI）结合以处理时间相依性尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现模型误设下的有效不确定性量化。

Method: 将双重稳健估计框架与自适应共形推断相结合：首先构造双重稳健的伪结果（结合结果回归模型与倾向得分/逆概率加权），然后在此伪结果基础上运行自适应共形推断算法，动态更新预测区间的宽度，以适应时间相依数据中的分布漂移。

Result: DR-ACI在理论上保证了在时间相依设定下，只要结果回归模型或倾向得分模型其中之一被正确指定，预测区间就能渐近达到名义覆盖水平；同时继承了ACI对分布漂移的自适应能力。模拟实验与实证分析验证了该方法相较单一模型方法的优越覆盖性能。

Conclusion: DR-ACI成功将双重稳健性与自适应共形推断融合，为时间相依观测数据提供了一种在模型部分误设下仍有效的预测区间构造方法，拓展了共形推断在因果推断与策略评估等领域的适用性。

Abstract: We propose doubly robust adaptive conformal inference (DR-ACI), which constructs prediction intervals for doubly robust pseudo-outcomes under temporal dependence.

</details>


### [204] [Optimization Dynamics Imprint Semantic Specificity in Contrastive Embedding Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30625)
*Ziwei Su,Junyu Ren,Victor Veitch*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为对比嵌入模型中嵌入向量范数编码语义信息这一经验现象提供了形式化理论框架，证明嵌入长度是训练过程的自然副产品，并可充当“免费”校准信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释一个反直觉的经验发现：使用尺度不变损失的对比嵌入模型通常忽略嵌入幅度（仅用余弦相似度），但被“丢弃”的嵌入范数却与概念特异性、词频、人类不确定性等语义属性显著相关。现有理解仅停留在启发式观察层面，缺乏形式化解释。

Method: 作者通过分析对比嵌入模型的优化动力学，推导出嵌入长度与语义属性之间关系的解析公式，从梯度流和损失景观的角度形式化地刻画了范数如何作为训练过程的副产品自然编码信息。

Result: 推导出的解析公式表明，嵌入长度确实编码了概念特异性、词频等语义信息；该信号可在特定模型和检索任务中作为“免费”校准工具使用，为先前启发式观察提供了有理论根基的解释。

Conclusion: 嵌入范数并非无意义的副产品，而是蕴含丰富语义信息的有用信号，可零成本用于模型校准和检索任务改进。

Abstract: Contrastive embedding models trained with scale-invariant losses are typically paired with distance metrics like cosine similarity, effectively ignoring embedding magnitudes. However, surprisingly, empirical studies reveal that despite this, these "discarded" norms seem to correlate with semantic properties such as concept specificity, token frequency, and human uncertainty. In this work, we provide a formal theoretical framework explaining this phenomenon. By analyzing the optimization dynamics, we derive an analytic formula demonstrating that embedding length naturally encodes this information as a byproduct of the training process. We also show how this gives rise to signals that can serve as "free" calibration tools in specific models and retrieval tasks, providing a grounded explanation for a previously heuristic observation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [Choosing the threshold in extreme value analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28540)
*Léo R. Belzile,Anthony C. Davison*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文综述了40余种极值分析中的阈值选取方法，涵盖半参数方法、可视化诊断、拟合优度检验及扩展广义Pareto模型等方法，系统评估了各方法的优劣与自动化潜力，并通过仿真研究和实际降雨数据进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量极值分析中，阈值的选择对推断结果影响巨大，但其不确定性常被忽视。目前缺乏对众多阈值选取方法的系统性比较与评估，研究者需要一个清晰的指南来选择合适的阈值选取程序。

Method: 作者综述了40多种阈值选取方法，包括基于Hill估计量的半参数方法、可视化诊断法、拟合优度检验法以及基于扩展广义Pareto模型的方法。在此基础上，从各类方法的统计性质出发进行批判性评估，讨论自动化实现的可能性，并通过广泛的仿真模拟研究和意大利Padova站点的日降雨量长时序数据进行实证比较。

Result: 仿真研究识别出了最有前景的阈值选取程序，并揭示了不同方法在实际应用中的表现差异。基于Padova日降雨数据的分析进一步验证了各方法在真实场景下的适用性和稳健性。

Conclusion: 阈值选取的不确定性不应被忽视；现有方法各有优劣，部分方法具备较好的自动化潜力；通过系统比较可为实践者提供方法选择的指导，提升极值分析推断的可靠性。

Abstract: One of the two dominant approaches for univariate extreme value analysis is to model exceedances above a large threshold, the choice of which has a large impact on inference and whose uncertainty is often subsequently ignored. In this article we review more than 40 threshold selection procedures, including semiparametric methods based on Hill's estimator, visual diagnostics, goodness-of-fit tests, and others based on extended generalized Pareto models. Starting with the statistical properties underlying the various proposals, we provide a critical assessment of their strengths and weaknesses, discuss how they might be automated and describe the results of an extensive simulation study used to identify the most promising procedures. The approaches are compared using a long time series of daily rainfall totals from Padova.

</details>


### [206] [Focused median bias reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28597)
*Davide Benussi,Ioannis Kosmidis,Alessandra Salvan,Nicola Sartori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式的中位数偏差修正估计方法，适用于作为参考参数化光滑标量变换的焦点参数。该方法基于Cornish-Fisher展开，仅需最大似然估计、变换的梯度和Hessian矩阵以及对数似然导数乘积的期望，即可获得三阶中位数无偏估计量，显著降低了传统隐式方法的计算负担，并能自然整合到基于hull的置信区间程序中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最大似然估计的中位数偏差修正方法通常是隐式的，需要求解非线性估计方程组，计算量大，且要求完整设定冗余参数化，在参数变换场景下涉及繁琐的代数和定制实现。因此需要一种更简洁、计算效率更高的显式中位数偏差修正方法。

Method: 基于焦点参数的标准化最大似然估计量的Cornish-Fisher展开，建立一个方程并求解至所需阶数，得到显式的中位数偏差修正估计量。该方法仅需：(1)参考参数化下的最大似然估计（或渐近等价估计）；(2)变换的梯度和Hessian矩阵；(3)对数似然导数乘积的期望（可从现有文献获取或通过蒙特卡洛模拟估计）。

Result: 所得估计量具有三阶中位数无偏性；当焦点参数包含在参考参数化中时，可提供隐式中位数偏差修正估计量的一步近似。该方法改进了标准渐近推断，并能与基于hull的置信程序自然整合，在中位数偏差控制下产生接近名义水平的有限样本覆盖。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦信息准则(FIC)的后选择推断、马氏距离、分位数及其他标量焦点参数，在回归模型、圆形模型和分层模型中验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Median bias reduction of maximum likelihood estimators can substantially improve estimation and inference. Existing generally applicable methods are, however, typically implicit, requiring the solution of nonlinear systems of estimating equations, which is computationally demanding. They also require a fully specified nuisance parameterization, and their application to transformations of parameters involves tedious algebra and bespoke implementations. We develop an explicit median bias-corrected estimator for focus parameters that are smooth scalar transformations of a chosen reference parameterization. The estimator is obtained by solving, to the required order, an equation based on the Cornish-Fisher expansion of the centred and scaled maximum likelihood estimator of the focus parameter. It requires only the maximum likelihood or an asymptotically equivalent estimator at the reference parameterization, the gradient and Hessian of the transformation, and expectations of products of log-likelihood derivatives. These expectations are available for many models from the existing bias reduction literature and can also be estimated by Monte Carlo simulation. The resulting estimators are third-order median unbiased and provide one-step approximations to estimators from implicit median bias reduction when the focus parameter is included in the reference parameterization. The method improves standard asymptotic inference and integrates naturally with hull-based confidence procedures, yielding intervals with near nominal finite-sample coverage under median bias control. We demonstrate the framework through post-selection inference using the Focused Information Criterion, Mahalanobis distances, quantiles, and other scalar focus parameters in regression, circular, and stratified models.

</details>


### [207] [Conformal Prediction with Macro-Coverage Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28598)
*Aabesh Bhattacharyya,Tiffany Ding,Rina Foygel Barber*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出使用标签加权共形预测来构建具有有限样本宏观覆盖率保证的预测集，并将其推广到任意类别分组的广义宏观覆盖率目标，同时刻画了满足该目标的最小预测集形式与对应的共形评分函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长尾分类场景中，类条件覆盖（class-conditional coverage）因稀有类别校准点不足而难以满足，而边际覆盖（marginal coverage）则倾向于忽略低频类别。宏观覆盖（macro-coverage）作为类条件覆盖的非加权平均，在二者之间取得了折中，但现有方法缺乏有限样本下的严格保证以及对更一般化覆盖率目标的统一框架。

Method: 作者将标签加权共形预测（label-weighted conformal prediction）引入宏观覆盖率问题，证明该方法可提供有限样本的宏观覆盖率保证；进一步将框架推广至广义宏观覆盖率目标，支持任意类别分组及加权平均；同时刻画了满足给定广义宏观覆盖率目标的最小预测集结构，并给出了对应的共形评分函数设计。

Result: 理论结果表明，标签加权共形预测能够实现有限样本下的（广义）宏观覆盖率保证，并且所提出的评分函数可构造出满足目标的最小预测集。在两个大规模图像分类数据集上的实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 标签加权共形预测为长尾分类场景提供了一种兼具理论保证与实用性的不确定性量化方法，在边际覆盖与类条件覆盖之间实现了灵活且可证明的折中。

Abstract: Prediction sets should have high coverage to be useful, but some coverage notions are more practically relevant than others. In the classification setting, class-conditional coverage requires that the prediction set (i.e., the set of candidate labels for a new test point) must achieve the target accuracy level within each class, which may be challenging to satisfy when many classes are rare and have few calibration points. At the other extreme, marginal coverage requires only that coverage holds on average over the distribution of all classes, which can lead to low-probability labels being essentially ignored. To find a middle ground, recent work has introduced macro-coverage, defined as the unweighted average of class-conditional coverages. Macro-coverage offers a compromise between marginal coverage and class-conditional coverage that is particularly appropriate for long-tailed settings. In this work, we show that label-weighted conformal prediction can be used to produce prediction sets with a finite-sample macro-coverage guarantee, and more generally a guarantee on a family of generalized macro-coverage objectives that aggregate coverage at the level of arbitrary class groupings and take a weighted average. We further characterize the form of the smallest prediction sets satisfying a given generalized macro-coverage objective and propose a corresponding conformal score function. We validate our theoretical results on two large-scale image classification datasets.

</details>


### [208] [A bootstrap approach to prediction-powered inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28621)
*Bradley Efron*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于bootstrap的预测驱动推断（PPI）新算法，利用无标签数据提升参数估计效率，避免渐近理论依赖，相比Angelopoulos et al. (2023a)的方法更具通用性和效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
