Motivation: 在预测驱动推断场景中，研究者拥有部分有标签数据和无标签数据，以及来自外部数据的预测规则$f(x)$。现有方法（如Angelopoulos et al. 2023a）依赖渐近理论，可能在有限样本下表现不佳。作者希望提出一种更通用、更高效且不依赖渐近近似的推断方法。

Method: 提出基于bootstrap的PPI算法，利用全部数据（包括无标签的$x$）进行参数估计。该方法在精神上回归Wang, McCormick和Leek (2020)的原始PPI框架，通过重抽样避免渐近依赖，提升有限样本下的推断性能。

Result: 所提bootstrap方法在效率和通用性上优于Angelopoulos et al. (2023a)的算法。此外，对无标签数据的信息量分析揭示了若干出人意料的现象，尤其在估计$y$的期望值时。

Conclusion: Bootstrap方案为预测驱动推断提供了一种更优的替代方法，同时引发了对无标签数据信息含量的重新思考，揭示了其中存在的反直觉现象。

Abstract: Prediction-powered inference (PPI) refers to a two-level situation where the statistician observes a set of $(x,y)$ pairs and another set of $x$s with the responses $y$ missing. Also available is some independent background data from which a prediction rule $f(x)$ has been produced, perhaps by a machine learning algorithm; $f(x)$ approximates $E\{y\mid x\}$ but there is no guarantee of its accuracy for the situation at hand. Angelopoulos et al. (2023a) developed an algorithm that makes use of all the data, including the unlabeled $x$s, for the estimation of a parameter of interest. A different algorithm is proposed here, using the bootstrap to avoid asymptotics, that is shown to have advantages of efficiency and generality. It is similar in spirit to the original PPI paper by Wang, McCormick and Leek (2020). Prediction-powered inference raises questions about the information available in unlabeled data, with some surprises here, particularly concerning the estimation of the expected value of $y$.

</details>


### [209] [Inverse Probability Weighting in a Post-Bayesian World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28685)
*Owen Thomas,William Denault,Valeria Vitelli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文为逆概率加权（IPW）在"后贝叶斯"框架中的使用提供了理论辩护，将IPW的频率学派偏差校正重新解释为对模型与真实参数间KL散度的重新加权，并通过理论和实证（含模拟数据与前列腺癌真实登记数据）证明了该方法能处理传统贝叶斯方法难以应对的选择偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯方法在处理存在选择偏差的观测数据时面临困难，而IPW在频率学派中虽能有效进行偏差校正，但缺乏贝叶斯框架下的合理解释。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，为IPW在贝叶斯推理中提供理论基础。

Method: 提出"后贝叶斯"（post-Bayesian）框架，将IPW的偏差校正重新概念化为对统计模型与真实数据生成参数值之间Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度的重新加权。推导了广义信念后验（generalised belief posteriors）的收敛性质及其他理论属性，并通过模拟实验和大规模真实数据验证方法的实用性。

Result: 理论上证明了广义信念后验的收敛性及相关性质；模拟实验中，该方法在两个存在选择偏差的场景下表现良好；真实数据案例中，成功应用于利用前列腺特异性抗原（PSA）预测前列腺癌死亡率时登记数据中系统性偏差的校正。

Conclusion: IPW在后贝叶斯框架中具有坚实的理论基础和实用价值，能够解决传统贝叶斯方法此前难以处理的一类偏差问题，拓展了贝叶斯推理的适用范围。

Abstract: We present a justification of the use of Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) in a post-Bayesian framework, in which the bias-correction provided by IPW in a frequentist context is reframed as a reweighting of the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence between the statistical model and the true data-generating parameter value. We provide a coherent argument in support of this approach, including theoretical results concerning convergence and properties of the generalised belief posteriors. We present examples demonstrating the utility of post-Bayesian IPW in practice: these include two simulated examples of inference under selection bias in the observed data, and a large-scale real-data example concerning systematic biases present in registry data when using prostate-specific antigen (PSA) to predict prostate cancer mortality. The empirical and theoretical results together show the utility of IPW to address classes of problems previously intractable within a Bayesian approach.

</details>


### [210] [Composition as Direction: An Active-Set Ray-Based Model for Sparse High-Dimensional Compositional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28738)
*Michael R Schwob,Jyotishka Datta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于活动集射线（ARC）的成分数据分析框架，通过将成分数据映射到单位超球面的非负象限，并引入活动集过程来显式建模零值成分，从而在保持潜在高斯解释的同时，高效处理高维成分数据中的精确零值、潜在依赖和单纯形约束问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物、生态和环境科学中的成分数据普遍存在四个难以同时处理的特性：精确零值、成分间的潜在依赖、高维度以及由单位和约束导致的非欧几里得几何。传统Dirichlet型和对数正态模型仅能部分解决这些问题。投影高斯模型虽能捕捉精确零值和潜在依赖，但其截断或折叠操作在高维时计算不可行。

Method: 提出ARC框架：(1) 将成分数据映射到单位超球面的非负象限；(2) 引入活动集过程（active-set process）来管理哪些成分存在（非零）；(3) 在给定活动集的条件下，沿活动子空间的正射线评估潜在高斯密度，并将半径作为辅助变量处理，从而建模正子成分。该框架将活动集过程与正子成分的建模分离，保留潜在高斯解释，并可容纳任意潜在依赖结构。

Result: ARC框架在概念上将成分重新定义为潜在丰度向量的观测方向，该向量具有未观测的幅度和显式建模的活动集。该框架在保持投影高斯模型优势的同时，在高维设置下仍保持计算可行性。

Conclusion: ARC框架为需要同时处理精确零值和共享正响应的高维成分数据应用提供了有效工具，特别适用于微生物组学、生态学等以零值和正值为科学核心的研究领域。

Abstract: [Working Draft] Compositional data are central to microbial, ecological, and environmental research, yet often have four features that are difficult to accommodate jointly: exact zeros, latent dependence among components, high-dimensionality, and a unit-sum constraint that induces a non-Euclidean geometry. Conventional Dirichlet-type and logistic-normal models address these features only partially. Projected Gaussian models offer a directional representation that captures exact zeros and latent dependence; however, support correctness on the simplex requires either truncation or folding, both of which become computationally prohibitive as the dimension grows. We develop an Active-set Ray-based Compositional (ARC) framework, which retains the benefits of projected Gaussian models while remaining computationally feasible in high-dimensional settings. In this framework, we map compositions to the nonnegative orthant of the unit hypersphere and specify an active-set process that governs which components are present. Conditional on the active set, the positive subcomposition is modeled by evaluating a latent Gaussian density along positive rays of the active subspace with the radius treated as an auxiliary variable. Such a construction (i) separates the active-set process that governs which components are present from the positive subcomposition on the active components, (ii) preserves a latent Gaussian interpretation, and (iii) accommodates arbitrary latent dependence. Thus, the framework is conducive to high-dimensional applications in which exact zeros and shared positive responses are scientifically central. Conceptually, the proposed framework reframes a composition as an observed direction of a latent abundance vector with an unobserved magnitude and an explicitly modeled active set.

</details>


### [211] [Inferring Comprehensive Cohort Causal Effects in the Presence of Unmeasured Confounding and Missing Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28741)
*Shiyao Xu,Razieh Nabi,Martin Underwood,Daniel Scharfstein*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出一种在混合设计临床研究（RCT+观察性研究）中估计综合队列因果效应的方法框架，结合半参数敏感性分析处理未测量混杂和随机缺失结果，推导有效影响函数并构建一步偏差校正估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合设计研究（同时包含RCT和观察性研究臂）在实践中日益常见，但如何在存在未测量混杂（观察性研究臂）和随机缺失数据的情况下，稳健地估计综合队列因果效应（CCCE）仍缺乏系统的方法论框架。

Method: 基于半参数理论的敏感性分析框架，推导CCCE的有效影响函数并以敏感性参数化；提出一步偏差校正估计量（one-step bias-corrected estimator），允许灵活建模（如机器学习方法），并证明其在特定条件下具有√n相合性。

Result: 将方法应用于TOIB研究（口服vs外用布洛芬治疗慢性膝痛的RCT+OBS混合设计），并在一项符合实际的模拟研究中验证了方法的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 所提方法为混合设计临床研究提供了统一的因果推断工具，能够在考虑未测量混杂和缺失数据的情况下，对CCCE进行稳健且灵活的估计与敏感性评估。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodological framework for estimating the comprehensive cohort causal effect (CCCE) in mixed-design clinical studies that combine randomized controlled trials (RCTs) and parallel observational study (OBS). Our approach is designed to evaluate robustness against unmeasured confounding in the OBS arm and to handle outcomes that are missing at random in either the RCT or OBS arm. By employing a semiparametric theory-based sensitivity analysis framework, we derive the efficient influence function for the CCCE, parameterized by sensitivity parameters. We propose a one-step bias-corrected estimator that allows for flexible modeling and establish conditions under which our CCCE estimator is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent. To illustrate our methods, we apply them to the TOIB study, which evaluates the efficacy and safety of oral versus topical ibuprofen in managing chronic knee pain among older adults. We also evaluate the performance of the proposed methodology in a realistic simulation study.

</details>


### [212] [Measurement Induced Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28774)
*George Perrett,Klint Kanopka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 观测研究中，对潜在特质（如动机、自我效能感）的测量误差会导致"测量诱导混杂"，使得传统调整方法产生有偏的因果效应估计。本文提出通过贝叶斯联合估计同时建模测量模型、处理分配模型和响应模型来解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测研究的关键假设是所有混杂变量被无误差地测量并充分调整。然而，社会科学和医学中许多重要的混杂变量（如动机、自我效能感、能力等）属于潜在特质，无法直接观测。传统做法使用总分、测量模型估计或项目反应来代理这些变量，但测量误差在此过程中传播并污染因果估计，这一现象尚未被系统揭示和解决。

Method: 作者提出了"测量诱导混杂"（Measurement Induced Confounding）的概念框架，用以描述测量误差如何在估计过程中传播并导致偏差。随后提出一种贝叶斯联合估计（Bayesian Joint Estimation）方法，将测量模型、处理分配模型和结果模型纳入统一的联合似然框架中同时进行估计，从而避免分步估计中误差传播的问题。

Result: 研究表明，传统方法（调整总分、测量模型能力估计值或项目反应）均产生有偏的平均处理效应估计，且置信区间覆盖率校准错误。相比之下，贝叶斯联合估计方法能够有效解决测量诱导混杂问题，得到无偏的因果效应估计和正确的推断区间。

Conclusion: 当前大量试图调整潜在混杂变量的观测研究，很可能给出了有偏的因果估计和错误的置信区间。贝叶斯联合估计为处理含有潜在特质混杂的观测研究提供了一条可行的解决路径，对社会科学和医学领域的因果推断实践具有重要启示。

Abstract: A critical assumption of observational studies is that all confounding variables must be known and sufficiently adjusted for to estimate causal effects. An implicit, and often overlooked, aspect of this assumption is that all confounding variables have been measured without error. In the social and medical sciences, latent traits such as motivation, self-efficacy, and ability measures are likely confounding variables. Because latent traits are not directly observable, conventional approaches to adjust for them in observational studies rely on collecting responses to individual items on a test or survey instrument and then adjust for sum scores, measurement model-derived ability estimates, or item responses directly. Through a process we describe as measurement induced confounding, we show that measurement error propagates through the estimation process and that current conventional approaches to adjusting for latent traits in observational studies produce biased estimates of the average treatment effect with incorrectly calibrated coverage properties. A critical implication of this finding is that current observational studies that attempt to adjust for latent confounding variables likely put forth biased causal estimates with incorrect uncertainty intervals. We show that measurement induced confounding can be resolved through a Bayesian Joint Estimation approach that simultaneously estimates the measurement model, the treatment assignment model, and the response model.

</details>


### [213] [Generated outcomes as generated regressors: Equivalences in recursive causal estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29009)
*Wisse Rutgers,Rahul Singh*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了递归回归设定下的因果估计量（递归plug-in、递归平衡权重、递归双重稳健），证明了在OLS拟合下三者有限样本等价，并分析了在岭惩罚及一般凸惩罚下的行为——双重稳健估计量退化为各阶段惩罚回归与OLS的混合，且OLS的权重随时间周期呈几何衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变处理效应、替代识别处理效应和中介效应均可表示为递归回归形式，但标准因果估计量在此类递归设定中的行为尚未被系统研究。作者旨在填补这一理论空白，理解plug-in、平衡权重和双重稳健三类估计量在递归框架下的关系。

Method: 将递归plug-in、递归平衡权重和递归双重稳健三类估计量置于统一的递归回归框架下进行比较分析。首先证明OLS情形下三者的有限样本等价性；进而引入岭惩罚，推导双重稳健估计量的后向递归结构；最后推广至一般凸惩罚，建立逐阶段的恒等式。

Result: （1）当每阶段均用OLS拟合时，无论模型设定正确与否，三种递归估计量在任何有限样本中均一致；（2）在岭惩罚下，双重稳健估计量等价于惩罚回归与OLS逐阶段混合的后向递归，且OLS权重随周期数呈几何衰减，意味着截面设定中"偏差校正使估计量趋近OLS"的直觉在长期面板中逐渐失效；（3）对一般凸惩罚，得到了逐阶段的恒等式刻画。

Conclusion: 递归回归框架下，三种估计方法的关系高度依赖于估计方式：OLS下完全等价，惩罚回归下则呈现复杂的混合结构，且时间维度越长，OLS成分衰减越快。这为纵向/递归因果推断中的估计量选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Time-varying treatment effects, surrogate-identified treatment effects, and mediation effects can all be written as recursive regressions, in which each regression's predicted values become generated outcomes for the next regression. We study how standard causal estimators behave in this setting. Formally, we compare the recursive plug-in, recursive balancing weight, and recursive doubly robust estimators. When every stage is fitted by ordinary least squares (OLS), the three recursive estimators coincide in any finite sample, whether or not the models are correctly specified. As such, estimation by recursively regressing generated outcomes is numerically equivalent to estimation by recursively balancing generated regressors. Under ridge penalisation for the balancing weights, the doubly robust estimator is a backward recursion of stage-wise blends of penalised and OLS regressions. The weight on the recursive OLS regression decays geometrically in the number of time periods. Therefore, the intuition from the cross-sectional setting, where the bias correction moves the estimator towards OLS, applies less and less as the number of time periods increases. For general convex penalties, we derive an identity at each stage.

</details>


### [214] [Beta-trees for testing multivariate goodness-of-fit and localizing deviations from a model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29021)
*Valerie N. P. Ho,Guenther Walther*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出基于Beta-tree划分的拟合优度检验方法，通过数据自适应分区和有限样本置信区间，检测模型与数据的局部偏离，应用于混合模型分量数选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局GOF检验（如KS、AD检验）只能通过单一统计量衡量整体差异，无法有效检测局部模型误设或定位偏离区域，需要一种能捕捉局部偏离的检验方法。

Method: 采用Beta-tree对样本空间进行数据自适应划分，为每个区域提供有限样本保证的概率内容置信区间；通过检验零分布F0赋予的概率是否落入这些区间来评估模型-数据一致性；在混合模型应用中，通过k-means聚类构建零分布。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，该方法能有效检测零分布的局部偏离，优于传统全局GOF检验。

Conclusion: Beta-tree GOF检验能有效识别局部模型误设区域，在混合模型分量数选择等应用中表现良好。

Abstract: We introduce a novel goodness-of-fit (GOF) procedure based on Beta-tree partitions. A Beta-tree produces a data-adaptive partition of the sample space into regions and provides guaranteed finite sample confidence intervals for the probability contents of each region. The proposed test assesses whether the probabilities assigned by a null distribution $F_0$ fall within these intervals, thereby quantifying agreement between the model and the data. A key application is the selection of the number of components in a mixture model, where the null distribution is constructed via $k$-means clustering. In contrast to classical global GOF tests such as Kolmogorov-Smirnov or Anderson-Darling, which quantify the discrepancy through a single global statistic, our method is designed to detect local departures from the null and to identify regions of model misspecification. We demonstrate the efficiency of our test in detecting departures from the null on some simulated and real datasets.

</details>


### [215] [On Modeling Cylindrical Data with a Discrete Circular Component and Its Environmental Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29041)
*Brajesh Kumar Dhakad,Jayant Jha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联合建模离散圆形变量与连续线性变量的灵活解析模型，通过结合包裹对称几何分布、Weibull分布和三角连接函数，给出了闭合形式的联合、边缘及条件分布，并基于Weibull分布实现了便捷的逆变换采样。模型还提供了条件矩的显式表达，支持灵活的圆-线性回归，且从理论上证明了条件均值与方差对依赖参数的单调性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的统计方法难以充分建模线性变量与圆形变量之间的联合依赖关系，而现有方法仅适用于连续变量。然而在实际中，圆形数据常因舍入或实验设计而以有限等间距方向的形式观测到（即离散圆形数据）。这一方法学空白亟需填补。

Method: 提出一个联合模型：离散圆形变量部分采用包裹对称几何分布 (wrapped symmetric geometric distribution)；连续线性变量部分采用 Weibull 分布；二者通过三角连接函数 (trigonometric linking function) 耦合。模型推导出联合、边缘与条件分布的闭合形式，利用逆变换方法可直接从Weibull分布生成样本，并给出条件矩的显式表达式以构建圆-线性回归框架。

Result: 模型具有闭合形式的分布与条件矩表达式；数学上严格证明了条件均值和条件方差关于依赖参数的单调性；广泛数值模拟验证了估计量的良好性能；两个环境科学实证数据集的分析展示了模型的实用价值。

Conclusion: 所提模型有效解决了离散圆形变量与连续线性变量的联合建模问题，兼具理论解析性与实际可操作性，在环境科学等领域具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Standard statistical methods are often inadequate for modeling the joint dependence between linear and circular variables, and existing methods for modeling this dependence are designed only for continuous variables. However, circular data are frequently observed on a finite set of equally spaced directions, either due to rounding prior to reporting or because of the experimental design employed for data collection. To address this gap, we propose a flexible, analytically tractable model for jointly representing a discrete circular and a continuous linear variable. The construction combines a wrapped symmetric geometric distribution, a Weibull distribution, and a trigonometric linking function. This formulation yields closed-form expressions for the joint, marginal, and conditional distributions. The choice of the Weibull distribution facilitates direct sample generation using the inverse transform technique. Additionally, it provides explicit expressions for conditional moments, enabling a flexible circular-linear regression framework. We detail the theoretical interpretation of the model parameters, mathematically establishing the monotonicity of the conditional mean and variance with respect to the dependence parameters. The performance of the estimators is demonstrated through extensive simulations, and the utility of the model is illustrated by analyzing two empirical environmental datasets.

</details>


### [216] [Learning heterogeneous treatment effects under principal stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29076)
*Jiaqi Tong,Fan Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究了主分层框架下条件主因果效应的识别与估计问题，提出了一种新颖的双重交叉拟合双重稳健机器学习估计量，在序贯正交学习和正则化最小二乘筛分方法下建立了L²和一致极限理论，并达到神谕效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主分层文献主要关注跨层平均效应，忽视了层内治疗效应异质性，而这种异质性对个体化治疗决策至关重要。同时，主分层固有的嵌套冗余结构也给估计带来挑战，亟需填补这一方法学空白。

Method: 在主可忽略性假设下，结合比值比敏感性参数化（放松单调性假设），识别条件主因果效应。提出双重交叉拟合双重稳健机器学习估计量，利用序贯正交学习和正则化最小二乘筛分(sieves)处理嵌套冗余结构，推导L²收敛率与一致极限理论，证明神谕效率，构造一致置信带。

Result: 模拟实验验证了所提估计量在有限样本下的优良表现。针对急性肺损伤随机试验的实证分析揭示了"始终存活者"子群体内治疗效果异质性的信息模式。

Conclusion: 该方法有效填补了主分层框架中条件因果效应估计的空白，通过放松单调性假设和高效处理嵌套冗余结构，为个体化治疗决策提供了可靠的统计工具。

Abstract: Principal stratification provides a foundational framework for causal inference with intermediate outcomes by defining causal effects within subpopulations, yet existing work has largely focused on average effects across strata rather than treatment effect heterogeneity within strata. Such within-stratum heterogeneity informs individualized treatment decisions but the associated methods are sparse. We address this gap by studying the identification and estimation of the conditional principal causal effects under principal ignorability combined with an odds ratio sensitivity parameterization, which relaxes the monotonicity assumption. To efficiently learn these estimands, we propose a novel doubly cross-fit doubly robust machine learner that resolves the nested nuisance structure inherent to principal stratification. Leveraging sequential orthogonal learning with regularized least-squares sieves, we derive $\mathcal{L}^2$ and uniform limit theory, establish oracle efficiency, and construct uniform confidence bands for the proposed estimator. We use simulations to demonstrate the finite-sample performance of our estimator, and provide an empirical analysis of a randomized trial in acute lung injury, revealing informative patterns of treatment effect heterogeneity within the always-survivor subpopulation.

</details>


### [217] [Panel Flow Matching: A Generative Approach to Learning Distributions of Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29105)
*Jianbin Tan,Pixu Shi,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: PFM 是一种用于学习纵向数据分布的生成框架，通过连续面板流模型跨时间汇集信息，结合前向流匹配与后向核拟合步骤，能够灵活处理不规则、稀疏的纵向观测数据，并在密度估计、数据补全、合成生成及分类等任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据在可视化、补全、分类和合成数据生成等任务中至关重要，但由于观测通常不规则、稀疏且受试者数量有限，学习其分布面临显著的统计挑战。现有方法常需预先降维以处理数据不规则性，缺乏灵活且具有统计保证的统一框架。

Method: 提出面板流匹配（Panel Flow Matching, PFM）框架。核心思想是将连续面板流模型与流匹配（flow matching）相结合：前向流匹配步骤学习从基分布到目标分布的连续时间流，后向核拟合步骤利用核方法对跨时间的面板结构进行自适应建模。该方法无需预先降维即可直接处理不规则和稀疏采样。

Result: 理论层面，PFM在面板密度（截面密度）估计上建立了不规则稀疏采样设计下的统计保证。仿真实验表明，PFM在纵向数据补全、合成数据生成和分类任务上均优于现有方法。在包含188例怀孕（足月与早产标签）的阴道微生物组纵向数据应用中，PFM提升了分类准确率，并揭示了随时间变化的组间分布差异。

Conclusion: PFM为不规则稀疏纵向数据的分布学习提供了一个统一、灵活且有理论保证的生成框架，能够直接支持多种下游任务，并在真实微生物组数据中展现出揭示动态分布差异和提升分类性能的实用价值。

Abstract: Learning distributions of longitudinal data is central to tasks such as visualization, completion, classification, and synthetic data generation, but it remains statistically challenging because longitudinal observations are often irregular, sparse, and collected from only a limited number of subjects. To address this, we develop a novel generative framework, termed panel flow matching (PFM), for learning longitudinal distributions by pooling information across time via a continuous panel flow model. PFM combines a forward flow-matching step with a backward kernel-fitting step, yielding a flexible and data-adaptive approach for capturing complex distributional structures. We apply PFM to estimate panel densities, namely the cross-sectional densities of longitudinal data, and establish statistical guarantees under irregular and sparse sampling designs. Under this, PFM naturally supports tasks including longitudinal completion, synthetic data generation, and classification, without requiring a preliminary dimension-reduction step to handle data irregularity. Extensive simulations demonstrate that PFM outperforms existing methods across these tasks. We further apply PFM to a vaginal microbiome longitudinal dataset from 188 pregnancies labeled as term or preterm, where it improves classification accuracy and reveals time-varying distributional differences between the two groups.

</details>


### [218] [Multivariate Varying-Coefficient BART with Graphical Horseshoe Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29114)
*Soham Ghosh,Sameer K. Deshpande*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 多变量变系数BART（multiVCBART）框架，通过独立的BART集成建模结果特异性的非线性和异质性系数，并结合Graphical Horseshoe先验捕获稀疏的残差精度矩阵，实现了灵活的多元回归与残差依赖联合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元贝叶斯树方法要么强制共享树结构（限制性过强），要么仅建模简单均值结构而忽略残差依赖，缺乏能同时处理非线性、异质结果特异性效应与残差依赖结构的统一框架。

Method: 提出multiVCBART：系数矩阵B(x)的每个元素用独立BART集成表示，允许预测效应随修饰因子x非线性变化；精度矩阵Ω采用Graphical Horseshoe先验实现稀疏估计。计算上，通过将多元高斯似然解耦为一系列标量伪响应更新，分离树回拟与图Horseshoe步骤。

Result: 理论上首次建立了联合估计残差依赖的多元BART模型的后验收缩率，证明其近乎极小极大适应潜在光滑性和结构稀疏性。实证中在稀疏高维数据上优于现有多元树模型和贝叶斯SUR方法，并在癌症药物敏感性基因组学数据中识别出不同生物标志物信号和连贯的残差药理网络。

Conclusion: multiVCBART为多变量回归提供了一个统一、灵活且理论有保障的框架，能同时捕获复杂的非线性预测效应和结果间的残差条件依赖，在方法论、理论和应用层面均有显著贡献。

Abstract: Modern multivariate regression problems involve several related outcomes whose regression effects are not only nonlinear, heterogeneous, and outcome-specific, but also where the residual dependence among outcomes is scientifically meaningful. Existing multivariate Bayesian tree-based methods typically address only part of this problem: some impose substantial sharing of tree architecture across outcomes, which is overly restrictive when responses depend on distinct predictors or effect modifiers, while others accommodate residual dependence but retain simpler mean structures. This paper develops multiVCBART, a multivariate varying-coefficient Bayesian additive regression tree framework that jointly models flexible outcome-specific coefficient surfaces and a sparse residual precision matrix. Each entry of the coefficient matrix $B(x)$ is represented by an independent BART ensemble, allowing predictor effects to vary nonlinearly with modifiers $x$ across outcomes, while a Graphical Horseshoe prior on the precision matrix $Ω$ captures parsimonious residual conditional dependence. To permit efficient computation, we introduce a sampler that reduces the multivariate Gaussian likelihood to a sequence of scalar pseudo-response updates, decoupling the tree backfitting from the Graphical Horseshoe step. Theoretically, we establish the first posterior contraction rates for a multivariate BART model with jointly estimated residual dependence, proving near-minimax adaptation to underlying smoothness and structural sparsity. Empirically, multiVCBART outperforms existing multivariate tree models and Bayesian SUR competitors on sparse, high-dimensional datasets. Finally, in a re-analysis of the Genomics of Drug Sensitivity in Cancer dataset, our method identifies distinct biomarker signals and recovers a coherent residual pharmacologic network.

</details>


### [219] [Scalable Bayesian Spatial Mixture Modelling for Remote Sensing Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29448)
*Bao Khanh Nguyen,Iain Cameron,Cecilia Balocchi,Torben Sell*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出POTTERS框架，一种贝叶斯空间混合模型用于遥感图像地物分类。该模型扩展经典Potts模型，引入广义空间依赖结构和来自已有标注数据的先验信息，无需目标区域标注数据即可实现不确定性量化与新类别发现，并通过变分推断实现大规模图像的可扩展计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感地物分类方法缺乏原则性的不确定性量化，且需要大量标注数据，在新区域存在分布偏移时可用性和可靠性受限。亟需一种无需目标区域标注数据、能提供不确定性量化、并可检测新类别的方法。

Method: 提出POTTERS框架，扩展经典Potts模型：(1)引入广义空间依赖结构；(2)利用已有外部标注数据估计信息先验，融入先验标签信息；(3)可检测目标区域新聚类；(4)开发高效变分推断算法进行后验近似，确保对大规模遥感图像的可扩展性。

Result: 模拟研究表明该方法有效；在苏格兰地物分类案例中，利用英格兰公开遥感数据作为先验，成功实现分类且无需苏格兰本地标注数据。

Conclusion: POTTERS框架可在无目标区域标注数据条件下实现可靠的地物分类，提供鲁棒的不确定性量化，并能检测新地物类别，对全球保护监测和政策制定具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable land cover classification is essential for global conservation monitoring and policy-making. While remote sensing images provide a cost-effective alternative to ground surveys, current methods often lack principled uncertainty quantification and require substantial labelled data, limiting their usability and reliability in new regions with distribution shifts. We propose a Bayesian spatial mixture modelling approach for image segmentation, extending the classical Potts model by allowing for a generalised spatial dependence structure and incorporating informative priors estimated from pre-existing labelled data. Our framework, called POTTERS (Potts Model for Enhanced Remote Sensing), enables robust uncertainty quantification, accounts for class interactions, and can detect new clusters in the target region of interest. Crucially, our model does not require labelled data from the target region; instead, it incorporates prior information about the labels from pre-existing externally labelled images. To ensure scalability to large remote sensing images, we develop an efficient variational inference algorithm for posterior approximation. We demonstrate the benefits of our approach in simulation studies and apply it to land cover classification in a case study in Scotland, leveraging publicly available remote sensing data from England.

</details>


### [220] [Modelling and detecting mild and gross anomalies in circular data via double-contaminated models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29524)
*Antonio Punzo,Andriëtte Bekker,Arno Otto,Priyanka Nagar,Cristina Tortora*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模型的框架，用于在存在异常观测的情况下对圆周数据进行稳健推断。该框架通过构建有限混合模型，区分轻微异常和严重异常：严重异常模型加入均匀分布成分，轻微异常模型混入同均值方向但更低浓度的分布，以及结合两者的双重污染模型。后验成分概率实现自动分类，混合权重提供异常流行率和离散度膨胀的解释性度量。方法在包裹正态和von Mises分布上进行了模拟研究和三个实际数据应用的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆周数据（如动物移动方向、风向）的统计分析常受异常观测值干扰。现有方法或仅处理严重异常（如加入均匀分布），或仅处理轻微异常（如重尾模型），缺乏一个统一框架同时区分并建模这两类不同性质的异常，从而限制了方向数据的稳健推断和潜在结构的解释能力。

Method: 以单峰对称的圆周参考分布（包裹正态或von Mises）为基础，参数化均值方向和浓度参数。构建三类有限混合模型：(1) 严重异常模型——参考分布与圆周均匀分布的混合；(2) 轻微异常（污染）模型——参考分布与同均值但更低浓度的同族分布的混合；(3) 双重污染模型——同时包含参考分布、低浓度同均值分布和均匀分布的三成分混合。通过后验成分概率实现无需特定阈值的自动分类，混合权重直接量化异常比例和离散度膨胀程度。

Result: 广泛的模拟研究表明，双重污染模型能有效区分轻微和严重异常。三个实际数据应用（动物移动方向和风向）显示，联合建模两类偏离显著改善模型拟合，并提供方向数据的分解信息——不仅检测异常，还能揭示数据中的潜在结构。

Conclusion: 基于混合的稳健方法不仅对方向数据中的异常检测有价值，而且对解释和识别潜在结构也具有重要意义。联合建模轻微和严重偏离能够提供更丰富的推断，优于仅处理单一类型异常的传统方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a model-based framework to robustify inference for circular data in the presence of anomalous observations, distinguishing between mild and gross anomalies. Starting from a unimodal and symmetric reference model on $[0,2π)$, parametrized by a mean direction and concentration, we construct a family of finite mixtures: a gross-anomaly model obtained by adding a circular uniform component; a mild-anomaly (contaminated) model obtained by mixing the reference distribution with a less concentrated version sharing the same mean direction; and a general three-component specification combining both models, the double-contaminated model. Posterior component probabilities provide an automatic classification of observations without ad hoc thresholds, while the mixing weights yield interpretable measures of anomaly prevalence and dispersion inflation. For illustration, we consider two classical circular reference distributions, the wrapped normal and von Mises. The methodology is evaluated through an extensive simulation study and three real-data applications involving animal movement directions and wind directions. The results indicate that jointly modelling mild and gross departures improves model fit and yields an informative decomposition of the directional data, demonstrating that mixture-based robustness is valuable not only for anomaly detection but also for the interpretation and the identification of latent structure in directional data.

</details>


### [221] [Beyond Local Independence: High-Dimensional Latent Class Graphical Models with Shared Block Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29631)
*Seunghyun Lee,Yuqi Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向有序分类变量的高维潜在类图模型，通过共享块划分结构刻画类内局部依赖，并开发了可扩展的三步估计方法：谱聚类恢复潜在类、协方差聚合恢复块结构、稀疏精度矩阵估计，在有限样本下实现了所有模型组件的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典潜在类分析依赖局部独立性假设，在高维现代应用中往往不现实。需要在保留潜在类模型可解释性和简洁性的同时，灵活刻画变量间的局部依赖结构。

Method: 提出高维潜在类图模型，采用共享块划分（block partition）结构，允许每类在块内具有不同的图依赖。三步估计方法：(1) 对展平后的响应矩阵做谱聚类恢复潜在类；(2) 估计各类潜在协方差矩阵并聚合以恢复共享块划分；(3) 在各块内估计稀疏精度矩阵。建立了聚类、协方差估计、块恢复和精度矩阵估计的有限样本误差界。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该方法能在可扩展计算下准确恢复潜在类、共享块划分和类特定依赖图。在美国国家选举研究调查数据和HapMap3基因型数据的应用中，方法揭示了可解释的局部依赖结构，同时考虑了潜在异质性。

Conclusion: 所提模型和方法在高维设定下实现了端到端的一致性，兼具经典潜在类分析的简洁可解释性和图模型的灵活依赖刻画能力，在调查数据和基因组数据中验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Latent class models are central tools for multivariate categorical data from heterogeneous populations, but their standard local-independence assumption is often unrealistic in modern high-dimensional applications. We propose a high-dimensional latent class graphical model for ordinal responses with block-structured local dependence. The model retains the interpretability and parsimony of classical latent class analysis by imposing a shared block partition of variables, while allowing class-specific graphical dependence within each block. We develop a scalable three-step estimator that first recovers latent classes by spectral clustering of a flattened response matrix, then estimates class-specific latent covariance matrices and aggregates them to recover the shared block partition, and finally estimates sparse within-block precision matrices. We establish finite-sample error bounds for clustering, covariance estimation, block recovery, and precision-matrix estimation, yielding end-to-end consistency of all model components under high-dimensional scaling. Simulations demonstrate accurate recovery of latent classes, the shared block partition, and class-specific dependence graphs with scalable computation. Applications to American National Election Studies survey data and HapMap3 genotype data show that the method uncovers interpretable local dependence structures while accounting for latent heterogeneity.

</details>


### [222] [Multi-Source Transfer Learning of Sparse Single-Index Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29658)
*Ye Tian*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单指数模型（SIM）的无源数据迁移学习框架，仅通过一次性传输由广义Stein引理导出的汇总统计量来实现迁移，无需访问原始源数据，保护隐私的同时能灵活捕捉未知非线性。多层感知机利用预估计指数缓解过拟合，在合成和真实数据上均优于现有线性迁移方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移学习理论主要局限于（广义）线性模型，存在两个关键限制：(1) 假设已知的链接函数或线性结构，无法适应未知非线性；(2) 需要直接访问源域原始数据，带来隐私风险和通信负担。作者希望突破这两大约束，实现隐私保护且能适应非线性的迁移学习。

Method: 提出基于单指数模型（SIM）的无源数据迁移框架。核心思路：利用广义Stein引理从源域数据中提取汇总统计量，仅通过一次性通信传输这些统计量（而非原始数据）。目标域利用这些统计量预估计单指数方向，再以该指数引导多层感知机（MLP）学习非线性链接函数，从而在保护隐私的同时灵活捕捉未知非线性结构。

Result: 在合成数据和真实应用上的大量实验表明，所提框架相比于现有基于（广义）线性模型的迁移学习方法取得了持续一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该框架为迁移学习提供了一种实用、隐私保护且非线性自适应的解决方案，有效突破了传统线性模型在链接函数已知性和源数据可访问性上的双重限制。

Abstract: Transfer learning leverages knowledge from related source domains to improve learning in a target domain. Recent theoretical advances cover a broad range of regression settings within (generalized) linear models. Despite their diversity, these methods share two common constraints: they assume a known link function or linear structure and require direct access to raw source data. To move beyond these constraints, we propose a source-data-free transfer learning framework based on the single-index model (SIM). Instead of requiring raw source data, our method transfers only summary statistics derived from a generalized Stein's lemma in a one-time communication. This design preserves privacy and avoids side effects caused by dissimilarities of unknown nonlinear link functions across domains. To capture flexible, unknown nonlinearity, we employ a multilayer perceptron guided by the pre-estimated index from the transferred statistics, which significantly mitigates overfitting. Extensive experiments on synthetic data and a real-world application demonstrate consistent improvements over existing (generalized) linear model-based approaches. The proposed framework thus offers a practical, privacy-preserving, and nonlinear-adaptive solution for transfer learning.

</details>


### [223] [Testing hypotheses via orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29732)
*Ameer Dharamshi,Runjia Zou,Daniela Witten*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的假设检验框架，通过对原始数据添加和减去来自对称移位族的外部噪声，将数据分割为两部分。在零假设下对第二部分关于第一部分进行正交化，检验正交化是否成功即可作为有效的假设检验。该方法自然扩展到选择后推断，无需预先指定选择机制，极大扩展了有效选择后推断的适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典假设检验框架在当代场景中失效，原因包括：零假设日益抽象、同一数据被用于生成和检验假设、对数据生成分布只需极弱假设。现有方法难以在这些广泛背景下进行有效推断，尤其在后选择推断中受到严格限制。

Method: 提出通过加减来自对称移位族的外部噪声将数据X分割为X^(1)和X^(2)。核心策略是在H0下将X^(2)对X^(1)正交化，然后检验正交化是否成功——成功则支持H0。该方法自然扩展至后选择推断：在X^(1)上选择假设，然后在所选零假设下进行正交化检验。

Result: 在温和假设下，正交化成功与否的检验为H0提供了有效检验。该方法无需预指定选择机制，也不限于特定数据生成分布类别，大幅扩展了后选择推断的适用范围。通过多个案例研究展示了方法的灵活性，涵盖具有挑战性的预指定零假设和后选择推断场景。

Conclusion: 该框架为广泛的抽象零假设和后选择推断场景提供了一种统一、灵活且有效的假设检验方法，突破了经典框架的诸多限制。

Abstract: Classical hypothesis testing frameworks break down in contemporary settings in which null hypotheses are increasingly abstract, the same data are used to both generate and test hypotheses, and minimal assumptions about the underlying data are made. In this work, we propose a new framework for conducting valid hypothesis tests in broad contexts. We propose to add and subtract external noise generated from a symmetric shift-family to our data, $X$, to partition it into two pieces, $X^{(1)}$ and $X^{(2)}$. We provide a generic strategy for orthogonalizing $X^{(2)}$ against $X^{(1)}$ under the null hypothesis $H_0$, then show that testing whether the orthogonalization was successful provides a valid test of $H_0$ under mild assumptions. Remarkably, this framework extends naturally to the post-selection inference setting: we simply select a hypothesis on $X^{(1)}$, then perform orthogonalization under the selected null. As our approach neither requires pre-specification of the selection mechanism, nor is restricted to a small class of data-generating distributions, it dramatically expands the settings for which valid post-selection inference can be conducted. We showcase the flexibility of our proposal in several case studies involving challenging pre-specified null hypotheses and post-selection inference scenarios.

</details>


### [224] [HERO: Improving the Reliability and Sensitivity of Generative Model Evaluation Using Historical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29784)
*Xinrui Ruan,Zhenyu Zhao,Waverly Wei,Yueshan Zhang,Zeyu Zheng,Sui Huang,Jingshen Wang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: HERO 框架利用历史评估数据来抑制偏差并降低方差，通过校准银标标注者的历史表现并将其锚定到高精度协变量信息上，从而提升AI模型评估的可靠性和灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金标（专家标注）昂贵且稀缺，银标（众包/供应商标注）量大但噪声多。现有方法中，简单聚合银标会引入偏差，而仅基于稀疏金标构建的估计器方差过高。由于模型评估已成为跨版本、跨领域的持续性运营实践，如何利用历史评估数据改进每一轮新评估成为一个关键问题。

Method: 提出HERO框架：(1)利用历史金标数据校准各银标标注者的性能表现，抑制偏差；(2)将估计器锚定到历史数据中高精度测量的协变量信息上，降低方差。HERO适用于多种常见评估任务，且在当前轮次仅有部分历史标注者出现时依然有效。

Result: 在模拟研究中验证了HERO在偏差和方差缩减上的性能；在真实世界模型评估基准数据集上展示了其有效性。论文还建立了偏差和方差缩减成立的理论条件。

Conclusion: HERO是一种利用历史数据来提升模型评估质量的有效框架，能够同时提高评估的可靠性（降偏差）和灵敏度（降方差），具有广泛的适用性和理论基础。

Abstract: Reliable generative AI models critically rely on expert human annotations to evaluate output quality, yet these "gold" labels are expensive to collect and limited in quantity. Organizations thus often turn to collecting vast but noisy "silver" labels from crowdsourced workers or vendor annotators as proxies for gold labels. Because gold remains the evaluation target, naively aggregating noisy silver labels may introduce bias, and estimators built on sparsely observed gold labels may have high variance to resolve the model performance gaps that guide practical decisions. Model evaluation has become an ongoing operational practice rather than a one-time exercise, with evaluation rounds repeating across model versions, releases, and content domains. A natural question is whether the previous historical evaluation data can be used to improve each new round of evaluation. We introduce HERO (History Enhanced RObust model evaluation), a novel framework that uses historical data to suppress bias (improve reliability) and reduce variance (improve sensitivity) in model performance evaluation. HERO calibrates silver labelers' performance learned from historical gold annotations, and stabilizes the resulting estimator by anchoring it to covariate information measured with high precision in the historical data. HERO can be broadly applied across multiple common evaluation tasks, and remains valid when only a subset of historical labelers appears in the current round. We establish conditions under which the bias and variance reductions hold, showcase HERO's performance in simulation studies, and demonstrate its effectiveness on real-world model evaluation benchmarking datasets.

</details>


### [225] [Scalable coarse-to-fine spatial downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29798)
*Daisuke Murakami,Yongwan Chun,Takahiro Yoshida,Hajime Seya*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出了粗到精降尺度方法(CF-DS)，一种无需协方差矩阵求逆和似然评估的可扩展空间降尺度方法，通过多尺度局部模型合成实现近似聚合约束，在保持与面到点克里金相当精度的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统空间统计降尺度方法（如面到点克里金）需要协方差矩阵求逆和似然评估，在面对大规模数据集时计算成本过高，亟需一种计算高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 提出CF-DS方法，继承粗到精空间建模思想，通过合成多尺度局部模型来表示潜在空间过程，在近似满足聚合约束的同时避免协方差矩阵求逆和似然评估。

Result: 蒙特卡洛实验表明CF-DS预测精度与面到点克里金相当，但计算时间大幅缩短，尤其在大型数据集上优势显著；东京都市圈电力消费降尺度应用进一步验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: CF-DS为大规模空间降尺度问题提供了一种高效的替代方法，已实现为R包spCF并发布在CRAN上。

Abstract: This study proposes coarse-to-fine downscaling (CF-DS), a scalable spatial downscaling method extending coarse-to-fine spatial modeling. Unlike conventional spatial-statistical downscaling methods such as area-to-point kriging, CF-DS does not require covariance matrix inversion or likelihood evaluation. Instead, it represents latent spatial processes through the synthesis of multi-scale local models, substantially reducing computational cost while approximately satisfying the aggregation constraint. Monte Carlo experiments show that CF-DS achieves predictive accuracy comparable to area-to-point kriging with dramatically shorter computation times, particularly for large datasets. An application to downscaling electricity consumption in the Tokyo metropolitan area further demonstrates its practical usefulness. The results suggest that CF-DS provides an efficient alternative for large-scale spatial downscaling problems. CF-DS is implemented in an R package spCF (https://cran.r-project.org/web/packages/spCF/index.html).

</details>


### [226] [Beyond Equidistant Assumptions: An Autoregressive Ordered Stereotype Model for Ordinal Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29931)
*Anna Nalpantidi,Dimitris Karlis,Daniel Fernández*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出自回归有序刻板模型(AR-OSM)，通过将响应变量的滞后值作为协变量纳入系统成分来捕捉序数时间序列的序列依赖性。该模型不假设类别等距，而是让数据决定相对间距，适用于婴儿睡眠状态等非等距类别场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序数时间序列回归模型通常假设类别之间是等距的，而这一假设在许多实际应用中并不现实。需要一种更灵活的模型，让数据本身来决定类别之间的相对间距。

Method: 在有序刻板模型(OSM)的基础上引入自回归结构，将响应变量的滞后项作为协变量纳入系统成分，构建AR-OSM模型。通过模拟研究评估不同样本量下的模型表现，并考察参数值对序列依赖结构的影响。

Result: 通过婴儿睡眠状态数据的实证分析，展示了模型在非等距类别场景下的适用性。模拟研究系统评估了模型在不同样本量下的表现，并揭示了参数值如何影响诱导的序列依赖结构。

Conclusion: AR-OSM模型有效克服了传统序数时间序列模型对等距假设的依赖，在类别间距不均匀的实际场景中具有显著优势，为序数时间序列分析提供了更灵活的建模工具。

Abstract: We propose an extension of the ordered stereotype model (OSM) for ordinal time series data, referred to as the Autoregressive OSM (AR-OSM). The model captures serial dependence by incorporating lagged values of the response as covariates in the systematic component. In contrast to existing regression models for ordinal time series, the AR-OSM does not assume equidistant categories, but instead allows the data to determine their relative spacing. This property makes the model particularly suitable for applications where the equidistance assumption is unrealistic. Such a case is illustrated through the analysis of infant sleep state data. Additionally, a comprehensive simulation study is conducted to assess the performance of the model under varying sample sizes and to investigate how parameter values influence the induced serial dependence structure.

</details>


### [227] [Evaluating HWE and Association in Genome Wide Association Studies: A Unified Procedure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30311)
*Stefan Böhringer,Hajo Holzmann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件基因型检验方法，在病例-对照GWAS中，将3×2列联表的Pearson χ²统计量以对照组HWE的χ²统计量为条件进行推断，避免了传统基于任意HWE阈值剔除SNP的做法。该方法在大多数场景下统计功效更高，且p值天然包含HWE信息，使SNP排序更优，可替代单独的HWE检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GWAS病例-对照设计中，SNP需同时通过关联检验和HWE拟合优度检验，不符合HWE的SNP常被基于任意阈值剔除，以避免假阳性。但这种两阶段做法存在阈值选择主观、信息利用不充分的问题，需要一个统一框架来避免任意阈值并提升检验效能。

Method: 提出条件基因型检验（conditional genotype-based test）：将3×2列联表（基因型×病例/对照状态）的Pearson χ²关联统计量，以对照组HWE的χ²统计量为条件，推导其渐近分布理论，从而在单一框架下同时考虑关联和HWE信息。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该检验在多数场景下比两种竞争性回顾性方法功效更高；在脱发症真实数据上展示了该方法因p值包含HWE信息而能更好排序SNP的优势。

Conclusion: 该检验使单独的HWE检验成为多余，提供统一框架，在功效和可解释性上严格优于标准流程，使重复验证更具成本效益，并改进了后续精细定位。

Abstract: In genome wide association studies (GWASs) based on a case-control design, single nucleotide polymorphisms (SNPs) are typically evaluated for an association test and a Hardy-Weinberg equilibrium (HWE) goodness-of-fit test. SNPs are then excluded from analysis based on a HWE cutoff to avoid false positives. In order to avoid cutoffs based on arbitrary threshold values, we propose a conditional genotype--based test that conditions the Pearson $χ^2$-statistic in the 3x2 contingency table on the $χ^2$-statistic for HWE in the control group, and develop the relevant asymptotic distribution theory. We show by simulations that our test in most scenarios is more powerful than two competing retrospective procedures. Another important advantage of the proposed method is a better ranking of SNPs in GWASs as HWE is accounted for in computing p-values of SNP association. We demonstrate this effect on a data set in an alopecia study. In conclusion, our test makes separate HWE testing superfluous by providing a unified framework and strictly improves on the standard procedure in terms of power and interpretability, thereby making replication more cost effective and improving subsequent fine mapping.\par

</details>


### [228] [Multiscale Dynamic Dependence Estimation over Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30399)
*Cristian F. Jiménez-Varón,Marina I. Knight,Matthew A. Nunes*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出Net-LSW框架，将网络结构显式嵌入非平稳多元时间序列的多尺度时变协方差建模中，并开发了基于子过程的推断方法，成功应用于全球银行网络股价波动率分析，捕捉到脱欧与新冠疫情期间的系统性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多元时间序列常同时具备两个特征：二阶统计特性随时间变化的非平稳性，以及序列间交叉依赖受底层网络拓扑结构约束。传统方法未能将网络结构显式融入非平稳过程的协方差建模中，亟需一个统一框架来同时处理时变依赖与网络拓扑的复杂交互。

Method: 提出网络局部平稳小波过程（Net-LSW），将图结构直接编码进过程随机增量的协方差结构中；引入局部偏相关图概念，将图中边与时-频依赖结构的非零项关联；开发基于子过程的局部跨节点（偏）依赖推断方法，并证明其一致性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，该框架能准确恢复演化中的依赖结构，同时尊重底层图拓扑。在全球银行网络日度股价波动率的应用中，成功捕捉到脱欧和COVID-19疫情等重大金融冲击期间的多尺度、高度非平稳依赖及系统性转变。

Conclusion: Net-LSW框架为网络化非平稳时间序列的多尺度时变依赖建模提供了统一解决方案，在金融系统性风险监测等领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In numerous scientific and industrial settings, observed multivariate time series are often nonstationary in nature, i.e., comprise data whose second order properties vary over time. An additional feature of many modern datasets is that the cross-dependencies of such series are structured by an underlying network, giving rise to complex interactions between temporal dynamics and network topology. In this article we propose Locally Stationary Wavelet processes on Networks (Net-LSW), a new framework for modelling multiscale, time-varying dependencies that explicitly incorporates the network structure. Unlike traditional multivariate approaches, the Net-LSW process encodes the graph directly in the covariance structure of the process's random increments. We also introduce the concept of the local partial correlation graph, which connects edges in the graph to non-zero entries in the time- and scale-dependent dependence structure of a nonstationary process. For inference on the local cross-nodal (partial) dependence, we develop a novel subprocess-based estimation scheme and establish its desirable consistency properties. Simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately recovers evolving dependence structures whilst respecting the underlying graph topology. Finally, we apply our framework to daily stock price volatilities across a global bank network, demonstrating its ability to capture multiscale, highly nonstationary dependencies and identify time-varying systemic shifts during major financial shocks, including Brexit and the COVID-19 pandemic.

</details>


### [229] [Tuning-Free Efficient Estimation for Multi-Source Data via Covariance-Aware Shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30615)
*Wenbo Jing,Xi Chen,Yaqi Duan,Kaizheng Wang,Yichen Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需调参的协方差感知收缩框架，利用协方差信息构造收缩方向以提升多源数据下的学习效率，并设计了序列化算法逐次引入源数据，在异质性较高的场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代统计学习中常面临多个相关数据集，利用源数据可提升目标集的学习效率，但源数据间的异质性会引入偏差。现有方法存在多源场景下性能次优、协方差信息利用不足以及调参计算负担大等局限。

Method: 提出协方差感知的收缩框架，利用协方差信息构造收缩方向；建立有限样本风险界，导出显式的风险改善区间，使收缩大小完全由数据驱动。针对多源场景，提出序列化算法，按估计的风险降低程度逐次向各源收缩；进一步通过局部二次近似扩展至一般光滑M-估计问题。

Result: 在温和条件下，序列算法渐近达到Oracle风险，且能保证优于文献中的单步收缩方法。数值研究表明，尤其在源数据集高度异质时，相比竞争方法有显著增益。

Conclusion: 该框架在多源异质场景下实现了免调参、协方差感知的高效估计，序列化收缩策略在理论和实践上均表现出优越性，并可推广至一般光滑M-估计。

Abstract: Modern statistical learning problems often involve multiple related data sets, where learning efficiency on a target set can be improved by utilizing related source sets, while heterogeneity among the source sets may introduce bias. Existing approaches are limited by suboptimal performance in multi-source settings, insufficient use of covariance information, or the computational burden of tuning procedures. We propose a tuning-free and covariance-aware shrinkage framework that constructs shrinkage directions using covariance information to improve efficiency. We establish finite-sample risk bounds that yield an explicit risk-improving interval for the shrinkage size, making the procedure fully data-driven and tuning-free. When multiple source sets are available, we further propose a novel sequential algorithm that shrinks the estimator toward the sources one at a time according to their estimated risk reduction. The proposed algorithm asymptotically attains the oracle risk under mild conditions and is guaranteed to improve over the single-step shrinkage method in the literature. The framework is further extended to general smooth \(M\)-estimation problems via a local quadratic approximation. Numerical studies show substantial gains over competing methods, especially when the source data sets are highly heterogeneous.

</details>
